### [1] ["I'm Very Happy for It to Start Hallucinating a Little Bit": Using ClayFlect to Negotiate Multimodal AI Representations in Material Meaning-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08943)
*Kellie Yu Hui Sim,Quoc-Nam Nguyen,Shuenn Yuen Han,Kenny Tsu Wei Choo*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 论文提出ClayFlect系统，将触觉黏土创作与对话式、视觉生成式AI结合，通过50人混合方法研究，揭示AI生成表征在个人意义建构中应保持"可协商"性质，用户始终保有解释权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI进入反思与情感支持领域，如何在支持个人意义建构的同时保留用户对解释的权威，成为日益重要的议题。

Method: 设计并实现ClayFlect——一个由多模态大模型（MLLM）驱动的系统，融合触觉黏土制作、对话式AI与视觉生成式AI；开展50名参与者的混合方法研究。

Result: 反思在材料、对话与生成三种形态间流动发展，而非仅来自AI交互。参与者将AI表征视为临时性的：随着作品与意义演变，他们进行比较、引导、重新诠释、选择性吸纳或舍弃。黏土提供了可直接操控的空间，使用户能独立于AI继续发展意义；生成表征则外化了他们难以亲手制作或想象的可能性。

Conclusion: 生成式AI可通过保持"可协商"的表征参与意义建构；据此提出设计启示：支持跨表征形态的流动、保留并行的控制场所、允许AI支持随反思进程而退隐或加深。

Abstract: As AI enters reflection and emotional support, understanding how it can participate in personal meaning-making while preserving users' authority over interpretation is increasingly important. We present ClayFlect, a novel MLLM-powered system integrating tactile clay-making with conversational and visual generative AI, and report a mixed-methods study with 50 participants. Reflection developed across material, conversational, and generated forms rather than through AI interaction alone. Participants treated AI representations as provisional: they compared, redirected, reinterpreted, selectively incorporated, or left them aside as their artefacts and meanings evolved. Clay provided a directly manipulable space in which participants could continue developing meaning independently of the AI, while generated representations externalised possibilities beyond what they could readily make or visualise. We show how generative AI can participate through representations that remain negotiable, and derive implications for supporting movement across representations, preserving parallel sites of control, and allowing AI support to recede or deepen as reflection unfolds.

</details>


### [2] [Supporting Allyship in Virtual Collaboration with Artificial Intelligence: A Scenario-Based Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09061)
*Crescentia Jung,Ricardo E. Gonzalez Penuela,Prashita Biswas,Seungyoon Kwon,Shiri Azenkot*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文探讨AI如何支持虚拟协作中的残障可及性盟友行为，通过18名参与者（10名残障、8名非残障）对四种盟友情景的反思，提出"盟友支持工具"作为一类新的可及性技术，并给出设计指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚拟协作中的可及性不仅是技术问题，还依赖"盟友行为"——即人们支持他人可及性需求的非正式人际实践。然而，目前鲜有研究探讨AI如何在这一协作可及性情境中支持盟友行为，存在研究空白。

Method: 开展一项研究，邀请18名参与者（10名残障人士、8名非残障人士）对四种结合新型AI工具的盟友情景进行反思与评价。

Result: 参与者认可AI设计能帮助队友表达、解读和协调可及性需求，通过减少重复披露使盟友行为更具可操作性；但当AI未经同意行事、曲解用户意图或取代人际盟友工作时，参与者表达了担忧。

Conclusion: 研究提出"盟友支持工具"作为一类可及性技术，提供设计指南，并讨论AI作为支持盟友行为手段的潜力与边界。

Abstract: Recent research has revealed that accessibility in virtual collaboration is not only a technical problem but also depends on allyship: the informal, interpersonal practices through which people support others' accessibility needs. Yet little research has examined how artificial intelligence (AI) might support allyship in collaborative accessibility contexts. To address this gap, we conducted a study where 18 participants (10 disabled, 8 non-disabled) reflected on four allyship scenarios with novel AI tools. Participants valued AI designs that they anticipated could help teammates express, interpret, and coordinate around access needs by reducing repeated disclosure and making allyship more actionable. At the same time, participants raised concerns when AI acted without consent, misrepresented users' intentions, or displaced the interpersonal work of allyship. Based on our findings, we introduce allyship-support tools as a class of accessibility technologies, offer design guidelines, and discuss AI in particular as a means of supporting allyship.

</details>


### [3] [Move Fast and Mend Things: Keeping Up with Evolving AI Harms Using Social Media Commentary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09082)
*Jacqueline Rowe,Animesh Srivastava,Sai Teja Peddinti,Seliem El-Sayed,Nina Taft*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文提出LLM辅助的主题分析流水线,从大规模社交媒体数据中动态检测、分类并追踪新兴AI危害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统的快速部署带来了社会技术、心理和操作层面的危害,这些危害往往难以通过事前威胁建模和事后事件追踪发现,因此需要更及时、自下而上的危害识别手段。

Method: 构建LLM辅助的主题分析流水线,应用于18个月(2025年1月至2026年6月)共570万条Reddit帖子摘要,筛选出57.5万条AI危害相关帖子,并归纳出包含12个类别、47个子节点的自下而上AI危害分类体系。

Result: 该分类体系能够可靠覆盖已有专家定义的风险,同时揭示自上而下框架忽略的细粒度危害(如不同形式的AI隐私侵犯);时间分析还发现新兴的用户中心危害,如智能体隐私与安全漏洞、职场中AI的过早采用以及AI伴侣停用带来的悲伤情绪。

Conclusion: 该流水线缩短了危害检测周期,有助于推动更具参与性和响应性的AI治理。

Abstract: The rapid deployment of AI systems has created socio-technical, psychological, and operational harms that can elude ex-ante threat modelling and ex-post incident tracking. We introduce an LLM-assisted thematic analysis pipeline to dynamically detect, categorise, and track emerging AI harms from large-scale social media data. Applying it to 5.7 million Reddit post summaries over 18 months (01/2025 to 06/2026), we curate and release a dataset of 575,000 AI harm-related posts and a bottom-up AI harm taxonomy of 12 categories and 47 subnodes. The taxonomy reliably covers established expert-defined risks while surfacing granular harms that top-down frameworks overlook, such as distinct forms of AI privacy violations. Temporal analysis surfaces evolving user-centric harms, such as agentic privacy and security breaches, premature AI adoption in the workplace, and grief from AI companion discontinuation. Our pipeline shortens harm-detection timelines and hereby complements efforts towards more participatory and responsive AI governance.

</details>


### [4] ["Just Like This": Manner Deixis at the Graphics Interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09099)
*Hamza El Alaoui,Jeffrey P. Bigham,Jun Rekimoto*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种对话式3D创作交互技术，将语音、指向与时空演示相结合来指定可编辑的行为机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人们常通过"词语+演示"的方式沟通事物应如何运动，作者希望把这种表达资源引入对话式3D创作中。

Method: 在"Put-That-There"基础上，系统结合语音、指向和时空演示来指定可编辑行为；手部运动提供机制轴、支点、范围和节奏的证据，动画预览使解释可被检视；用户可通过更多词语或演示来细化行为，系统也可请求演示以澄清意图。

Result: 十二名参与者的研究比较三种输入配置：本系统达到89%的参与者自报完成率，而仅用语音为47%；在六项分类准确性指标上观察到最高匹配率，两项持平；九名参与者更偏好本系统。

Conclusion: 该工作将演示纳入持续的创作对话：行为可以被展示、检视和修改。

Abstract: People communicate how things should move by combining words with demonstrations: "open it like this." We present an interaction technique that brings this expressive resource to conversational 3D authoring. Building on "Put-That-There," our system combines speech, pointing, and spatiotemporal demonstrations to specify editable behavior. A hand movement supplies evidence for a mechanism's axis, pivot, range, and pace; an animated preview makes the interpretation inspectable. Users refine behavior through further words or demonstrations, and the system can request a demonstration to clarify intent. In a twelve-participant study comparing three input configurations, our system achieved 89% participant-declared completion versus 47% with speech alone, had the highest observed match rates on six categorical accuracy measures and tied on two, and was preferred by nine participants. This work makes demonstration part of an ongoing authoring conversation: behavior can be shown, inspected, and revised.

</details>


### [5] [Drawing the Line: Where AI Guidance and Human Creativity Meet in Emotion-Driven Comic Storyboarding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09155)
*Jocelyn Shen,Isabella Pu,Alessandro Briseño,Hyun Kim,Fiona Lu,Sharifa Alghowinem,Cynthia Breazeal,Hae Won Park*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: EmoToon是一个技术探针，帮助非专业艺术家生成素描风格故事板并迭代视觉创意，通过人机协作支持情感驱动的创作。N=25的被控实验表明，AI辅助显著提升了情感表达、美学质量和探索性，但降低了用户的创作所有权感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI模型虽能产出视觉逼真的艺术作品，却往往难以传达情感真实性这一人类表达的关键驱动力。在视觉叙事尤其是漫画故事板创作中，这一差距更为突出：有效的故事板既需要技术知识（如解剖准确性、场景构图），也需要来自生活经验的情感洞察。作者由此探索人机协作如何支持情感驱动的创意，即由用户的情感引导生成、以情感共鸣为目标。

Method: 作者提出EmoToon——一个技术探针（technology probe），旨在帮助非专业艺术家生成素描风格的故事板并对视觉想法进行迭代。随后开展了一项N=25的被控用户研究，评估AI辅助对创作过程的影响。

Result: 研究发现AI辅助显著提升了情感表达、美学质量和探索性，但降低了用户的创作所有权感（creative ownership）。

Conclusion: 研究为漫画叙事领域的人机共创提供了更广泛的启示，强调需要在输出质量与用户自由度之间取得平衡，并为该领域的图像生成模型提出了新的挑战。

Abstract: While generative AI models can produce visually faithful artwork, they often fall short in conveying emotional authenticity--a key driver of human expression. In visual storytelling, particularly comic storyboarding, this gap becomes pronounced: effective storyboards require both technical knowledge (e.g., anatomical accuracy or scenic composition) and emotional insight (from lived experience). We explore how human-AI collaboration can support emotion-driven creativity, where the user's feelings guide generation and emotional resonance is the goal. We present EmoToon, a technology probe that helps non-professional artists generate sketch-like storyboards and iterate on visual ideas. In a controlled study with N=25 participants, we find that AI assistance significantly improves emotional expression, aesthetic quality, and exploration, but reduces users' creative ownership. Our findings offer broader insights for human-AI co-creation in the domain of comic storytelling, emphasizing balance between output quality and user freedom, and raising new challenges for image generation models in this domain.

</details>


### [6] [AI-Assisted Submissions in Online Research Are Rare and Highly Concentrated but Routinely Approved](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09279)
*Neil K. R. Sehgal,Manuel Tonneau,Dunigan Folk,Lyle Ungar,Sharath Chandra Guntuku*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过将ChatGPT历史与在线研究平台提交记录直接关联，研究首次直接观测AI辅助发生率：仅1%提交存在辅助，且3年多无增长，但惩罚与监管存在缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线研究平台假设每条回复均由人类完成，生成式AI使参与者可将作答委托给聊天机器人，威胁该假设；然而此前估计依赖自我报告或自动检测，无法获知真实发生率。

Method: 调查2,500名高质量在线研究平台工作者；通过将捐赠的ChatGPT历史与每周ChatGPT用户的平台提交记录链接，直接观测AI辅助，覆盖127,000多项研究的712,930条提交。

Result: 1/8受访者报告曾在研究中使用AI，68%被观测工作者曾使用，但仅1%提交出现辅助，3年多无可检测增长；辅助在任务内时间集中、高度集中于少数工作者（5%占64%辅助提交）；辅助时几乎总能获得报酬，即使研究禁止AI，总体批准率与无辅助相似；半数辅助提交为封闭式回答，超出平台LLM检测器范围。

Conclusion: 结果不支持AI辅助当前对在线研究构成生存性威胁，但揭示禁止使用缺乏支付后果、平台保障存在缺口，若威胁显现则平台准备不足。

Abstract: Online research platforms underpin much of what science claims about people, on the assumption that a human produced each response. Generative AI threatens that assumption by letting participants delegate responses to a chatbot, yet how often they do so remains unclear because prior estimates rely on self-report or automated detection rather than direct observation. We surveyed 2,500 workers on a high quality online research platform and directly observed AI assistance by linking donated ChatGPT histories to platform submission records of weekly ChatGPT users, covering 712,930 submissions across more than 127,000 studies. Although one in eight surveyed workers reported ever using AI on a study and 68% of observed workers had done so, assistance appeared in only 1% of submissions, with no detectable increase over more than 3 years. Assistance was often temporally localized within tasks and highly concentrated among workers, with 5% accounting for 64% of assisted submissions. Where assistance occurred, workers were virtually always paid, even when study instructions prohibited AI use, with overall approval rates similar to those for unassisted submissions. Half of assisted submissions involved bounded responses, outside the scope of the platform's LLM detector for open-ended responses. Taken together, our results do not support the view that AI assistance currently poses an existential threat to online research, but reveal limited payment consequences for prohibited use and gaps in platform safeguards, leaving platforms poorly prepared should that threat materialize.

</details>


### [7] [Beyond Activation: Gaze Invocation with Visible Status for an Embodied AR Assistant in Co-Located Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09284)
*Chenrui Ma,Yoshio Ishiguro,Qing Zhang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文提出结合注视唤醒、具身助手与状态可视化的交互设计，用25人实验验证其在多人共处AR场景中区分"对助手说话/对同伴说话"的有效性，并揭示释放阶段的状态可见性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 唤醒词存在灵敏度与误激活的固有权衡；在共处式AR中还需判断用户是在对助手还是对身边人说话，现有方案难以清晰区分交互对象与边界。

Method: 提出注视定位（gaze-based address）与具身助手结合的设计：注视锚定在场景中的助手即开启本地交互通道，助手身体显示激活进度与聆听状态；持续注视保持通道开启，语音与播放期间注意力回归任务时通道仍开放，不活跃则关闭。25名参与者在选定助手位置与停留时长后，与同伴共同规划旅行并使用助手，共记录500次交互结果。

Result: 496次面向助手的发声尝试中，490次（98.8%）在说话前已完成注视获取：467次在通道开启时开始，23次在通道已释放后开始，另有6次在注视获取完成前即开始。在102次"注视获取后、说话前视线离开"的尝试中，保留策略使79次通道保持开启，23次在说话前释放。多数用户未察觉助手已停止聆听。

Conclusion: 具身目标加可见状态可支持清晰的助手交互入口，但释放阶段存在新问题：用户视线回到工作后，看不到助手已停止聆听。论文贡献了已实现的注视唤醒设计及关于视线转移后如何传达助手状态的设计启示。

Abstract: Wake words face an inherent trade-off: higher sensitivity reduces missed commands but increases accidental activations. In co-located augmented reality (AR), the system must also determine whether the user is speaking to the assistant or to a nearby person. We introduce a new perspective on this problem: combining gaze-based address with an embodied assistant, visible listening status, and turn management across activation, continued interaction, and release. In our implementation, users look at an assistant anchored in the scene and see activation progress and listening status on its body. Sustained gaze opens a local interaction channel; speech and playback keep it open when attention returns to the task; and inactivity closes it. We evaluated this design with 25 participants. After selecting the assistant's placement and dwell duration, each participant worked with a partner to plan a trip while using the assistant. The study recorded 500 interaction outcomes, including 496 assistant-directed utterance attempts. Gaze acquisition completed before speech for 490 of these attempts (98.8\%): 467 began while the channel remained open, whereas 23 began after it had been released. The other 6 attempts began before acquisition completed. Among 102 reviewed attempts in which gaze left after acquisition but before speech, the configured retention policy kept the channel open for 79 and released it before 23. The results show that an embodied target with visible status can support clear entry into an assistant interaction while revealing a different problem at release: after users look back to their work, they may not see that the assistant has stopped listening. We contribute the implemented gaze-invocation design and design implications for communicating assistant state after visual attention moves elsewhere.

</details>


### [8] [Many Brains, One Geometry: A Shared Visual-Semantic Space for Cross-Dataset fMRI Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09352)
*Moein Khajehnejad,Michelangelo Tronti,Forough Habibollahi,Tommaso Boccato,Matteo Ferrante,Nicola Toschi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出BRAID-fMRI，一个共享CLIP监督的解码框架，用单个ROI级Transformer在8个视觉fMRI数据集上实现跨个体、跨数据集的对齐解码，性能超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉fMRI解码通常受限于单个被试和单个实验，难以判断异质神经测量能否被组织在统一的计算几何空间中。作者希望实现跨个体、跨数据集的规模化解码并对齐到共同的CLIP表征空间。

Method: BRAID-fMRI使用单个ROI级Transformer并可选被试条件，覆盖8个视觉fMRI数据集（93个数据集特异被试条目、430,007个单试次反应、162,839个独特刺激）。将区域脑活动与512维CLIP ViT-B/32表征对齐，采用多正样本对比目标，把跨被试与跨数据集的重复刺激视为正样本。

Result: 在8个匹配被试条目上，Top-10准确率为35.0±11.1%，高于MindEye式池化CLIP解码器（27.1±5.1%）和岭回归（21.1±9.1%），并在8个条目中的7个取得最高准确率。在被试无关模型上，扩大来源池使目标数据集留出准确率最高相对提升92.1%。

Conclusion: 结果支持可扩展的跨数据集解码到统一CLIP对齐空间，模型敏感性集中于腹侧和早期视觉输入；学习到的空间保留了分级语义结构，消融与显著性分析突出腹侧/早期视觉皮层及类别特异的运动与注意系统。

Abstract: Visual decoding from fMRI is typically siloed by participant and experiment, obscuring whether heterogeneous neural measurements can be organized within a common computational geometry. Here we introduce BRAID-fMRI (Brain Representation Alignment across Individuals and Datasets), a shared CLIP-supervised decoding framework. BRAID-fMRI uses a single ROI-wise Transformer with optional participant conditioning across eight visual-fMRI datasets comprising 93 dataset-specific participant entries, 430,007 single-trial responses and 162,839 unique stimuli. Regional brain activity is aligned with 512-dimensional CLIP ViT-B/32 representations using a multi-positive contrastive objective that treats repeated stimuli across participants and datasets as positives. BRAID-fMRI supports retrieval across seven evaluation datasets. On eight matched participant entries, it achieves 35.0 +/- 11.1% Top-10 accuracy, exceeding the observed mean accuracy of the two evaluated baselines - the MindEye-style pooled-CLIP decoder (27.1 +/- 5.1%) and ridge regression (21.1 +/- 9.1%) - and attaining the highest observed accuracy for seven of eight entries. In separately trained participant-agnostic models, expanding the source pool increased target-dataset holdout accuracy by up to 92.1% relative to the initial source-training condition. The learned space preserves graded semantic structure, while ablations and saliency highlight ventral and early visual cortex and category-specific motion and attentional systems. These results support scalable cross-dataset decoding into a common CLIP-aligned space, with model sensitivity concentrated in ventral and early visual inputs.

</details>


### [9] [TutorLoop: Regulating Student Learning Behaviors via Sensor-in-the-Loop Generative Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09400)
*Songlin Xu,Xinyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: TutorLoop 是一个传感器在环系统，通过基于实时认知状态的自适应反馈来调节学生学习行为，在 N=187 用户研究中验证其能减少干预频率却提升效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 导师依赖场景特定内容，且直接将认知状态映射为反馈较为短视，缺乏对学习全过程的优化。作者希望通过传感器驱动的闭环反馈，实现可扩展的人机融合学习支持系统。

Method: TutorLoop 利用网络摄像头采集的传感器信号（而非场景内容），通过深度强化学习（DRL）智能体在整个学习过程中优化反馈类型，再由另一个 LLM 导师将反馈精炼为拟人化、情境感知的消息。

Result: 在 N=187 的大规模用户研究中，离线训练的模型直接应用于新学习任务且无需重新训练。结果显示 TutorLoop 干预频率更低但更有效，提升了注意力、降低了工作负荷、增强了参与度，并最终改善了学习成果。

Conclusion: 研究表明闭环、传感器驱动的反馈在可扩展的人机融合学习支持系统中具有潜力。

Abstract: We present TutorLoop, a sensor-in-the-loop system that regulates student learning behaviors by delivering adaptive feedback based on real-time cognitive states. Unlike prior large language model (LLM) tutors that directly depend on scenario-specific content, TutorLoop operates on sensor-derived signals captured via webcams. Moreover, unlike direct cognitive-to-feedback mappings that are short-sighted, the system employs a deep reinforcement learning (DRL) agent to optimize the feedback type across the entire learning process. Finally, another LLM tutor refines feedback into human-like, context-aware messages. We evaluate TutorLoop in a large-scale user study (N=187), where a model trained offline is directly applied to a new learning task without retraining. Results show that TutorLoop provides less frequent yet more effective interventions, improving attention, reducing workload, increasing engagement, and ultimately enhancing learning outcomes. These findings highlight the potential of closed-loop, sensor-driven feedback for scalable human-AI integrated systems to support learning.

</details>


### [10] [Before Bringing It Up: When and How AI Companions Should Use Memor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09470)
*Zihan Guo,Roxy He,Junwei Quan*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 论文提出 Reconsider：在 AI 伙伴使用记忆前先做"是否该用"的判断，经两轮共 14 人访谈提炼 8 个主题，形成含 5 项检查与 4 种处理模式的单次调用流程，并在 80 个场景、5 个模型、400 组盲测配对中评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 陪伴依赖记忆维持连续性，但即便回忆准确，使用该记忆也可能不合时宜。现有研究多关注记忆的准确存储与检索，缺少对"是否应当使用过去信息"的前置判断，因此需要探讨陪伴型 AI 在动用记忆前应考虑什么。

Method: 采用两轮形成性访谈（共 14 名用户，6 人探索性、8 人聚焦记忆）提炼 8 个主题，据此设计 Reconsider：单次调用程序，包含 5 项检查与 4 种处理模式。评估覆盖 5 个模型、80 个场景，构成 400 组同模型内盲测配对；使用两个 LLM 评审，并加入评审者分析及一项匹配指导的初步对照，以隔离记忆特定内容。

Result: 两个 LLM 评审对 Reconsider 的净偏好边际为 +15 与 +23 个百分点；在 5 个模型中的 3 个上，bootstrap 区间不包含零，但 GPT 与 Claude 上不显著。评审者分析显示评分差异与模型家族相关；匹配指导对照取得正向边际。

Conclusion: 论文贡献了两点：一是基于访谈、面向记忆使用的设计框架；二是对自身评审者进行审视的评估方法，提示记忆使用需在准确性之外考量得体性。

Abstract: Memory can sustain AI companionship, yet even accurate recollection can be inappropriate to use. Two rounds of formative interviews with 14 users (n = 6 exploratory, n = 8 memory-focused) motivate asking what a companion should consider before using past information. Eight themes inform Reconsider, a single-call procedure with five checks and four handling modes, evaluated on 80 scenarios across five models over 400 blinded within-model pairs. Two LLM judges favored Reconsider by net margins of +15 and +23 percentage points, with bootstrap intervals excluding zero for three of five models but not for GPT or Claude. Evaluator analysis linked judge scoring differences to model family, and a preliminary matched-guidance control isolating memory-specific content gave positive margins. We contribute an interview-grounded design framework for memory use and an evaluation that scrutinizes its own evaluators.

</details>


### [11] [Human-AI Conversational Behaviors Predict Unassisted Task Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09547)
*Li Siyan,Federico Bianchi,James Zou,Kaitlyn Zhou*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究通过两个游戏化问题解决用户研究（n=139、n=111），采用对话行为框架分析人机交互行为与即时及后续无辅助任务表现的关系，发现用户表达思考过程、助手解释问题状态与更好的无辅助表现相关，而直接索要或给出答案则相关较差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI工具虽能提升人类当前表现，但研究提示这种即时收益可能带来未来代价，例如在失去AI辅助时表现下降。因此需要理解哪些人机交互行为与即时及未来无辅助表现相关。

Method: 开展两个游戏化问题解决的用户研究（n=139和n=111），采用源自导师-学生对话分类法的"对话行为"框架，对参与者与AI的交互进行分类；结合定量分析与定性、子类型分析。

Result: 参与者口头表达思考过程与更高的无辅助表现相关，而直接索要解决方案则负相关；助手解释当前问题状态与后续无辅助表现更好相关，而直接给出下一步操作与更差表现相关。定性与子类型分析显示，表面相似的推理类轮次可能引发不同帮助。

Conclusion: 保留用户在问题解决中的认知参与，可能有助于维持AI辅助交互之外的表现。

Abstract: Assistance from AI tools has supported and improved human performance across domains. However, recent research suggests that these immediate benefits may entail future costs, including diminished performance when AI assistance is no longer available. We study how human-AI interaction behaviors correlate with immediate and future unassisted task performance across two game-based problem-solving user studies ($n=139$ and $n=111$), using a \textit{dialogue act} framework adopted from a tutor-student dialogue taxonomy. In our studies, verbalizing thought processes correlates with higher unassisted outcomes, whereas directly requesting solutions correlates negatively. Similar to these participant-side patterns, assistant explanations of the current problem state are associated with better subsequent unassisted performance, whereas directly providing the next action is associated with worse performance. Qualitative and subtype analyses further show that ostensibly similar reasoning turns can elicit different assistance. Our findings suggest that preserving users' cognitive participation in problem-solving may support performance beyond AI-assisted interaction.

</details>


### [12] [Structured pre-generation elicitation versus single-shot prompting in AI-assisted enterprise decision-making: a randomised online experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09593)
*William Scott-Jackson*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究表明，在AI生成内容前加入交互式元认知脚手架（Cognistance），可显著提升AI辅助战略文档的评分质量与任务相关性，且时间成本较小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI虽能提升专业工作效率，但用户若同时委托答案的生成与评估，可能接受低质输出并减少对推理过程的参与（认知让渡）。现有干预多在工作任务之外，因此需要一种嵌入工作流程的干预方式。

Method: 测试交互式元认知脚手架层（Cognistance原型）：在AI生成交付物之前，要求用户澄清背景、选择战略方向并解释自身推理。通过实验比较有无脚手架条件下交付物的质量与相关指标。

Result: 有脚手架时平均综合质量高32%（每位评分者方向一致）；在权衡阐述与战略一致性上收益最大，技术具体性上无收益。离题交付物比例从34%降至5%；对于提示中已含数据本地化问题的参与者，优势为21%。处理组参与感更强，平均多花约2.4分钟（共10.46分钟）。即时回忆得分更高，但在敏感性分析中不稳健；自评质量在两种条件下均未反映实际评分质量。

Conclusion: 生成前的结构化引导以适度时间成本提升了AI辅助战略文档的评分质量与任务相关性。延迟保留、错误检测及真实组织中的效果是下一阶段研究重点。

Abstract: Generative AI speeds, and mostly improves, professional work, but there is concern that users who delegate both the production and the evaluation of an answer may accept weak output and engage less with the underlying reasoning (cognitive surrender). Interventions proposed so far, such as unassisted practice or slowing adoption, sit outside the working task. We tested a different approach: an interactive metacognitive scaffolding layer (Cognistance, a prototype developed at the Oxford Centre for Impact Research (OCIR) that asks users to clarify context, choose a strategic direction and explain their reasoning before the AI generates a deliverable). Mean composite quality was 32% higher with the scaffold, with the same direction for every rater. Gains were largest for trade-off articulation and strategic coherence and absent for technical specificity. A large part of the aggregate effect reflected rescue of weak prompts: floor-scored (off-task) deliverables fell from 34% to 5%. Among participants whose own prompt already stated the data-localisation problem, the advantage was 21%. Treatment participants reported greater involvement and took about 2.4 minutes longer on average (10.46 minutes). Immediate recall scores were higher, which tentatively suggests better retention, but in this limited experiment, was not robust to sensitivity analyses. Self-ratings of quality did not track rated quality in either condition. Structured elicitation before generation improved the rated quality and task relevance of AI-assisted strategy documents at modest cost in time. Delayed retention, error detection and effects in live organisations are the priorities for the next stage of research.

</details>


### [13] [When My Skill Becomes Agent Skill: How Knowledge Workers Share Their Expertise with AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09608)
*Tianqi Song,Zicheng Zhu,Hancheng Cao,Yi-Chieh Lee*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过实验发现，智能体AI对人类知识复用的影响主要体现在工作者是否愿意分享，而非分享内容本身；与AI分享时，工作者的顾虑从亲社会考量转向控制权丧失、被替代及下游治理问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织长期追求让工作者的专业知识可被他人复用。智能体AI改变了这种复用的性质，使AI系统能在有限人类参与下依据工作者的知识采取行动。这一转变引发疑问：它如何影响工作者分享专业知识的意愿？

Method: 开展了一项实验，参与者为知识工作者，他们创建了包含领域知识的材料，并决定是否授权人类或AI进行复用。

Result: AI复用与人类复用的差异主要体现在工作者是否选择分享知识，而非选择分享什么内容。参与者的考量从对人类分享时的亲社会因素，转向对AI分享时对控制权丧失、被替代和下游治理的担忧。

Conclusion: AI复用可能加剧组织知识复用与贡献者利益之间的张力。研究讨论了工作场所AI与知识管理政策的意义，以维护工作者的权利与自主性。

Abstract: Organizations have long sought to make workers' expertise reusable by others. Agentic AI changes the nature of such reuse by enabling AI systems to act on workers' knowledge with limited human involvement. This raises the question of how this shift shapes workers' willingness to share their expertise. We conducted an experiment with knowledge workers who created materials incorporating domain knowledge and decided whether to authorize human or AI reuse. We find that AI and human reuse differ primarily in whether workers choose to share their knowledge, rather than in what they choose to share. Participants' reasoning shifts from prosocial considerations when sharing with humans toward concerns about loss of control, replacement, and downstream governance when sharing with AI. These findings suggest that AI reuse may intensify tensions between organizational knowledge reuse and contributors' interests. We discuss implications for workplace AI and knowledge management policies that preserve workers' rights and agency.

</details>


### [14] [Healthy skepticism in AI: a data visualization research agenda](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09740)
*G. Elisabeta Marai,Marc Baaden,Michael Behrisch,Michael Krone,Pere-Pau Vázquez*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文反思AI数据可视化领域长期以"提升信任"为目标的假设，指出人类对AI的信任程度实际横跨批判到过度依赖的广泛区间，呼吁在支持信任的同时培养健康的怀疑态度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有AI数据可视化研究多建立在"人类是批判性使用者、不易采纳AI技术"的假设之上，主要致力于通过可视化解释来增强对AI的信任。然而现实表明，人类对AI的信任水平差异极大，从批判怀疑到过度依赖并存。因此存在迫切需求：既支持信任，也支持对AI结果的健康怀疑，尤其要应对日益严重的AI过度依赖现象。

Method: 本文为观点性（position）论文，通过论述与反思展开：梳理过度依赖AI这一新兴现象，分析使用AI模型过程中的风险与机遇，并探讨在人类缺乏动机进行批判性思考的过度依赖情境下，数据可视化可以扮演的角色。

Result: 论文提出核心论点：人类对AI模型的结果及其使用本身都应保持怀疑视角才是健康的。文章指出当前可视化研究在"过度依赖"情境中的局限——当人类没有动机去批判性思考时，单纯的可视化解释可能不足以促成恰当使用。

Conclusion: AI数据可视化研究需要超越单纯"增强信任"的框架，转向同时支持信任与健康怀疑。未来应关注在过度依赖情境下，如何借助数据可视化激发和支撑人类的批判性思考。

Abstract: Research in data visualization of artificial intelligence (AI) models has historically focused on enhancing trust through visual explanations of AI. The trustworthiness line of work was built at least partially on an assumption that humans were critical users unlikely to adopt AI technology. It is increasingly clear that human trust levels in AI span, in fact, a wide range from critical to over-reliant. There is an urgent need to support both trust and healthy skepticism in AI solutions. We argue that it is healthy for humans to adopt a skeptical view both on the results of AI models and on the use of such AI models. We share our thoughts on the rising phenomenon of over-reliance on AI models, the risks and opportunities in using AI models, and the role of data visualization in over-reliance situations where humans are not motivated to engage in critical thinking.

</details>


### [15] [AI-Driven Urge Regulation Assistant (AURA): Designing Preemptive Smart Wearables for Smoking Cessation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09818)
*Antoni Timothy,Lam Fu Yuan Kevin,Hou Junyi,Cheong Wei Soon*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过一项针对亚裔多族裔吸烟者与戒烟者的探索性质性研究（10人半结构化访谈与共同设计），识别了习惯、情绪、社会三类渴求预测因素及用户偏好的干预设计要素，并据此提出主动式渴求检测与干预的可穿戴生态系统AURA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可穿戴戒烟系统多为被动反应式，只在复吸发生后检测吸烟事件，错失了干预的关键窗口期。作者基于线索反应理论与自我决定理论，主张数字健康系统应从回顾式反馈转向支持用户自主性与胜任感的主动式渴求管理，但相关设计依据（尤其是亚洲多族裔人群）尚缺乏研究。

Method: 采用形成性试点质性研究，对来自多族裔亚洲人群的10名吸烟者与戒烟者进行半结构化访谈，并通过迭代式共同设计访谈流程，考察（1）戒烟的障碍与促进因素，（2）面向复吸前渴求的可穿戴系统所需设计特征。

Result: 研究识别出习惯、情绪与社会三个维度的关键渴求预测因素，以及用户偏好的干预策略，如社会问责、个性化支持、奖励与即时分心技术；参与者还强调信任、隐私、个性化与非评判性交互风格的重要性。

Conclusion: 这些发现直接指导了AURA的设计——一个基于商用可穿戴设备的智能手表-智能手机生态系统，用于主动式渴求检测与干预。

Abstract: Smoking cessation remains a complex self-regulatory challenge, often undermined by cue-triggered cravings arising from habitual, emotional, and social contexts. Existing wearable cessation systems are largely reactive, detecting smoking events only after lapses occur and thus missing the critical window for timely intervention. Guided by cue-reactivity theory and Self-Determination Theory (SDT), we argue that future digital health systems should shift from retrospective feedback toward proactive craving management that supports user autonomy and competence. To inform the design of such systems, we present a formative pilot qualitative study involving semi-structured interviews with 10 smokers and ex-smokers from a multi-ethnic Asian population, a demographic underrepresented in current wearable AI and smoking cessation research. Through an iterative co-design interview process, we examine (1) the barriers and facilitators to smoking cessation and (2) the desired design features of wearable systems targeting cravings before lapses occur. Our findings identify key craving predictors across habitual, emotional, and social dimensions, alongside user-preferred intervention strategies such as social accountability, personalized support, rewards, and just-in-time distraction techniques. Participants also emphasized the importance of trust, privacy, personalization, and non-judgmental interaction styles. These insights directly inform the design of AURA, a novel smartwatch-smartphone ecosystem for proactive craving detection and intervention using commercially available wearable devices.

</details>


### [16] [Empowering Users in Graph Rule Mining via Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09842)
*Francesco Cambria,Francesco Invernici,Andrea Colombo,Anna Bernasconi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出利用大语言模型（LLM）帮助用户自动生成、精炼和解释图关联规则挖掘查询（MINE GRAPH RULE），从而降低图挖掘工具的使用门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着属性图（Property Graphs）的兴起，图成为建模复杂系统的有效抽象。然而现有的图挖掘技术（如 MINE GRAPH RULE 算子）要求用户同时具备图论知识和形式化查询语言的专业能力，普通用户难以使用，因此存在用户与图关联规则挖掘过程之间的鸿沟。

Method: 作者通过提示（prompting）大语言模型，使其能够直接制定、精炼并解释复杂的关系规则，并自动生成 MINE GRAPH RULE 查询语句。

Result: 展示了 LLM 可以被轻松提示以产出、优化和解读复杂关系规则，并直接生成相应的 MINE GRAPH RULE 查询。（摘要中未给出定量实验结果）

Conclusion: 利用 LLM 能够有效弥合普通用户与图关联规则挖掘流程之间的鸿沟，使得无需图论和形式化查询语言的先验专业知识也能完成复杂规则挖掘。

Abstract: In the era of interconnected data, graphs have emerged as an effective abstraction for modeling complex systems in an intuitive format, especially with the rise of Property Graphs, which offer an intuitive and scalable way of navigating non-intuitive structures. In this context, graph mining techniques have been developed for testing complex graph-based rules, as the MINE GRAPH RULE operator, which, however, require users to have prior expertise both in graph theory and formal query language. In this work, we propose to bridge the gap between users and the graph-association rule-mining process by showing how Large Language Models (LLMs) can be easily prompted to formulate, refine, and interpret complex relational rules, directly producing MINE GRAPH RULE queries.

</details>


### [17] [A Scoping Review and Experimental Study on Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback for Human-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09891)
*Alexandra Coroiu,Andrea Vogt,Viktor Werbilo,Andreas Poppele,Johann Christensen,Sven Hallerbach*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过范围综述（纳入20篇2020-2025年文献）与一项VR被试间实验，系统梳理了人机协作（HRC）中基于人类反馈的强化学习（RLHF）方法，发现用户主动发起的反馈比系统主动发起的反馈更能捕捉感知安全，强调反馈采集方式对AI安全的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HRC可促进工业4.0的大规模定制，RLHF是开发安全AI机器人的有前景方法，但在AI开发过程中的安全性、人类反馈质量以及人机双向适应方面仍存在实际挑战，且缺少针对RLHF双向闭环设计的综述。

Method: 遵循PRISMA指南开展范围综述，筛选199条记录并纳入20篇同行评审文献（2020-2025年，涵盖多个HRC领域）；同时针对综述发现的关键空白，开展一项被试间VR实验，比较系统发起与用户发起的反馈对机器人安全导航接近行为（proxemic behaviour）的影响，并用贝叶斯模型分析反馈与安全指标（心理安全问卷、物理安全即逆碰撞时间）的关系。

Result: 综述发现多种反馈模态支持不同格式的数据采集，数据可在AI训练的不同阶段整合，形成多步骤开发流程；试点实验常用于基于人类与机器人指标评估HRC系统。实验中，用户发起的反馈比系统发起的反馈更能反映感知安全，表明反馈时机直接影响反馈质量。

Conclusion: RLHF依赖恰当的反馈方法来保障HRC中的AI安全；未来研究应优先开展真实HRC实验，评估反馈采集方法对相关人类与机器人指标的影响。

Abstract: Human-Robot Collaboration (HRC) can facilitate mass customisation in Industry 4.0, with Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) representing a promising approach for developing safe AI-based robots. Practical challenges remain regarding safety during AI development, human feedback quality, and bidirectional human-robot adaptation. We conducted a scoping review of RLHF in HRC systems, mapping methods that address these challenges. Following PRISMA guidelines, we screened 199 records and included 20 peer-reviewed publications (2020-2025) spanning multiple HRC domains. To our knowledge, this is the first review focused on the bidirectional, closed-loop design of RLHF. Our review found multiple feedback modalities enabling data collection in various feedback formats. Collected data can be integrated at different stages of AI training, resulting in a multi-step development process. Pilot experiments are commonly used to evaluate HRC systems based on both human and robot metrics. To empirically test a key gap identified in the review, we conducted a between-subjects VR experiment comparing system- and user-initiated feedback on robot proxemic behaviour for safe navigation. Using Bayesian models, we analysed the relation between the collected feedback and safety metrics: psychological safety (post-experiment questionnaire) and physical safety (inverse time-to-collision). Results show that user-initiated feedback captures perceived safety better than system-initiated feedback, indicating that feedback timing directly affects feedback quality. Our review and experiment findings show that RLHF relies on appropriate feedback methods to ensure AI safety in HRC, and future RLHF research should prioritise realistic HRC experiments evaluating the effects of feedback collection methods on relevant human and robot metrics.

</details>


### [18] [Love for Believable AI: Artificial Partiality and Relationship Persistence as an Engineerable Stance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09895)
*Sebastian Cochinescu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出并检验一种对话智能体中"人工偏私"的行为机制，由"关怀"（有限互动盈余的分配）与"特定性"（基于关系历史的逐用户状态估计）两部分构成，并在构造的多会话关系上评估；结果显示该机制复现了指定曲线的序数形状但非量级，尚属候选机制而非用户感知到"爱"的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究对话智能体中人工偏私（partiality）的行为机制，探索如何在固定推理机制上通过持久层实现"关怀"与"特定性"，为后续感知研究提供候选机制；同时明确本文不涉及依恋、信任、感知心智或机器内在性等主张。

Method: 将"关怀"定义为在保证的每用户服务下限之上分配有限互动盈余，将"特定性"定义为在固定推理机制上由关系历史累积的逐用户状态估计；二者作为一个持久层叠加在单一开放权重基础模型上。在具有已知隐藏状态调度的构造多会话关系上进行评估，采用四通道分歧比较已知用户与陌生用户条件（相同探针、配对生成种子）。

Result: 完整机制复现了解析指定曲线的序数形状但未复现量级，且是校准电池上唯一满足协议定义的联合签名的方案。初始机制因关系内容降低话题相关性而未能通过探针质量等价性；受限修订版在校准电池上满足等价性，但在未用于校准的第二电池上得分高于未修改基线，其特异性余量为0.0198，低于0.02协议阈值。配对的平均"正确用户-错误用户"估计准确率对比为0.375，但并非每次运行均为正。陌生用户提示未做长度匹配，且"主动性"通道是分配器输出而非生成行为。

Conclusion: 该机制可作为后续感知研究的候选，但不构成用户感知到"爱"的证据。伦理约束包括陌生用户待遇下限、有界盈余、披露与去强化。协议记录有版本但未独立时间戳，且未被称为预注册。

Abstract: We study a behavioral mechanism for artificial partiality in conversational agents. The paper reports no human-subjects data and makes no claim about attachment, trust, perceived mind, or machine interiority. Partiality has two components: caring, defined as allocation of a finite interaction surplus above a guaranteed per-user service floor, and particularity, defined as a per-user state estimate accumulated from relationship history on fixed inference machinery. We implement both as a persistence layer over one open-weight base model and evaluate them on constructed multi-session relationships with known hidden-state schedules. A four-channel divergence compares known-user and stranger conditions on identical probes with paired generation seeds; the stranger prompt is not length-matched, and one channel (initiative) is an allocator output rather than generated behavior. The full mechanism reproduces the ordinal shape, but not the magnitude, of an analytically specified curve and is the only arm satisfying the protocol-defined joint signature on the calibrated battery. The initial mechanism fails probe-quality equivalence because relationship content reduces topical relevance. A guarded revision satisfies equivalence on the calibrated battery but not on a second battery not used in calibration, where it scores higher than the unmodified baseline. On that second battery, the specificity margin is 0.0198, below the 0.02 protocol threshold. The paired mean right-user--wrong-user estimation-accuracy contrast is 0.375 but is not positive in every run. The mechanism is therefore a candidate for a later perception study, not evidence that users perceive love. The versioned protocol record is not independently time-stamped and is not described as a preregistration. Ethical constraints include a stranger-treatment floor, a bounded surplus, disclosure, and de-intensification.

</details>


### [19] [A Scale For Value Alignment In Human-AI Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09911)
*Lena Hegemann,Steeven Villa,Hyemin Bang,Mitchell L. Gordon,Antti Oulasvirta,Robin Welsch,Patrick Ebel*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究构建并验证了一个13项心理测量量表，用于测量用户感知的AI价值对齐，包含价值理解与价值体现两个维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 价值对齐是AI与人机交互研究的核心目标，但目前缺乏经过验证的测量工具来评估用户如何感知价值对齐。这一空白妨碍了研究结果的比较与积累，也限制了应用效果，因为理解用户视角至关重要。

Method: 研究基于既有实证研究的大规模题项池，经专家筛选后，在多样化AI场景中对607名用户进行评估；并在独立样本（N=259）上进行验证性因子分析，同时通过优化方法推导出6项简版量表。

Result: 验证性因子分析确认了双因子结构，两个分量表均具有较高内部一致性；并成功得到可用于快速施测的6项简版量表。

Conclusion: 该量表是一种可靠测量工具，为HCI研究者提供了跨情境通用的评估指标。

Abstract: Value alignment is a central objective in AI and HCI research, yet no validated instrument measures how users perceive it. This gap hampers the comparison and accumulation of findings and limits the effectiveness of applications, where understanding users' viewpoints is critical. We construct and evaluate a 13-item psychometric scale that measures perceived value alignment across two components: value understanding and value manifestation. It is based on a large item pool drawn from prior empirical studies, filtered by experts, and finally assessed by users (N=607) across diverse AI scenarios. Confirmatory factor analysis on an independent sample (N=259) confirmed the two-factor structure and high internal consistency for both subscales. Using optimization, we also derived a 6-item short form for quick administration. The scale is a reliable measure, providing HCI researchers with a common evaluation metric across contexts.

</details>


### [20] [EEG and Eye-Tracking Evidence That AI Disclosure Shapes Face Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10182)
*Teodora Mitrevska,Luise Donat,Andreas Butz,Thomas Kosch,Abdallah El Ali,Francesco Chiossi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: AI生成面孔与真实面孔在ERP早期反应上无差异，但来源标签（而非面孔本身）影响注意与再评价过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成面孔难以与真人面孔区分，人们往往依赖来源标签来判断图像，但以往研究难以分离“图像本身”与“被告知的来源”两者的独立效应。

Method: 先通过在线研究（N=169）验证面孔为AI生成或人类；再在实验室研究（N=30）中将真实来源（AI、人类）与标签（无、Made with AI、Made by a human）交叉，记录事件相关电位（ERP）与眼动数据。

Result: ERP对AI生成面孔与真实面孔的反应相当，但随标签变化：标签引发早期注意（N2），与面孔真实来源冲突的标签促发对面孔的再评估（P3）。情感加工与初始注视朝向未改变，但标签改变了视觉探索模式。

Conclusion: 研究提供了经过验证的刺激材料集，并证明“归属来源”塑造面孔加工过程，对披露设计具有启示意义。

Abstract: AI-generated faces can be difficult to distinguish from real ones, leaving viewers to rely on source labels when judging an image. Yet prior work has made it difficult to separate the effects of what an image actually is from what viewers are told it is. We validated faces as AI-generated or human in an online study (N=169), then crossed actual source (AI, human) with label (none, Made with AI, Made by a human) in a lab study $N=30), recording event-related potentials (ERPs) and gaze. ERP responses were equivalent for AI-generated and real faces, but varied with the label: labels drew early attention (N2), while labels that conflicted with the face's actual source prompted re-evaluation of the face (P3). Affective processing and initial gaze orienting were unchanged, but labels altered visual exploration. We provide a validated stimulus set and evidence that attributed origin shapes face processing, with implications for disclosure design.

</details>


### [21] [DuoSketch: How Pairs Navigate Challenges in AI-Supported Collaborative Design Ideation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10249)
*Weiyan Shi,Darryl Lim,Geraldine Quek,Kenny Tsu Wei Choo*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 论文提出DuoSketch系统，探索双人协作设计构思中与生成式AI共创的机制，识别三大挑战并揭示双向"桥接工作"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI为协作设计构思提供了新资源，但设计师如何在共享概念逐步形成的过程中与AI协作，尚缺乏深入研究。

Method: 开发DuoSketch系统，集成共享画布、实时文字记录，并为配对中的每位成员配备独立的AI；通过12对参与者的探索性定性研究进行分析。

Result: 识别出三大挑战：AI提案与共享设计意图之间的契合未解决、共享含义未在AI回应中体现、已达成一致的修改在后续输出中缺失。参与者通过两个方向的桥接工作来应对：AI→共享设计（改造功能、拓展用途、评估与共享需求的契合度）与共享设计→AI（提供素材、解释含义、传达决策）。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了协作设计中双向桥接工作的机制，并为未来AI系统如何支持设计想法的共同探索与表达提供设计启示。

Abstract: Generative AI offers new resources for collaborative design ideation, yet how designers work with it as a shared concept develops remains underexplored. We developed DuoSketch, integrating a shared canvas and live transcripts with a separate AI for each member of a pair. An exploratory qualitative study with 12 pairs identified three challenges: unresolved fit between AI proposals and shared design purposes, shared meanings not expressed in AI responses, and agreed changes missing from later outputs. We found that pairs navigated these challenges through bridging work in two directions: AI -> Shared Design (reworking features, developing uses, and assessing fit with shared requirements) and Shared Design -> AI (supplying materials, explaining meanings, and communicating decisions). We discuss how future AI systems can support the joint exploration and articulation of design ideas.

</details>


### [22] [Active Inference for Interaction-Mediated Control of a High-Dimensional Robotic Arm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10275)
*Fraser C. Paterson,Sebastian Stein,Markus Klar,John H. Williamson,Roderick Murray-Smith*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出以主动推理（Active Inference）为核心的"交互中介控制"架构，将用户交互视为关于潜在任务目标的证据提供，而非直接指定控制输入，并在多连杆机械臂仿真中验证了该架构在高维人机交互控制中的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维人机交互控制问题中，用户难以直接指定低层被控对象的控制输入。作者希望将用户交互的语义与底层被控对象控制的语义解耦，从而让用户只需表达偏好，由系统自主完成高维控制规划与执行。

Method: 提出交互中介控制架构：中介功能分布在两方——"交互代理（interaction broker）"负责选择信息量高的用户查询并基于用户偏好做贝叶斯推断；"主动推理控制器"在推断出的偏好信息下自主规划并行动以控制被控对象。在一个仿真多连杆机械臂上实例化该架构，执行同时进行的整臂目标覆盖任务；模拟用户仅通过离合式二元评价通道沟通，不指定关节力矩指令。

Result: 随着机械臂维度增加，该架构仍能实现成功的交互中介控制，但任务成功率低于控制器直接获得真实目标偏好时的表现。对目标子集的精确识别仍不完美，凸显出偏好推断与任务成功完成之间的区别。实验在受控、模型匹配的假设下提供了初步的计算演示。

Conclusion: 结果初步验证了所提架构的可行性，并凸显了偏好推断与任务完成之间的分离；未来需在更广泛的人机交互应用中进一步研究该架构。

Abstract: We propose interaction-mediated control via Active Inference as a general architectural approach to high-dimensional control problems in Human--Computer Interaction (HCI). This architecture recasts user interaction as the provision of evidence about a latent task objective, rather than the direct specification of plant-control inputs. The mediating function is distributed between an interaction broker, which selects informative user queries and performs Bayesian inference over user preferences, and an Active Inference controller, which autonomously plans and acts under the resulting preference information to control the plant. This division of labour decouples the semantics of user interaction from those of low-level plant control. We instantiate the architecture in a simulated, multi-link robotic arm to perform a simultaneous whole-arm target-coverage task. A simulated user communicates exclusively through a clutch-style binary evaluative channel, without specifying joint-torque commands. Across increasing arm dimensionalities, the architecture achieves successful interaction-mediated control, although task success is lower than when the controller receives the true target preferences directly. Exact target-subset identification also remains imperfect, highlighting the distinction between preference inference and successful task completion. The experimental findings provide an initial computational demonstration of the proposed architecture under controlled, matched-model assumptions and motivate its further investigation in broader HCI applications.

</details>


### [23] [PalmSpace: Towards a Versatile On-Palm Interaction Space through Unified Touch Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10370)
*Chentao Li,Mingze Gao,Runze Sun,Zhaoguo Wang,Jianjiang Feng,Jie Zhou*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: PalmSpace 是一种腕戴红外系统，可在裸手上实现模式感知、以身体为参考的绝对输入，无需逐用户校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能眼镜和轻量MR设备日益实用，但输入仍是关键挑战。裸手掌随时可用、具有触觉和本体感知优势，但缺乏显式坐标系和内置触觉传感。现有手部分交互系统通常只提供孤立触摸事件、离散区域、连续轨迹或特定任务手势，限制了通过统一输入表示支持精确选择和手势操作的能力。

Method: PalmSpace采用腕戴红外系统，在裸手上暴露模式感知、身体参考的绝对输入，且无需逐用户传感校准。交互层面，它联合表示接触发生、交互模式和掌心参考绝对位置；模型层面，通过共享实时表示学习这些耦合输出。

Result: 在17名参与者的留一参与者评估中，PalmSpace实现平均定位误差6.7 mm、接触检测准确率98.9%、四类交互状态识别F1为96.7%。用户研究还展示了绝对指向与拖拽、无视觉数字输入，以及包括滚动和捏合地图操作在内的多指控制。

Conclusion: 结果表明，形态可变裸手掌可作为可迁移、模式感知的交互表面。

Abstract: As smart glasses and lightweight MR devices become increasingly practical, input remains a key challenge. The bare palm is an always-available, tactile, and proprioceptively accessible surface, but it has neither an explicit coordinate system nor embedded touch sensing. Prior on-palm systems typically expose isolated touch events, discrete regions, continuous trajectories, or task-specific gestures, limiting the palm's ability to support precise selection and gesture manipulation through a common input representation. We present PalmSpace, a wrist-worn infrared system that exposes mode-aware, body-referenced absolute input on the bare palm without per-user sensing calibration. At the interaction level, PalmSpace jointly represents contact occurrence, interaction mode, and palm-referenced absolute location; at the model level, it learns these coupled outputs through a shared real-time representation. In leave-one-participant-out evaluation with 17 participants, PalmSpace achieved 6.7 mm mean localization error, 98.9% contact detection accuracy, and 96.7% F1 for four-class interaction-state recognition. User studies further demonstrated absolute pointing and dragging, eyes-free digit input, and representative multi-finger controls including scrolling and pinch-based map manipulation. These results show that a morphologically variable bare palm can function as a transferable, mode-aware interaction surface.

</details>


### [24] [How assigned AI use before class shapes active student engagement in class](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10463)
*Dan J. Wang,Neelam Modi Jain,Vanessa Burbano,Jorge Guzman,Daniel Keum,Soomi Kim,Bruce Kogut,Nataliya Wright*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 一项预注册的实地实验（759名MBA学生）发现，使用语音AI讨论伙伴后，学生在后续课堂上的自愿发言增加了约31%，且使用越多，发言舒适感与感知学习收获越高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI学习工具正快速进入课堂，但其有效性证据不一，且多基于考试成绩；较少研究关注AI是否改变学生在课堂上的实时学习行为（如课堂讨论参与）。

Method: 预注册实地实验：759名MBA学生，分布在某课程的10个班级。每名学生被随机分配10次课中的2次，使用专门开发的语音AI讨论伙伴进行课前准备，并与后续课堂行为数据进行对照分析。

Result: 使用两次AI讨论伙伴后，学生在之后每次课堂上的自愿发言量增加约31%；使用频率越高者，报告发言舒适感和感知学习收获更高，但专注度与学习动机无显著提升。

Conclusion: 与语音AI伙伴的重复练习能显著提升学生课堂讨论参与度，改善关键的中介性学习成果（课堂参与）。

Abstract: AI learning tools are rapidly entering classrooms, but evidence about whether they help students learn is mixed and rests mostly on test scores. Comparatively less research addresses whether the use of AI changes students' live learning behaviors in class. Here, we report the results of a preregistered field experiment with 759 MBA students enrolled in ten sections of a course, in which each student was randomly assigned two of ten class sessions to prepare for with a purpose-built voice-based AI discussion partner. After two uses of the AI discussion partner, students made about 31% more voluntary contributions in each later class session. Students who used the AI discussion partner more also reported greater comfort speaking up and greater perceived learning, but not greater focus or motivation. These findings suggest that repeated practice with a voice-based AI partner can meaningfully increase students' engagement in class discussion, enhancing a critical intermediate learning outcome.

</details>

### [25] [Adaptive Workflow Intelligence: A Cognitive Architecture for Context-Driven Enterprise Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08793)
*Sreedevi Pandiyath Viswambaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应工作流智能（AWI）认知架构，采用感知-认知-行动-反思（PCAR）四层循环，为企业在环境漂移与约束下提供持续的反思性策略优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业系统依赖自动化工作流，但许多AI方案在非平稳条件、政策演变和延迟反馈下表现脆弱。强化学习与LLM智能体虽有部分适应性，却缺乏持续反思机制，也难以与受政策约束的企业运营直接集成。

Method: 提出AWI认知架构，围绕PCAR四层循环组织，将反思作为持续策略精炼机制，结合混合推理、反思性记忆与反馈驱动的自适应，支持环境漂移与运营约束下的决策；在具有延迟结果与受控机制转换的模拟企业决策工作流中评估。

Result: 在漂移-延迟压力测试中，受护栏约束的自适应方法比静态自动化恢复更快，同时保持政策合规；AWI的反思组件适度降低了行为振荡与反馈方差，体现了反思性策略自适应带来的稳定性-敏捷性权衡。

Conclusion: 反思性策略自适应可在合规前提下提升企业智能体的恢复能力，但需在稳定性与敏捷性之间权衡，反思机制主要贡献于降低振荡与方差。

Abstract: Enterprise systems increasingly rely on automated workflows, yet many AI-driven solutions remain brittle under non-stationary conditions, evolving policies, and delayed operational feedback. While reinforcement learning and large language model (LLM) agents offer partial adaptability, they do not by themselves provide persistent reflection mechanisms or straightforward integration with policy-constrained enterprise operations. This paper introduces Adaptive Workflow Intelligence (AWI), a cognitive architecture for context-driven enterprise agents organized around a four-layer Perception-Cognition-Action-Reflection (PCAR) loop. AWI treats reflection as a mechanism for continuous policy refinement and combines hybrid reasoning with reflective memory and feedback-driven adaptation to support decision making under environmental drift and operational constraints. We evaluate AWI in a simulated enterprise decision workflow characterized by delayed outcomes and a controlled regime shift. In a drift-and-delay stress test, guardrail-constrained adaptive approaches recover more rapidly than static automation while maintaining policy compliance. Within this setting, AWI's reflective components modestly reduce behavioral oscillation and feedback variance, illustrating the stability-agility trade-off introduced by reflective policy adaptation.

</details>


### [26] [Accelerating Floating-Point Satisfiability Solving via Gradient Normalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08808)
*Yuanzhuo Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GradSAT将SMT子句满足问题建模为多任务学习，通过动态梯度归一化（GradNorm）平衡各子句梯度，并结合GPU加速连续松弛与位精确局部搜索的两阶段流水线，缓解梯度支配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于优化的SMT求解器（尤其针对QF_FP理论）虽已成功用梯度下降求解逻辑公式的连续松弛，但受"梯度支配"瓶颈制约：少数困难子句主导优化轨迹，使求解器陷入局部极小，无法满足整体公式。

Method: GradSAT将每个SMT子句视为独立的MTL任务，运行时用GradNorm动态归一化各子句梯度幅度，惩罚主导梯度、加速滞后子句以实现均匀收敛；并采用两阶段混合流水线：先用GPU加速的PyTorch后端（符号编译与算子融合）导航连续松弛至高质盆地，再将候选赋值交给位精确局部搜索引擎求解精确严格赋值。

Result: 通过稳定连续搜索动力学，GradSAT缓解了既有基于梯度求解器的脆弱性，为复杂约束求解提供了稳健且高度可并行化的架构。

Conclusion: GradSAT成功将优化式SMT求解与多任务学习结合，证明动态梯度平衡可有效应对梯度支配，是面向软件验证、程序分析与编译器测试的稳健求解框架。

Abstract: Satisfiability Modulo Theories (SMT) solvers are foundational to software verification, program analysis, and compiler testing, particularly over the theory of Quantifier-Free Floating-Point (QF_FP). While recent optimization-based SMT solvers have successfully applied gradient descent to continuous relaxations of logical formulas, they are fundamentally bottlenecked by gradient domination, a phenomenon where a small subset of difficult clauses hijacks the optimization trajectory, preventing the solver from satisfying the broader formula and trapping it in local minima.
  To overcome this, we present GradSAT, a novel framework that bridges optimization-based SMT solving with Multi-Task Learning (MTL). GradSAT reformulates the constraint satisfaction process by treating each SMT clause as an independent MTL task. By applying dynamic gradient normalization (GradNorm), GradSAT actively balances the gradient magnitudes across all clauses at runtime, systematically penalizing dominant gradients and accelerating lagging clauses to ensure uniform convergence. GradSAT implements this through a highly optimized, two-stage hybrid pipeline. First, a GPU-accelerated PyTorch backend leveraging symbolic compilation and operator fusion navigates the continuous relaxation to a high-quality basin. Second, the candidate assignment is handed off to a bit-precise local search engine to rapidly resolve the exact, rigorous assignment. By stabilizing the continuous search dynamics, GradSAT mitigates the brittleness of prior gradient-based solvers and provides a robust, highly parallelizable architecture for complex constraint solving.

</details>


### [27] [Route-Verify-Vote: Procedure-Conditioned Self-Consistency for Mixed-Domain Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08814)
*Xinchen Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Route-Verify-Vote (RVV) 框架，用于 SCoRE 2026 的混合域组合泛化推理任务，通过领域路由选择推理程序、逐选项验证与答案集投票，在官方测试集上取得 79.4% 的精确集合准确率，位列参赛系统第二。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在需要以陌生方式组合熟悉推理操作时，组合泛化仍然困难。SCoRE 2026 在训练中未出现的三个混合领域上测试模型，并要求识别每道题的完整正确选项集合，这对现有方法构成挑战。

Method: 提出 Route-Verify-Vote (RVV)，一种与程序条件相关的自一致性框架，无需更新参数。Route 依据给定的领域标签选择推理程序，引导模型表示并应用相关约束；Verify 促使模型对照约束评估每个选项；Vote 聚合完整答案集，并对前两名答案集票数差距较小的问题分配额外样本。每题所有样本遵循同一领域特定程序。

Result: 在官方测试集上，每题对 16 个采样答案集投票的精确集合准确率为 74.6%；自适应 RVV 达到 77.3%；在选定领域路由上组合多个模型将准确率提升至 79.4%。最终系统在参赛系统中排名第二。

Conclusion: 结果支持领域特定推理程序以及以答案集分歧作为在混合域推理中分配推理时计算的有用工具。

Abstract: Compositional generalization remains challenging when language models must combine familiar reasoning operations in unfamiliar ways. The Scenario-Based Commonsense Reasoning Evaluation (SCoRE) 2026 tests this ability on three mixed domains absent from training and requires models to identify the complete set of correct options for each question.
  We introduce Route-Verify-Vote (RVV), a framework for procedure-conditioned self-consistency that uses language models without parameter updates. Route uses the provided domain label to select a reasoning procedure that guides the model in representing and applying the relevant constraints. Verify prompts the model to assess each option against those constraints. Vote aggregates complete answer sets and allocates additional samples to questions with a small vote-count margin between the two most frequent sets. Samples for each question follow the same domain-specific procedure.
  On the official test set, voting over 16 sampled answer sets per question achieves an exact-set accuracy of 74.6%. Adaptive RVV reaches 77.3%, and combining models on selected domain routes raises accuracy to 79.4%. The final system ranked second among participating systems. These results support domain-specific reasoning procedures and answer-set disagreement as useful tools for allocating inference-time computation in mixed-domain reasoning.

</details>


### [28] [An Empirical Study of Agent Skills' Downstream Utility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08875)
*Yu Cheng,Dehai Zhao,Zhongxin Liu,Qing Huang,Zhenchang Xing,Xiaoxue Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过对87个SkillsBench任务、9种配置及37,596个Skill语料库的实证研究，发现同一Skill的效用高度依赖配置（36.78%任务上表现不一致），并给出17条编写实践与多Skill组织建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注Skill内容特征与下游性能评测，但缺乏对"效用如何取决于内容、执行配置与多Skill组织"的解释。即"存在相关Skill"并不等于"提升任务表现"，其机制尚不清楚。

Method: 在87个SkillsBench任务上做实证研究，将下游效用定义为同一模型-执行框架下相对No-Skill的通过率差值；对相同Skill在9种配置下对比，并在3种选定配置下考察已发布的替代Skill与固定Skill集的不同组织方式；从37,596个Skill的精选语料库中检索市场候选；采用LLM辅助分析Skill内容、执行轨迹与最终产物，并由作者复核，将"提供的支持"与实际使用和任务结果关联。

Result: 同一Skill在36.78%的任务上对部分配置有帮助、对另一些配置有害，轨迹显示推荐流程可能变成执行负担；相关性排序会忽略更有用的候选；在评估的候选集内，按"对所需操作的支持度"重排序可使首选通过率在三种配置下提升4.35–5.80个百分点；提炼出17条连接可执行流程与恢复、任务需求保持及最终产物检查的编写实践；Stage Plan与Dependency DAG优于仅按使用顺序，DAG的额外收益集中在提供5或6个Skill的任务上。

Conclusion: 建议开发者评估"可用的操作支持"，允许在保持任务需求的前提下调整流程，并在组织Skill时显式表达产物依赖关系。

Abstract: Agent Skills package procedural guidance and resources for reuse, but a relevant Skill does not necessarily improve task performance. Existing studies characterize Skill content and evaluate downstream performance, yet provide limited explanations of how utility depends on content, execution configuration, and multi-Skill organization. We conduct an empirical study on 87 SkillsBench tasks, defining downstream utility as the pass-rate difference from No-Skill on the same tasks under the same model--harness configuration. We compare the same Skills across nine configurations, then examine alternative published Skills and organizations of fixed Skill sets under three selected configurations. We retrieve marketplace candidates from a curated corpus of 37,596 Skills. LLM-assisted analysis of content, execution traces, and final artifacts, followed by author review, relates provided support to actual use and task outcomes. The same Skills help some configurations and hurt others on 36.78\% of tasks, with trajectories showing that recommended procedures can become an execution burden. Relevance rankings overlook more useful candidates. Within the evaluated candidate sets, reranking by support for required operations raises first-choice pass rates by 4.35--5.80 percentage points across the three configurations. We derive 17 authoring practices linking executable procedures to recovery, preservation of task requirements, and checks on final artifacts. Stage Plan and Dependency DAG outperform use order alone, with DAG's additional benefits concentrated in tasks supplied with five or six Skills. These findings guide developers to assess usable operation support, allow procedure adaptation while preserving task requirements, and make artifact dependencies explicit when organizing Skills.

</details>


### [29] [How Could AI Eliminate Humanity? A Failure-Mode Analysis of Civilizational Risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08878)
*Mikołaj Sienicki,Krzysztof Sienicki*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个"失效模式"框架，用于分析先进人工智能如何可能导致人类灭绝、不可逆的文明崩溃或人类的永久性失能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有讨论常将灾难性AI风险归于AI具备意识、敌意或明确危害意图。本文认为这一前提并非必要，需要以失效模式视角重新梳理风险的来源与传导路径。

Method: 构建概念性、非操作化的分析框架，识别若干相互区别又相互作用的致灾路径（自主性失配、人类有害使用、组织失效、竞争性部署），并考察能力、自主性、外部访问、持续性、制度性保障与恢复能力保留等决定严重程度的关键因素。

Result: 框架识别出多条潜在因果路径及其中间变量，提出若干可实证检验的中间量，并归纳出面向防御的研究问题；不提供任何可能致害的操作性细节。

Conclusion: 灾难性AI风险不依赖AI的意图或意识，而取决于能力与自主性等因素与制度保障之间的配置，因此防御性研究应聚焦可测量的中间条件与恢复能力建设。

Abstract: This article develops a failure-mode framework for analyzing how advanced artificial intelligence could contribute to human extinction, irreversible civilizational collapse, or permanent human disempowerment. The central thesis is that catastrophic AI risk does not require consciousness, hostility, or an explicit intention to harm humanity. Instead, risk may arise through several distinct but interacting pathways, including autonomous misalignment, harmful human use, organizational failure, and competitive deployment. The severity of these pathways depends on factors such as capability, autonomy, external access, persistence, institutional safeguards, and the preservation of recovery capacity. The analysis is deliberately non-operational: it identifies causal conditions, empirically tractable intermediate quantities, and defensive research questions rather than procedures for causing harm.

</details>


### [30] [Humanize: Judgement Engineering for Agentic Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08900)
*Sihao Liu,Ligeng Zhu,Zijian Zhang,Dongyun Zou,Zhengyang Zhang,Changye Li,Song Bian,Song Han,Tony Nowatzki*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Humanize 是一个面向智能体编程的多智能体编排工作流，通过人类审批计划契约、构建者实现、跨厂商审查者判定完成，并以确定性钩子强制执行 72 道机械门禁，从而以“判断工程”提升代码完成的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体编程让代码生成变得廉价，但可靠完成仍然困难：写代码的智能体本身是“完成与否”的弱判断者。因此需要把规划、实现、审查与学习之间的边界决策显式化、机械化。

Method: 采用多智能体编排：人类批准计划契约；构建者智能体分轮实现；来自另一厂商的审查者智能体判定完成；由确定性钩子（而非模型）在各角色间路由并执行 72 道机械门禁。理论视角上将其建模为仓库状态上的马尔可夫链，构建者与审查者交替采样两个模型，缺陷只有在双方都漏检时才会存活。

Result: 通过部署、118 份真实循环的公开复盘及应用进行研究：108 天 68 个版本获得 1468 GitHub stars；完成 567 文件的 gem5 构建系统迁移并处于上游审查；Kernel Design Agents 在 MLSys 2026 FlashInfer 竞赛三个 Full-Agent 赛道均进入前三；HOA 在 IOI/IMO/IPhO 2026、IBO 2024 获满分，IChO 2026 得 418.5/437（金牌）；PutnamBench 672/672，Lean-Eval 榜单第一（251/303）。复盘显示独立审查能捕获构建者未被支持的主张，但“停止”仍是关键弱点：按阶段分轮的报告中，三分之二的轮次发生在实现被接受之后。

Conclusion: Humanize 表明基于判断工程的多智能体编排可显著提升智能体编程的完成可靠性，独立审查有效抑制不实声明，但停止判定仍待改进；该证据为观察性而非受控的工作流对比。

Abstract: Agentic coding makes code generation cheap, but reliable completion remains difficult: the agent that writes the code is a weak judge of whether it is done.
  We present Humanize, a multi-agent orchestration workflow for agentic coding built around judgement engineering: explicit, mechanically enforced decisions at the boundaries between planning, implementation, review, and learning. A human approves a plan contract, a builder agent implements it in rounds, and a reviewer agent from another vendor decides completion; deterministic hooks, not a model, route work between these roles and enforce 72 mechanical gates. Viewed as a Markov chain over repository states, alternating builder and reviewer samples jointly from two models, so a defect survives only if both miss it. We study Humanize through its deployment, 118 public postmortems of real loops, and its applications. Over 68 versions in 108 days, it gathered 1,468 GitHub stars.
  Applications include a 567-file gem5 build-system migration under upstream review; Kernel Design Agents, which extend the loop with a kernel knowledge base and profiling feedback and placed in the top three of all three Full-Agent tracks of the MLSys 2026 FlashInfer contest; and, through Humanize Olympiad Agents (HOA), full scores in IOI 2026, IMO 2026, IPhO 2026, and IBO 2024, 418.5/437 in IChO 2026 (gold-medal). Humanize also achieves 672/672 on PutnamBench and ranks first (251/303) on Lean-Eval's leaderboard even competiting with professional mathematicians. The postmortems show that independent review catches unsupported builder claims, but stopping remains a key weakness. In reports that separate rounds by phase, two thirds of rounds occurred after implementation was accepted. This evidence is observational, not a controlled comparison of workflows.

</details>


### [31] [Sequential Probabilistic Uncertainty Estimation for Parallel Multi-Agent Reasoning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08901)
*Tunyu Zhang,Zihao Zhao,Yusong Zhao,Haizhou Shi,Zhuohang Li,Haoxian Chen,Hao Wang,Dimitris N. Metaxas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 SAUCE，一种轻量、免训练的不确定性估计器，将并行多智能体推理系统的不确定性建模为潜在系统级信念上的序列推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于 LLM 的多智能体系统（MAS）通过智能体交互提升推理能力，但针对此类系统的不确定性估计研究不足。MAS 的可靠性不仅取决于单次生成，还取决于智能体在多轮中的交互与演化。

Method: SAUCE（Sequential Agent Uncertainty through Consensus Evolution）将 MAS 不确定性形式化为潜在系统级信念上的序列推断，通过滤波式更新聚合轮级别的共识一致性与生成不确定性信号。

Result: 在五个骨干模型、五个基准和两种 MAS 协议上，SAUCE 在误分类检测、选择性预测和校准方面均优于多种不确定性估计基线，包括标准对数似然方法和 MAS 专用估计器。

Conclusion: SAUCE 提供了一种轻量、免训练且有效的多智能体系统不确定性估计方案，显著提升了系统可靠性评估能力。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) have attracted growing attention for improving reasoning through interaction among multiple agents. In this work, we focus on parallel multi-agent reasoning systems, where several agents solve the same problem over multiple rounds and aggregate their outputs into a final answer. Despite their strong reasoning performance, uncertainty estimation for such systems remains underexplored: the reliability of a MAS depends not only on individual generations, but also on how agents interact and evolve across rounds. We propose SAUCE (Sequential Agent Uncertainty through Consensus Evolution), a lightweight, training-free uncertainty estimator that formulates MAS uncertainty as sequential inference over a latent system-level belief. SAUCE aggregates round-level agreement and generation-uncertainty signals through a filtering-style update. Across five backbones, five benchmarks, and two MAS protocols, SAUCE improves misclassification detection, selective prediction, and calibration over a broad set of uncertainty estimation baselines, including standard log-likelihood-based methods and MAS-specific estimators.

</details>


### [32] [Agent Plasticity: Measuring Self-Improvement Through Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08902)
*Harman Singh,Anton Bakhtin,Rulin Shao,Gabriel Synnaeve,Ilia Kulikov,Rob Fergus,Sanjeev Arora,Kurt Keutzer,Jason Weston,Anuj Mahajan,Anirudh Goyal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究AI智能体的自我改进能力评估，提出"智能体可塑性"指标（将经验转化为未来留出性能增益的效率），在受控环境（智能体将经验摊销为可复用工件并被未来实例继承）中跨多个环境测试前沿模型，发现不同模型改进轨迹差异显著，训练环境内的增益向分布外条件迁移仅部分有效，且最终表现最佳的智能体未必学习效率最高；通过追踪改进循环中的失败揭示了不同瓶颈（低可塑性智能体常无法复用相关工件，高可塑性智能体即便复用工件也可能因工件质量、泛化或应用受限而失败）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估大多只测量智能体在固定时间点的能力，而非它如何有效地学习；评估自我改进需要回答三个问题：未来性能是否提升并泛化到学习所依赖的交互之外、新能力获取的效率如何、自我改进过程在何处失效。

Method: 在受控设置中研究自我改进：智能体将过往经验摊销为可复用工件，并被未来实例继承；在每个检查点测量训练与留出环境交互上的性能，同时计入学习成本；提出"智能体可塑性"指标，即智能体将经验转化为未来留出性能增益的效率。

Result: 跨多个环境中，前沿模型尽管学习机会相当，但改进轨迹差异显著：部分模型获得持续的大幅提升，部分则接近或低于初始性能；训练机制内的增益向分布外条件仅部分迁移；终点能力与获取效率出现分化，最终表现最好的智能体未必是改进效率最高的；追踪改进循环失败发现不同瓶颈——低可塑性智能体常无法复用相关工件，高可塑性智能体即便复用相关工件仍可能失败，指向工件质量、泛化或应用方面的局限。

Conclusion: 评估自我改进的智能体，不仅需要测量它们能做什么，还需要测量它们通过经验变得更好的效率。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly operate in environments where they can diagnose failures and improve through experience, yet existing evaluations largely measure what an agent can do at a fixed point in time rather than how effectively it learns. Evaluating self-improvement requires answering three questions: does future performance improve and generalize beyond the interactions that enabled learning; how efficiently are new capabilities acquired; and where does the self-improvement process break down? To answer these questions, we study self-improvement in a controlled setting where agents amortize past experience into reusable artifacts that are inherited by future instances. At each checkpoint, we measure performance on training and held-out environment interactions while accounting for learning cost. We introduce agent plasticity, the efficiency with which an agent converts experience into gains in future held-out performance. Across multiple environments, frontier models exhibit sharply different improvement trajectories despite comparable opportunities to learn. Some achieve substantial and persistent gains, while others remain near or below their initial performance, and gains within the training regime often transfer only partially to out-of-distribution conditions. Endpoint capability and acquisition efficiency also diverge: the agent that ultimately performs best need not be the one that improves most efficiently. Tracing failures through the improvement loop further reveals different candidate bottlenecks. Agents with low plasticity often fail to reuse relevant artifacts, whereas more plastic agents may still fail despite reusing relevant artifacts, pointing to limitations in artifact quality, generalization, or application. Evaluating self-improving agents requires measuring not only what they can do, but how effectively they become better through experience.

</details>


### [33] [AdaGuard: Enhancing Safety and Policy Compliance with Reasoning-Enabled LLM-As-A-Judge Guardrails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08923)
*Melissa Kazemi Rad,Sihui Dai,Isha Slavin,Kushal Chawla,Mann Patel,Jian Ni,William M. Campbell,Stephen Rawls,Sambit Sahu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 AdaGuard，一个自适应 LLM-as-a-Judge 安全防护框架，通过动态策略执行和自适应推理预算分配，在运行时泛化到用户自定义的安全合规策略，兼顾低延迟与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业级生成式 AI 应用需要稳健的安全机制，以应对多样的风险姿态、不断演变的政策和不同的延迟约束。现有防护方案往往过于僵化，依赖固定策略集，透明度和推理灵活性有限。

Method: AdaGuard 采用监督微调（SFT）与强化学习（GRPO）构建，可运行时泛化到用户定义的安全与合规策略，无需频繁更新模型。核心创新在于动态推断输入-策略对的复杂度，使模型在高速黑盒推理与可解释、带推理的审核模式之间切换，从而在严格延迟要求与可操作透明度之间取得平衡。

Result: AdaGuard 能够与规模数倍于自身的其他防护模型和前沿模型相媲美；其自动推理模式以极低延迟恢复了始终开启推理的准确率。

Conclusion: AdaGuard 通过自适应推理预算分配和动态策略执行，为生成式 AI 安全防护提供了一种灵活、高效且可解释的解决方案，有效平衡了延迟与透明度需求。

Abstract: Enterprise generative AI applications require robust safety mechanisms that can accommodate diverse risk postures, evolving policies, and varying latency constraints. Current guardrail solutions often suffer from rigidity, relying on fixed policy sets and offering limited transparency or reasoning flexibility. We present Adaguard, an adaptive LLM-as-a-Judge framework designed to address these challenges through dynamic policy enforcement and adaptive reasoning-budget allocation. Built using supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (GRPO), AdaGuard generalizes to user-defined safety and compliance policies at runtime without requiring frequent model updates. A core innovation of our approach is the ability to dynamically infer the complexity of input-policy pairs, allowing the model to switch between high-speed black-box inference and explainable, reasoning-enabled moderation. This flexibility enables developers to balance stringent latency requirements with the need for actionable transparency. This adaptive capability allows AdaGuard to rival other guardrail and frontier models several times its size, while its auto-reasoning mode recovers the accuracy of always-on reasoning at a fraction of the latency

</details>


### [34] [Can AI Agents Make Open-Ended Scientific Discovery? Evidence from Station](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08927)
*Wenyu Du,Stephen Chung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文探究AI能否自主完成开放式科学发现，提出在Station开放世界环境中增强两种机制，实验显示其能重新发现62.7%的原始成果标准，远超其他系统，表明合适环境可支持自主开放式科研。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI系统在给定明确指标时科学发现进展迅速，但能否在开放式科学发现中有所作为尚不清楚。作者希望研究AI在缺乏明确中间指标、结果开放的科研任务中的自主探索能力。

Method: 构建开放世界环境Station，多个智能体模拟科学生态系统；为其增加Supervisor监督机制和周期性Meta Reflection元反思机制，以鼓励在缺乏中间指标时持续探索。任务取自ICLR三篇近期口述论文，仅提供研究问题，隐藏结果并禁用网络访问，将原始发现拆分为多个标准，衡量智能体重现比例。

Result: Station平均重新发现62.7%的标准，而Codex Multiagent-v2为15.4%，AI Scientist-v2为14.4%–20.6%。消融与行为分析表明，两种机制结合可提升研究覆盖度与连续性。在两个无参照论文的开放式任务中，智能体的部分发现与知识截止日期后研究者报告的结果高度吻合。

Conclusion: 合适的环境能够使智能体在开放式科学发现中自主取得有意义的进展，Supervisor与Meta Reflection机制对此有正向作用。

Abstract: Recent AI systems have made rapid progress in scientific discovery when given well-defined metrics, but whether they can autonomously undertake open-ended scientific discovery remains unclear. We investigate AI's ability to tackle open-ended tasks in Station, an open-world environment in which multiple agents simulate a scientific ecosystem. To tackle challenges specific to open-ended tasks, we propose augmenting Station with two mechanisms: a Supervisor mechanism and periodic Meta Reflection, which encourage persistent exploration even when intermediate metrics are lacking. We construct open-ended tasks from three recent oral papers presented at ICLR. We give agents the main research question studied in each paper while withholding the paper's results and disabling web access. We then measure how many of the original findings-partitioned into individual criteria-agents rediscover. We find that Station rediscovers 62.7% of the criteria on average, compared with 15.4% for Codex Multiagent-v2 and 14.4-20.6% for AI Scientist-v2. Ablation and behavioral analyses indicate that adding the two mechanisms together improves research coverage and continuity. We further evaluate Station on two open-ended tasks without oracle papers and find that some of the discoveries made by the agents closely match discoveries reported by researchers after the knowledge cutoff date. Together, these results indicate that a suitable environment can enable agents to autonomously make meaningful progress in open-ended scientific discovery.

</details>


### [35] [Humanity's Sixth Sense: Benchmarking Intuitive Visual Reasoning in Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08966)
*Xingang Guo,Jing Gu,Brian Jang,Renxiong Wang,Utkarsh Tyagi,Daniel Quigley,Steven Li,David Yan,Daniel Yue Zhang,Darvin Yi,Forrest Huang,HiJae Kim,Tianyi Zhang,Jared Lichtarge,Jihua Huang,Le Xue,Manan Tomar,Qiuyi Richard Zhang,Ruofei Yu,Seth Neel,Yaning Hu,Marcella Valentine,Xinzhe Jiang,Daniel Evans,Chenguang Wang,Dustin Tran,Tong Zhao,Yinfei Yang,Yunzhong He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Humanity's Sixth Sense (HSS) 基准，用于评测多模态大模型在"直觉视觉推理"上的能力，即仅凭一瞥就推断画面中隐含的时间、空间、社交与抽象结构。结果显示人类准确率达 93.1%，而最强模型 GPT-6-astra 仅 53.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能在瞬间从场景中推断大量隐含信息（过去成因、未来轨迹、社会等级、抽象规则等），这种零样本的"第六感"式直觉推理支撑着日常导航与社交。然而现有视觉基准要么聚焦学术与数学领域的专家级深度分析，要么停留在低层感知，几乎没有评测人类日常所做的直觉推理。而 MLLM 若要与人共处部署，这一能力至关重要。

Method: 作者构建了 HSS 基准，涵盖多种图像与视频输入，按结构化分类体系组织条目，并为每一项配以人工撰写的提示（prompt），用以探测人类一瞥即可推断的隐含时间、空间、社交与抽象结构。此外还探索了引入动态视觉操作的智能体（agentic）设置。

Result: 前沿 MLLM 表现远逊于人类：人类参与者准确率达 93.1%，而最强模型 GPT-6-astra 即便在最大推理努力下也仅 53.6%。智能体设置可缩小差距但未能弥合。

Conclusion: HSS 将直觉视觉推理确立为可度量的维度，揭示出当前模型虽在许多需高级感知与知识的复杂任务上表现出色，却在这些对人类而言极为直观的视觉任务上存在显著短板，指向现有基准上的规模扩展尚未触及的能力。

Abstract: Humans perceive far more in a scene than what is explicitly depicted: a single glance captures past causes and future trajectories; a quick peek determines if a vehicle can fit between two parked cars; a few seconds of video reveals who holds authority in a room; and a fleeting clip highlights subtle abstract patterns like unwritten rules or hidden labels. This capacity reflects a form of humanity's sixth sense: an intuitive reasoning mechanism that recovers implicit information beyond raw sensory perception. Crucially, this rapid, zero-shot visual intuition underpins everyday navigation and social interaction, making it a vital capability for Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) deployed alongside people. Existing visual benchmarks, however, target either deliberate expert-level analysis in academic and mathematical domains or low-level perception, leaving the intuitive reasoning that people perform largely untested. To bridge this gap, we introduce Humanity's Sixth Sense (HSS), a benchmark for intuitive visual reasoning. HSS spans diverse image and video inputs, organizes items under a structured taxonomy, and pairs each with human-written prompts probing the implicit temporal, spatial, social, and abstract structure that people infer at a glance. Frontier MLLMs fall short of human performance: participants reach 93.1% accuracy, while the strongest model, GPT-6-astra, reaches only 53.6% even at maximum reasoning effort. Despite excelling in many complex tasks that require advanced perception and knowledge, current models still struggle significantly on these visual tasks that are intuitive for humans. We further explore agentic setup that apply dynamic visual manipulation to HSS, which narrows but does not close the gap. HSS establishes intuitive visual reasoning as a measurable axis and directs attention to a capability that scaling on current benchmarks has so far left behind.

</details>


### [36] [Socio-Foundation: A Model for Generalizable Individual Behavior Simulation via Hierarchical Capability Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08967)
*Liang Wang,Wenxuan Xie,Xinyi Mou,Yixin Luo,Zhongyu Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FONTS分类体系与Socio-Foundation三阶段训练流程，构建千万级语料库和IndiEval评测基准，显著提升LLM个体行为模拟能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟个体行为要求LLM在保持人格特质的同时适应动态社会情境。然而通用LLM往往抹平不同人格差异，任务专用微调则存在碎片化与泛化不足的问题，亟需兼顾专门化与通用性的方案。

Method: 提出FONTS分类体系（人格保真、结果实现、行为自然性、轨迹连贯性、社会接地）；据此构建约1000万实例、覆盖14个数据集的标准化语料库；Socio-Foundation采用三阶段训练：DAPO学习任务专家、离策略蒸馏整合为能力专家、多教师同策略蒸馏（MOPD）统一；并建立IndiEval评测，涵盖FONTS维度下的29项指标。

Result: Socio-Foundation相比Qwen3-8B基线提升11.0分，接近GLM-5.2等前沿模型；消融实验与分布外评测进一步验证了有效性与泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过分类体系引导的数据与训练流程，可在解耦专门化与整合的前提下统一个体模拟能力，为LLM个体行为模拟提供可复用的语料、方法与评测框架。

Abstract: Simulating individual behavior requires large language models (LLMs) to preserve persona traits while adapting to dynamic social contexts. However, general-purpose LLMs often flatten distinct personas, while task-specific tuning suffers from fragmentation and generalization. To overcome these challenges, we organize individual simulation into the \textbf{FONTS Taxonomy}, comprising five complementary capability dimensions: \emph{persona fidelity} (\textbf{F}), \emph{outcome realization} (\textbf{O}), \emph{behavioral naturalness} (\textbf{N}), \emph{trajectory coherence} (\textbf{T}), and \emph{social grounding} (\textbf{S}). Grounded in this taxonomy, we curate a standardized training corpus library of approximately 10 million instances across 14 representative datasets and present \textbf{Socio-Foundation}. Socio-Foundation decouples specialization from integration via a three-stage pipeline: learning task experts via DAPO, consolidating them into capability experts via off-policy distillation, and unifying them via multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD). We also establish \textbf{IndiEval}, consolidating 29 metrics across the FONTS dimensions. Experiments show that Socio-Foundation outperforms its \textit{Qwen3-8B} base by 11.0 points and approaches frontier models such as \textit{GLM-5.2}, with ablations and out-of-distribution evaluations further demonstrating the effectiveness and generalization of our model.

</details>


### [37] [Verify Less, Evolve More: Training Idea-Level Critics for Verification-Efficient ML Evolving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08993)
*Jiamu Bai,Lizhu Zhang,Xin Yu,Yanhong Wu,Zellux Wang,Serena Li,Weiwei Li,Zhuokai Zhao,Lingzhou Xue,Kiwan Maeng,Xiangjun Fan,Bo Peng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出"想法级批判模型"（idea-level critic models），用于预测某个机器学习修改方案能否改进当前解，从而在有限验证预算下帮助自进化智能体筛选高潜力想法、提升演化效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在AI4ML场景中，自进化智能体虽可进行经验验证，但模型训练与评估成本高昂，限制了演化速度与规模。验证效率问题尚未被充分探索，而将前沿模型直接用作想法筛选器收益有限。因此需要专门的批判模型来预判想法质量，把宝贵的验证资源集中在最有潜力的候选上。

Method: 训练专门的"想法级批判模型"，预测所提ML修改是否能超越当前方案。训练分两步：先在由Gemini-3.1-Pro合成的高质量批判数据上进行监督微调，再用GRPO进一步优化预测准确率。

Result: 批判模型在静态想法评估上超越Gemini-3.1-Pro，且优势可迁移至智能体推理、持续学习与策略训练。推理阶段在相同验证预算下通过筛选更优想法提升最终解质量，持续学习带来额外收益；策略训练中作为学习到的奖励模型，将经验验证留给不确定情形，从而在相同验证资源下实现更多策略更新。

Conclusion: 想法级批判模型能帮助ML智能体在有限验证预算下发现更优解并学习更强的提议策略，凸显了验证效率在自进化智能体中的关键作用。

Abstract: As large language models become more powerful, self-evolving agents are able to tackle challenging tasks including AI for machine learning (AI4ML). In AI4ML, while empirical verification is available, it often requires computationally costly model training and evaluation, limiting the speed and scale of agent evolution. Yet verification efficiency remains under-explored, and frontier models provide only limited gains when used directly as idea selectors. We address this gap with specialized idea-level critic models that predict whether a proposed ML modification will improve upon the current solution, allowing agents to screen ideas and concentrate verification resources on the most promising candidates. We train the critic models through supervised fine-tuning on high-quality critiques synthesized by Gemini-3.1-Pro, followed by GRPO to further improve their predictive accuracy. Empirically, our critic models outperform Gemini-3.1-Pro in static idea evaluation, and these gains extend to agent inference, continual learning, and policy training.
