Abstract: Fine-tuned geospatial foundation models (GeoFMs) pretrained on large satellite archives have been shown to improve crop classification accuracy and geographic transferability. However, their operational performance beyond the training distribution remains poorly characterized. We evaluated the out-of-distribution performance of a widely adopted GeoFM [Prithvi-EO-2.0] across 37 events in 12 countries on three continents and validated against regional reference products. Results indicated that the mean overall accuracy (OA) declined from 0.65 in the United States to 0.40 in Europe. Beyond accuracy metrics, we assessed five key aspects of model performance: whether model confidence indicates signal failure, sensitivity to observation windows, the effect of coarsening class schemes, and robustness to both band loss and cloud- and shadow-contamination. Accuracy collapsed when the observation window misaligned with local crop phenology, while deterministic confidence remained high. Expected calibration error increased for seven of eight paired events, and 12-51% of each affected scene was confidently mislabeled at near-zero precision. Monte Carlo dropout entropy registered the shift in all eight, indicating that much of the apparent cross-continent decline reflected phenological misalignment rather than spatial transfer. Two adjustments recovered accuracy without retraining. Consolidating 13 classes into 10, based on the model's dominant confusions, raised the mean OA by 8.4 percentage points. Compressing the window toward near-real-time use preserved accuracy across a 45- to 90-day plateau, peaking near 75 days, though arms tighter than 30 days fell about 0.11 below that plateau. Fine-tuned crop GeoFMs therefore transfer usefully only where observation windows match local growing seasons. We translate these findings into operational guidance for the reliable deployment of the released model.

</details>


### [138] [KVFetch: Temporal Prefetching for the Missing Half of KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08811)
*Linfeng Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出，即使内容评分再优化，也修不好“顺序遗忘”——即模型无法逐字复制上下文中长序列的后半段。KVFetch应运而生，为任意评分式KV压缩器增加一条“时间召回通道”，在几乎零成本下恢复逐字复制能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存压缩方法（评分淘汰、摘要补偿、卸载与召回）层出不穷，但它们都只实现了“按内容做关联查找”这一种访问模式，而忽略了“按位置做顺序遍历”这一根本需求。这在RAG、代码补全、结构化数据抽取等需要逐字复现标识符、字段值或代码token的场景中会引发不可逆的“顺序遗忘”——内容评分保留序列头部却丢弃后续，无论评分多好、预算多大都无法修复。

Method: 作者提出KVFetch，一个免训练、即插即用的框架：为任意基于评分的压缩器引入一条“时间召回通道”。具体做法是把被淘汰的候选降级到量化冷层，通过单调读指针检测活跃复制行为，并将位置上的后继预取进固定大小的热层槽位，且不增加注意力计算开销。

Result: 在iso-budget控制下的RULER-16K上，KVFetch将逐字复制从0.8提升到78.4，13任务平均提升+8.4，增益集中在需要顺序访问的任务上。在LongBench上（无任务需要顺序访问），该通道保持休眠，不带来任何开销。

Conclusion: KV缓存压缩的瓶颈不在于评分质量，而在于缺少“按位置顺序访问”这一维度。KVFetch以零训练成本补上这一通道，在需要逐字复现的任务上大幅回血，在不需要时完全静默，可作为通用插件与现有评分式压缩器兼容。

Abstract: As context windows scale to tens or hundreds of thousands of tokens, KV cache compression has become essential for efficient LLM inference. Existing methods fall into three families: score-based eviction, summary compensation, and offload-and-recall. Yet all three decide what to keep or recall by content relevance to the current query. We show this shared design is structurally incomplete. A cache supports two access modes: associative lookup by content and sequential traversal by position; current compressors implement only the first. The gap matters in practice: retrieval-augmented generation, code completion, and structured-data extraction all require the model to reproduce identifiers, field values, or code tokens verbatim from the context. Under compression, content-based eviction retains the head of such a sequence but discards its continuation, causing verbatim copying to break irreversibly midway, a failure we call sequential forgetting. This failure resists better scoring, larger budgets, summary compensation, and dynamic re-scoring; it is the dominant source of remaining quality loss under compression. We propose KVFetch, a training-free, drop-in framework that opens a temporal recall channel for any score-based compressor. It demotes evicted candidates to a quantized cold tier, detects active copying through a monotone read pointer, and prefetches positional successors into fixed-size hot-tier slots without increasing attention cost. On RULER-16K under an iso-budget control, KVFetch recovers verbatim copying from 0.8 to 78.4 and raises the 13-task average by +8.4, with gains concentrating on tasks that require sequential access. On LongBench, where no task requires sequential access, the channel remains dormant and imposes no cost.

</details>


### [139] [Bounded Autonomy and Verifiable Safety for Agentic AI Enabled Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08815)
*Srini Ramaswamy,Deveeshree Nayak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BRaVeS框架（DNRS），通过不变量锚点、深度感知访问机制与状态层级来约束智能体推理中的认知漂移，并用Lyapunov有界共识框架（LBCF）形式化有界恢复，在仿真中验证有限步收敛且无安全违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险环境下仅靠概率推理无法安全部署智能体AI自动化；核心风险是"认知漂移"——推理越深，系统行为可能越偏离领域专家（SME）设定的安全约束。

Method: 1) BRaVeS：将SME约束编码为不变量锚点；提出MoDA-Style（混合深度注意力）深度感知访问作为保持锚点可见的候选机制；用SMARtAutonomy状态层级在认知风险升高时降低自主性。2) 引入LBCF：把连续认知风险信号映射为有限K-bag抽象，采用屏蔽状态转移，强制Lyapunov式能量下降或转入人工介入的终止状态。

Result: 基于HAI 22.04工业控制系统时间序列数据（含合成噪声与传感器退化情形）进行离散事件蒙特卡洛仿真；在所测试的参数分组策略与阈值下，LBCF过程实现有限步收敛且未出现安全护栏违规。形式化收敛结论仅适用于固定阈值与可行屏蔽假设下的有限LBCF抽象，不证明完整连续神经激活空间的安全性。

Conclusion: 结果为"在既定抽象下可强制执行有界治理行为"提供了基于仿真的证据；未来需开展已部署Transformer实现、实时人在回路验证及更广泛的对抗场景研究。

Abstract: Agentic AI-enabled automation cannot be safely deployed in high-stakes environments on probabilistic reasoning alone. A recurring risk is epistemic drift: as reasoning deepens, system behavior may move away from subject-matter-expert constraints for safe operation. This paper presents BRaVeS, a bounded reasoning and safety-governance framework termed the Defensible Next-Gen Reasoning System (DNRS). BRaVeS encodes SME-defined constraints as invariant anchors, proposes MoDA-Style (Mixture of Depths Attention) depth-aware access as a candidate mechanism for keeping these anchors visible during inference, and uses a state hierarchy (SMARtAutonomy) to reduce autonomy as epistemic risk increases. To formalize bounded recovery, we introduce the Lyapunov-Bounded Consensus Framework (LBCF), which maps continuous epistemic-risk signals into a finite K-bag abstraction and applies shielded state transitions that enforce Lyapunov-style energy descent or route the system to a human-mediated terminal state. The formal convergence result applies to the finite LBCF abstraction under fixed thresholds and feasible-shield assumptions; it does not prove safety of the full continuous neural activation space. We evaluate the framework through a discrete event Monte Carlo simulation using HAI 22.04 industrial-control-system time-series data with synthetic noise and sensor-degradation regimes. Across the tested parameter-grouping strategies and thresholds, the LBCF process achieved finite-step convergence and no safety-guard violations. These results provide simulation-based evidence that bounded governance behavior can be enforced under the stated abstraction, while motivating future work on deployed transformer implementations, live human-in-the-loop validation, and broader adversarial settings.

</details>


### [140] [The Cost of Long Memory: State, Context, and Stability Complexity in Sequence Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08816)
*Yuheng Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究序列模型预测代数衰减记忆所需的资源量，针对指数模式与有限状态模式给出了匹配的上、下逼近界，并进一步分析分数长记忆下预测误差的几何变化与临界性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程时间依赖对序列模型构成资源问题：给定预测记忆规律，准确预测需要多少状态、上下文或动力学临界性尚不明确，本文旨在直接在预测风险中研究该问题并确定最优资源指数。

Method: 对代数衰减预测记忆，证明指数模式与有限状态模式的匹配逼近上界与下界；分析分数长记忆下经分数积分后的预测误差、有限上下文长度L的1/L首阶项、固定分数强度d的状态复杂度；对一致收缩的非线性上下文递推推导指数首次混沌包络及与收缩裕度相关的必要条件；并进行有限样本KL计算与定理匹配实验。

Result: 最优r模式预测误差以e^{-Θ(√r)}衰减，达到误差τ需r=Θ(log²(1/τ))个状态或模式；分数长记忆下有限上下文预测误差首阶为1/L，固定d保持平方对数状态复杂度律，短记忆边界附近识别出d²与d⁴尺度；收缩递推给出指数首次混沌包络与显式必要条件；预测误差消失迫使递推定量趋向临界性，该条件必要但不充分；收缩状态空间、门控递归与注意力模型的实验复现了状态与稳定性预测。

Conclusion: 本文为某一典型预测目标的预测风险确定了最优资源指数，并揭示分数长记忆改变预测几何本身，将预测几何与统计信息联系起来，同时给出与定理匹配的实验验证。

Abstract: Long-range temporal dependence poses a resource question for sequence models: for a specified predictive-memory law, how much state, context, or dynamical criticality is required in order to forecast accurately? We study this question directly in forecasting risk. For algebraically decaying predictive memory, we prove matching upper and lower approximation bounds for exponential and finite-state modes. The best $r$-mode forecast error decays as $e^{-Θ(\sqrt r)}$, so reaching forecast error $τ$ needs $r=Θ(\log^2(1/τ))$ states or modes. Earlier curse-of-memory results establish broad limitations of stable recurrent models under different approximation notions; here both sides match for one canonical predictive target in forecast risk, which fixes the optimal resource exponent for that target. We then show that genuine fractional long memory changes the geometry itself. In particular, forecast error is measured after fractional integration, prediction from a finite context of length $L$ has an exact $1/L$ leading order, and a fixed fractional strength $d$ keeps the square-log state-complexity law. Near the short-memory boundary, we identify the relevant $d^2$ and $d^4$ scales and give a uniform constructive law in the intermediate regime. For nonlinear contextual recurrences with uniformly contractive state dynamics, we derive an exponential first-chaos envelope and an explicit necessary condition that relates forecast accuracy to the contraction margin. Vanishing forecasting error on an algebraic target forces the recurrence quantitatively toward criticality, a condition that is necessary and not by itself sufficient. Finite-sample Kullback--Leibler calculations further connect the predictive geometry to statistical information. Theorem-matched experiments with contractive state-space, gated recurrent, and attention models reproduce the state and stability predictions.

</details>


### [141] [DenoFlow: Flow Matching for SSVEP Denoising under Real Physiological Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08817)
*Zhentao He,Ziwei Wang,Dongrui Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DenoFlow，将SSVEP去噪建模为"传输"问题，用矫正流(rectified flow)回归速度场并对观测前向积分，配合分类器监督，避免了无配对真值下的自复制与波形距离优化缺陷，在信号保真与解码精度上均优于七个基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SSVEP-BCI极易受噪声与伪迹影响，解码精度显著下降。现有去噪方法存在三大问题：缺乏配对真值进行拟合；容易收敛到仅复制输入；仅以波形距离为优化目标，无法保证输出仍然可解码。因此需要一种既能保持波形保真、又确保下游解码性能的去噪方案。

Method: 将SSVEP去噪视为传输过程：不学习从污染试次到干净试次的直接映射，而是用场网络（field network）回归两者之间直线路径的速度，遵循rectified-flow范式，去噪时从观测点前向积分该速度场。场网络为编码器-解码器结构，每一层都能看到污染试次，瓶颈处输入路径位置；同时训练一个分类器监督积分输出。由于观测本身既是条件输入又是积分起点，模型不从噪声生成试次，训练退化为回归问题，无需对抗min-max博弈。为在无真值数据集上获得配对数据，按受控信噪比注入记录的EMG与EOG生理伪迹。

Result: 在两个公开SSVEP数据集、五种主流SSVEP解码器上，DenoFlow在信号保真度和下游解码准确率两个指标上均优于七个基线去噪模型。代码已开源。

Conclusion: DenoFlow通过矫正流式传输建模与分类器联合监督，有效缓解了SSVEP去噪中无配对监督、自复制和忽视可解码性的问题，在保真与解码两方面取得一致性提升，为SSVEP-BCI的鲁棒去噪提供了新思路。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG)-based brain-computer interfaces (BCIs), particularly steady-state visual evoked potential (SSVEP) systems, are highly vulnerable to noise and artifacts, which severely degrade decoding accuracy. Although recent denoising approaches have shown promise, they are fitted without paired ground truth, can settle on reproducing their input, and are optimized on waveform distance alone, which says nothing about whether the output stays decodable. To address these issues, we propose DenoFlow, which casts SSVEP denoising as transport: instead of learning a direct map from a contaminated trial to a clean one, a field network regresses the velocity of the straight path between them, following the rectified-flow formulation, and denoising integrates that field forward from the observation. The field network is an encoder-decoder that sees the contaminated trial at every layer and the path position at its bottleneck, and a classifier trained alongside it supervises the integrated output. Because the observation itself is both the conditioning input and the starting point of the integration, the model never generates a trial from noise, and training reduces to regression, removing the adversarial min-max game. To obtain paired data on datasets with no ground truth, we injected physiological artifacts of the recorded electromyography (EMG) and electrooculography (EOG) signals under a controlled signal-to-noise target. Experiments on two public SSVEP datasets with five popular SSVEP decoders showed that DenoFlow outperformed seven baseline denoising models on both signal fidelity and downstream decoding accuracy. Code is available at https://github.com/wzwvv/DenoFlow.

</details>


### [142] [Just for FUNS: LLM-Guided Spatio-Temporal Graph Node Generation for Forecasting Unobserved Node States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08818)
*Shuhao Li,Weidong Yang,Changan Liu,Wei Zhuo,Yingbo Zhou,Fan Zhang,Siqiang Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GenST将"预测未观测节点状态(FUNS)"重新定义为时空图上的条件生成任务，引入预训练LLM提取节点语义特征，结合时空VAE与生成式Transformer，在6个交通和2个非交通数据集上零样本预测显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传感器网络因部署成本与维护资源限制，缺乏完整空间覆盖，导致未观测节点的时空预测成为关键难题；传统模型依赖历史观测，对无历史记录的节点往往失效，因此需要借助节点语义信息补偿缺失的时空信号。

Method: 将问题重定义为时空图上的条件生成任务，提出GenST框架：利用预训练并微调的LLM从节点描述(如功能区、路网结构)中提取语义特征，作为语义桥梁；设计两阶段生成架构——时空VAE先将时空动态压缩到隐空间，再由生成式Transformer(GenT)在语义、地理坐标、邻域上下文等多模态条件引导下，从噪声中重建未观测节点的未来状态。

Result: 在6个交通数据集和2个非交通数据集上的实验表明，GenST在零样本预测任务中显著优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 语义引导的生成方法在缓解时空数据稀疏性方面具有实际潜力。

Abstract: Spatio-temporal forecasting is a cornerstone of logistics, urban planning, and intelligent transportation systems. However, constrained by deployment costs and maintenance resources, sensor networks often lack comprehensive spatial coverage, rendering Forecast Unobserved Node States (FUNS) a critical yet formidable challenge. Conventional models rely on historical observations and typically falter when encountering nodes without prior records. To address this, we redefine the problem as a conditional generation task on spatio-temporal graphs and propose GenST, a framework that introduces Large Language Models (LLMs) as a semantic bridge, leveraging a pre-trained LLM fine-tuned to extract rich semantic features from node descriptions, such as functional zones and road network structures, to compensate for missing spatio-temporal signals. Specifically, we design a two-stage generative architecture: a Spatio-Temporal VAE first compresses spatio-temporal dynamics into a latent space, followed by a Generative Transformer (GenT) that reconstructs the future states of unobserved nodes from noise, guided by multi-modal conditions including semantics, geographic coordinates, and neighborhood contexts. Experiments on six traffic and two non-traffic datasets show GenST significantly outperforms existing baselines in zero-shot prediction tasks, demonstrating the practical potential of semantic-guided generation for mitigating spatio-temporal data sparsity.

</details>


### [143] [HydroSphere: A Framework for Governed, Self-Healing Wastewater Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08819)
*Prabu,Fancy C,Suresh A,Srini Ramaswamy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HydroSphere，一个受治理约束的数据驱动框架，用于实时水质监测、预测、处理优化与故障恢复，基于1940-2023年间282万条水质测量数据评估，集成TCN-LSTM预测、PPO自适应投药优化和SHADE异常检测三大模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速工业化与城市增长加剧了水质与污水处理系统压力，而传统处理厂依赖静态监测与控制策略，难以适应不断变化的污染物条件，因此需要更具适应性的智能水务管理方案。

Method: HydroSphere框架包含三个主要组件：一是混合TCN-LSTM模型，对七项水质参数进行多步预测；二是自适应投药优化模块，采用PPO强化学习调整化学药剂投加量；三是SHADE异常检测模块，使用深度自编码器识别传感器与过程异常。框架结合分级治理、确定性安全边界与人工监督。

Result: TCN-LSTM预测达到RMSE 0.1417、MAE 0.1047、R2 0.3596；PPO投药优化平均步奖励1.059，优于固定投药基线的1.017，但无约束奖励优化会导致过量投药，说明需要明确的操作保障；SHADE在受控故障注入下F1为0.651。

Conclusion: HydroSphere将预测、优化与异常检测能力与治理机制和安全约束相结合，支持更安全、更自适应的水务基础设施，为智能污水管理提供可扩展基础，并支持联合国可持续发展目标6和13。

Abstract: Rapid industrialization and urban growth are increasing pressure on water quality and wastewater treatment systems, while conventional treatment plants often rely on static monitoring and control strategies that cannot easily adapt to changing pollutant conditions. This paper presents HydroSphere, a governed, data-driven framework for real-time water quality monitoring, forecasting, treatment optimization, and fault recovery. HydroSphere is evaluated using 2.82 million water-quality measurements collected between 1940 and 2023. The framework integrates three main components. First, a hybrid TCN-LSTM model performs multi-step forecasting across seven water-quality parameters, achieving an RMSE of 0.1417, MAE of 0.1047, and R2 of 0.3596. Second, the Adaptive Dosage Optimization Module uses PPO reinforcement learning to adjust chemical dosing, achieving a mean step reward of 1.059 compared with 1.017 for a fixed-dose baseline. The results also show that unconstrained reward optimization can lead to excessive dosing, demonstrating the need for explicit operational safeguards. Third, the SHADE anomaly detection module uses a deep autoencoder to identify sensor and process anomalies, achieving an F1 score of 0.651 under controlled fault injection. HydroSphere combines these capabilities with tiered governance, deterministic safety bounds, and human oversight to support safer and more adaptive water infrastructure. The framework provides a scalable foundation for intelligent wastewater management and supports the objectives of UN Sustainable Development Goals 6 and 13.

</details>


### [144] [Task-Oriented Key-Layer KV Communication for Efficient Latent Multi-Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08820)
*Dongsen Zhang,Peipei Li,Zekun Li,Wenjun Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 KITE，一种无需训练的面向任务的单层 KV 潜空间通信框架，通过接收端轨迹失真准则定位关键层，仅传输该层的工作记忆并以其作为自回归潜推理入口，大幅降低通信与计算开销同时提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体系统基于大语言模型协作提升复杂问题求解能力，潜空间通信通过直接传输模型内部状态规避自然语言推理的高成本。但现有基于 KV 的潜通信方法以发送端状态保真为核心目标，导致通信与计算开销巨大，并可能引入冗余信息。

Method: 作者从任务导向视角重新审视潜空间通信，将目标由发送端状态保真转变为接收端任务充分性。据此提出 KITE 免训练框架：利用接收端轨迹失真准则识别对任务有效的关键层，仅传输与该关键层相关的潜工作记忆，并将同一层作为自回归潜推理的入口。

Result: 在两个模型家族、三种模型规模、七个基准上的实验表明，相比全层 KV 通信，KITE 将通信量减少 28–36 倍，端到端推理最高加速 3 倍，准确率最高提升 23.3 个百分点。

Conclusion: KITE 表明面向任务的潜空间通信可在保证甚至提升任务性能的同时显著降低通信与计算成本，为多智能体潜通信提供了更高效的新范式。

Abstract: Large language model-based multi-agent systems improve complex problem solving through collaboration, while latent communication directly transmits model internal states to avoid the high inference costs of natural language. However, existing KV-based latent communication methods prioritize sender-side state fidelity, leading to substantial communication and computation overhead and potentially introducing redundant information. To address these limitations, we revisit latent communication from a task-oriented perspective, shifting its objective from sender-side state fidelity to receiver-side task sufficiency. Under this formulation, we propose KITE, a training-free framework for task-oriented key-layer KV communication. KITE identifies a task-effective key layer using a receiver trajectory distortion criterion, transmits only the latent working memory associated with the key layer, and further uses the same layer as the entry point for autoregressive latent reasoning. Experiments on seven benchmarks across two model families and three model scales show that, compared with full-layer KV communication, KITE reduces communication volume by 28-36$\times$, achieves up to 3$\times$ end-to-end inference speedup, and improves accuracy by up to 23.3 percentage points.

</details>


### [145] [A Vehicle-Integrated Approach to Digital Twin Deployment for Bridges Through Drive-By Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08822)
*Zihao Liu,Daigo Kawabe,Jiaji Wang,Chul-Woo Kim,Mehrisadat Makki Alamdari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种车辆集成数字孪生框架，融合物理建模与机器学习，通过傅里叶神经算子代理模型、贝叶斯优化传感器设计及无监督损伤评估流程，实现对桥梁与道路状态的连续监测，并在澳大利亚和日本开展多场地验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球桥梁老化问题日益严峻，传统结构健康监测成本高、难以规模化，人工目视检测又具主观性。车辆搭载式（drive-by）检测虽可扩展，但仍面临三大挑战：从路面粗糙度中分离桥梁响应、在正常交通下检测损伤、以及跨多种桥型泛化。

Method: 框架含三大支柱：(1) 用傅里叶神经算子构建车桥相互作用与车路相互作用的代理模型，学习从工况到车辆响应的函数到函数映射，基于仿真与实测数据训练，实现毫秒级推理；(2) 通过贝叶斯优化设计定制电动检测车、传感器布局与信号处理链，最大化桥梁信息量并抑制路面与车辆噪声；(3) 开发基于对抗自编码器、矩阵剖面和Transformer的无监督损伤评估流程处理车辆数据。

Result: 代理模型实现毫秒级推理，替代计算密集的全阶分析；优化后的车辆与传感系统有效抑制噪声、提升桥梁信息产出；无监督损伤评估流程得到开发与验证；整体工作流在澳大利亚和日本的多场地现场试验中通过验证，覆盖多种桥型、交通与环境条件。

Conclusion: 该车辆集成数字孪生框架统一了物理建模与机器学习，为桥梁和道路状态的连续、可扩展监测提供了可行方案，并通过跨国多场地试验证明了其通用性与实用性。

Abstract: Ageing bridge infrastructure is a growing global concern, yet conventional Structural Health Monitoring (SHM) systems are costly and difficult to scale, and routine visual inspections remain subjective. Drive-by, or indirect, bridge inspection, in which a sensorised vehicle recovers structural information from vehicle-bridge interaction (VBI) and vehicle-road interaction (VRI) responses, offers a scalable alternative. However, key challenges remain unresolved, including separating bridge responses from road roughness, detecting damage under normal traffic, and generalising across diverse bridge types. This paper presents a vehicle-integrated digital twin framework that unifies physics-based modelling and machine learning for continuous monitoring of bridge and road conditions. The framework comprises three pillars. First, surrogate models of VBI and VRI are constructed using a Fourier Neural Operator that learns function-to-function mappings from operating conditions to vehicle responses. Trained on both simulated and field data, these surrogates deliver millisecond-scale inference, replacing computationally intensive full-order analyses. Second, the design of a custom electric inspection vehicle, its sensor layout, and signal processing chain are optimised through Bayesian optimisation to maximise bridge information yield while suppressing road and vehicle noise. Unsupervised damage-assessment pipelines based on adversarial autoencoders, matrix profiles, and transformer architectures have been developed and validated to process the resulting vehicle data. Third, the complete workflow is validated through coordinated multi-site field trials in Australia and Japan, covering a range of bridge types, traffic conditions, and environmental settings.

</details>


### [146] [HCPN-GCN: Scaling Hierarchical Prototype Networks with Cone Geometry for Continual Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08823)
*Sammuel R. Silva,Vander L. S. Freitas,Gladston Moreira,Eduardo J. S. Luz,Rodrigo Silva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HCPN-GCN 用 GCN 替换 HPN 的线性特征提取器，并引入锥形原型与多样性正则，实现更紧凑、更强抗遗忘的持续图学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续图学习（CGL）面临灾难性遗忘问题。层次原型网络（HPN）通过基于原型的记忆机制避免存储历史数据，但其依赖线性特征提取器，难以利用图拓扑结构；同时点式原型在结构多样的图上容易导致原型数量低效增长。

Method: 提出 HCPN-GCN，即 HPN 的图感知扩展：用图卷积网络（GCN）替换原有线性特征提取器，并引入锥形原型（cone-based prototypes）及多样性正则化目标。

Result: 在六个持续图学习基准上，HCPN-GCN 的平均分类准确率持续优于原始 HPN 与代表性持续学习基线，同时保持接近零的遗忘率；其原子原型数量约为原始 HPN 的 1/30，却能学到更丰富的类级原型层次结构。

Conclusion: 该设计在生成更丰富图感知表示的同时紧凑建模嵌入空间，减少原型增殖而不损失判别能力，为持续图学习提供了更紧凑有效的记忆表示。

Abstract: Continual Graph Learning (CGL) aims to incrementally learn from graph-structured data while preserving knowledge acquired from previous tasks. A major challenge in this setting is catastrophic forgetting, where learning new tasks degrades performance on previously learned ones. Hierarchical Prototype Networks (HPNs) address this problem through a prototype-based memory mechanism that avoids storing historical data, but their reliance on linear feature extractors limits their ability to exploit graph topology, while point-based prototypes often lead to inefficient prototype growth on structurally diverse graphs. In this work, we propose HCPN-GCN, a graph-aware extension of HPN that replaces the original linear feature extractors with Graph Convolutional Networks (GCNs) and introduces cone-based prototypes with a diversity regularization objective. The proposed design produces richer graph-aware representations while compactly modeling the embedding space, reducing prototype proliferation without sacrificing discriminability. Experimental results on six continual graph learning benchmarks demonstrate that HCPN-GCN consistently improves average classification accuracy over the original HPN and representative continual learning baselines while maintaining near-zero forgetting. Furthermore, our analysis shows that the proposed model learns substantially richer class-level prototype hierarchies using approximately $30\times$ fewer atomic prototypes than the original HPN, providing a more compact and effective memory representation for continual graph learning.

</details>


### [147] [FinVector-Market-4B: A Controlled Study of LoRA Adaptation for Structured Financial Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08882)
*Alina Khaybullina*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将Qwen3.5-4B经rank-16 LoRA在2.2万条金融语料上微调为FinVector-Market-4B，在600例基准上以隐式/显式JSON schema对比评估，发现显式提示下各任务指标大幅提升，但基准的泛化性受文件重叠与计算器目标不一致所限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究紧凑型金融领域适配能否在匹配提示条件下带来超越输出格式学习（即仅靠schema约束提升JSON合法性）的真实任务能力增益，并检验基准评测本身的可靠性。

Method: 以Qwen/Qwen3.5-4B为基座，采用rank-16 LoRA在22,000条结构化金融任务语料上适配；在相同600例基准上，对基座与适配模型分别施加隐式与显式JSON-schema提示契约进行对比；并开展事后策略评分审计以校正标签集变化带来的影响。

Result: 仅提供schema即使基座模型JSON合法率由0%升至91.3%。在匹配的显式提示下，基座到适配器分别提升：FinQA答案精确匹配14.7%→40.0%，计算器表达式正确率48.0%→82.7%，情景分支标签一致性20.1%→89.5%，蕴含方向一致性52.4%→87.2%。事后策略评分审计显示原报告的macro-F1下降源于标签集变化，采用相同三个目标类别时基座77.4%、适配器83.1%。文件重叠与计算器目标不一致对基准泛化性主张构成限定。

Conclusion: 在匹配提示契约下，紧凑型金融领域适配能在输出格式学习之外带来实质性的任务专项提升，但增益受所评估任务分布与提示契约的约束；同时基准设计上的数据重叠与目标不一致问题会影响其泛化性结论的可信度。

Abstract: FinVector-Market-4B adapts Qwen/Qwen3.5-4B with rank-16 LoRA on a 22,000-example corpus for structured financial tasks. We evaluate the base and adapted models on the same 600-example benchmark under implicit and explicit JSON-schema contracts. Supplying the schema alone raises base-model JSON validity from 0% to 91.3%. Under matched explicit prompting, the frozen scores improve from 14.7% to 40.0% for FinQA answer exact match, from 48.0% to 82.7% for calculator-expression correctness, from 20.1% to 89.5% for scenario branch-label agreement, and from 52.4% to 87.2% for implication-direction agreement. A post-hoc policy-scoring audit shows that the reported macro-F1 decline reflects a changing label set; using the same three target classes gives 77.4% for the base and 83.1% for the adapter. Filing overlap and calculator-target inconsistencies qualify the benchmark's generalization claims. The results show that compact financial domain adaptation can produce substantial task-specific gains beyond output-format learning under matched prompting, with gains bounded by the evaluated task distribution and prompt contract.

</details>


### [148] [GraphOPD: Graph-Augmented On-Policy Distillation for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08959)
*Bohan Lin,Liyi Chen,Zhuoning Guo,Muyang Li,Qimeng Wang,Yan Gao,Yao Hu,Yudong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GraphOPD 首次将图结构增强引入多轮智能体的同策略蒸馏，用环境状态变化构建步骤依赖图并以随机游走平稳分布打分，再与散度信号融合成轨迹相对掩码，在 ALFWorld、WebShop、SearchQA 上最多超越最强基线 5.8 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同策略蒸馏通过教师策略提供稠密的步骤级指导，缓解强化学习奖励稀疏、每条轨迹仅一次奖励的问题。但现有方法按教师-学生散度大小分配监督，这一规则基于单轮直觉，认为分歧大即代表值得纠正的错误。在多轮链式决策中该假设失效：早期漂移会进入后续所有上下文，使教师与已漂移轨迹保持一致而不再标记错误根源；同时可互换的步骤会产生大但与结果无关的散度。作者在智能体基准上验证，蒸馏最高散度步骤相比随机选择并无稳定收益，因此需要一种不受漂移干扰的结构化信用分配方式。

Method: GraphOPD 从环境自身记录的状态变化中读取哪些步骤促成了哪些后续步骤，该信号不受污染教师-学生差距的漂移影响；将这些依赖关系组织为依赖图，用图上的随机游走平稳分布为每个步骤打分，形成结构信用；再把结构信用与散度信号融合为轨迹相对掩码，使监督集中在每条 rollout 中最高适配度的步骤上。

Result: 在 ALFWorld、WebShop、SearchQA 三个基准上，覆盖三种模型规模和十一个基线，GraphOPD 全程表现有竞争力，相对最强基线最高提升 5.8 个百分点。执行回放审计进一步表明：该结构信用分数追踪真实因果影响的程度远高于随机水平；两种融合信号各自独立必要；同一信号可迁移到域外的工具集成推理任务。

Conclusion: 按散度大小选择蒸馏步骤在多轮智能体中并不可靠，因为它混淆了漂移导致的虚假分歧与真正值得纠正的错误。基于环境状态变化构建步骤依赖图并进行结构信用分配，能有效识别步骤的真实因果影响，与散度信号互补，为智能体能力的同策略蒸馏提供了更可靠的监督选择机制，并具备跨任务迁移性。

Abstract: On-policy distillation post-trains large language model agents by supplying dense, step-level guidance from a teacher policy when the reinforcement-learning reward is sparse and arrives only once per trajectory. Existing instantiations allocate this guidance by the size of the teacher-student divergence at each step, on the single-turn intuition that a large disagreement marks a mistake worth correcting. Once decisions chain over many turns, that rule misfires, since an early drift enters every later context both policies condition on, leaving the teacher consistent with the drifted trajectory instead of flagging its cause, while interchangeable steps register large but outcome-irrelevant divergences. We demonstrate this on an agentic benchmark, where distilling the highest-divergence steps brings no consistent benefit over random selection. To this end, we introduce GraphOPD, the first method to bring graph-based structural augmentation into on-policy distillation for agent capabilities. It reads which steps enabled which later ones from the environment's own record of state changes, immune to the drift that corrupts the teacher-student gap, organizes them into a dependency graph, scores each step by a random-walk stationary distribution over it, and fuses that structural credit with the divergence signal into a trajectory-relative mask concentrating supervision on each rollout's highest-aptitude steps. Across three model scales and eleven baselines on ALFWorld, WebShop, and SearchQA, GraphOPD shows competitive performance throughout, improving over the strongest baseline by up to +5.8 pp. An executed-replay audit further shows that this structural credit score tracks true causal impact far above chance, that both fused signals are independently necessary, and that the same signal transfers to out-of-domain tool-integrated reasoning.

</details>


### [149] [Directed Temporal Representations for Offline Visual Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08960)
*Chenyang Yuan,Haoyu Wang,Zhuo Sun,Xiaoyuan Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DTRC 在冻结的 LeWorldModel 特征上从离线视觉轨迹学习"有向时间准度量"表示，将时间可达成本作为进度信号用于目标条件策略学习，在十个视觉控制任务上取得优于规划与直接策略基线的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 控制需要与"时间可达性"对齐的潜在几何结构，而预测世界模型通常只提供面向预测相似性的表示，不足以支撑目标条件控制。

Method: 在冻结 LeWM 特征之上：构建有向时间准度量；用短程时间偏移标定距离尺度；用自举目标扩展长时程可达性；用动作条件一致性对齐局部转移动态；以距离变化定义目标相对时间进度，作为时间 critic 直接学习目标条件策略；并用模型辅助目标在行为支撑与动态一致性约束下做训练期精修。

Result: 在十个视觉控制任务上，DTRC 相对规划与直接策略基线表现强劲；在四个 LeWM 任务上的留出诊断显示短程时间标定一致、长程与方向结构依赖任务、转移级进度为正；时间监督在四个任务上均改善同一 flow-policy 参数化。

Conclusion: 时间监督可在冻结世界模型特征上塑造与可达性对齐的表示几何，使策略在测试时直接行动而无需迭代轨迹搜索。

Abstract: Predictive world models provide compact visual representations for control. Control requires a latent geometry aligned with temporal reachability rather than predictive similarity alone. We introduce Directed Temporal Representations for Control (DTRC), which learns such a geometry from offline visual trajectories on top of frozen LeWorldModel (LeWM) features. DTRC constructs a directed temporal quasimetric over the learned control representation. Short-range temporal offsets calibrate the distance scale. Bootstrapped targets extend temporal reachability across longer horizons. Action-conditioned consistency aligns the representation with local transition dynamics. The resulting distance estimates temporal reaching cost, and its change across a transition defines goal-relative temporal progress. We use this progress signal as a temporal critic for direct goal-conditioned policy learning. Model-assisted targets provide an additional training-time refinement under behavior-support and dynamics-agreement constraints. Across ten visual control tasks, DTRC achieves strong goal-conditioned control performance relative to planning and direct-policy baselines. Held-out diagnostics on the four LeWM tasks show consistent short-range temporal calibration, task-dependent long-range and directional structure, and positive transition-level progress. Temporal supervision improves the same flow-policy parameterization across all four LeWM tasks, while the resulting policy acts directly without iterative trajectory search at test time.

</details>


### [150] [On KL-Regularized Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08963)
*Yifan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出KLPO框架，将KL正则锚定在采样器上，获得闭式Gibbs解，无需重要性权重、评论家或分组响应，实现每提示单次采样的无评论家更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步强化学习训练LLM智能体时，轨迹来自过期检查点，且推理引擎与训练器即使参数相同概率也有差异。现有方法要么裁剪重要性比率导致更新偏差，要么如GRPO对每个提示采样一组响应，在长回合场景下代价高昂。

Method: 提出KL-Regularized Policy Optimization（KLPO），把KL正则项锚定在采样器处，使正则化改进步骤具有闭式Gibbs解；通过在采样器自身轨迹上对对数比率最优性条件做最小二乘拟合，采样器概率以对数比率形式进入，无需重要性权重；消去回归截距，用信号的采样器均值加采样器到训练器的KL散度替代难解的log-partition函数；对token级策略镜像下降目标，证明梯度可由终端回报计算而无需评论家；进一步证明KL项的独立蒙特卡洛估计保持梯度无偏，并给出top-K与二值近似的精确KL差距。

Result: 证明独立蒙特卡洛估计KL项时梯度保持无偏，推导出更廉价top-K与二值近似的精确KL差距，并表明SPPO、GPO、REBEL和BPO均为KLPO的特例。

Conclusion: KLPO得到一种无评论家更新，每个提示只用一次rollout，既不需要学习归一化器，也不需要一组响应。

Abstract: Asynchronous reinforcement learning (RL) for large language model (LLM) agents trains one policy on trajectories generated by another: rollouts come from stale checkpoints, and the inference engine's probabilities differ from the trainer's even at identical parameters. Standard remedies either clip importance ratios, which biases the update, or, as in GRPO, sample a group of responses per prompt, which is costly when episodes are long. We propose KL-Regularized Policy Optimization (KLPO), a framework that anchors the KL regularizer at the sampler. The regularized improvement step then has a closed-form Gibbs solution, and KLPO fits its log-ratio optimality condition by least squares on the sampler's own trajectories, so the sampler probability enters through a log-ratio and no importance weights are needed. Profiling out the regression intercept replaces the intractable log-partition function with the signal's sampler mean plus a sampler-to-trainer KL divergence. For token-level policy mirror descent targets, we show that the resulting gradient can be computed from terminal returns without a critic, via sampler-centered scores or a single trajectory residual, even under stochastic tool outputs. We further prove that independent Monte Carlo estimates of the KL term keep these gradients unbiased, derive the exact KL gap of cheaper top-$K$ and binary approximations, and show that SPPO, GPO, REBEL, and BPO arise as special cases of KLPO. The result is a critic-free update that uses one rollout per prompt and requires neither a learned normalizer nor a group of responses.

</details>


### [151] [Work While They Sleep: Exploiting Evaluation Latency for Fully Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08969)
*Gustavo Sutter,Alejandro Comas-Leon,David Holzmüller,Hao Wang,Luis Ricardez-Sandoval,Pascal Poupart,Agustinus Kristiadi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ELF-BO利用目标函数评估的延迟时间预先采样超参数后验，实现完全贝叶斯优化而不增加决策时间成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑箱优化中目标函数评估耗时且昂贵，样本效率至关重要。标准贝叶斯优化用点估计拟合代理模型超参数，而完全贝叶斯方法通过模型平均更好估计不确定性，适合低数据场景，但计算成本过高而很少被使用。

Method: 提出ELF-BO算法，利用目标评估延迟来提前计算下一个建议点：在目标函数评估过程中从超参数后验中采样，待观测到目标值后仅需对样本重新加权。

Result: 在合成函数和真实应用中，ELF-BO达到完全贝叶斯方法的性能，而决策延迟仅与标准BO相当甚至更优。

Conclusion: ELF-BO使完全贝叶斯优化在真实应用场景中变得切实可行。

Abstract: Black-box optimization problems are ubiquitous across science and engineering, often dealing with expensive objective functions. This objective latency has two consequences during optimization: (i) the objective evaluation dominates execution time, and (ii) sample-efficient algorithms are crucial to accelerate development and avoid wasting resources. Bayesian optimization (BO) methods are the \textit{de facto} choice of planners for suggesting the next point to try. Standard BO fits the surrogate model's hyperparameters with a point estimate. Alternatively, a fully Bayesian approach uses model averaging to account for uncertainty over the hyperparameters, leading to better uncertainty estimates---useful in the low-data regime that is pervasive in BO. However, it is often prohibitively expensive and thus rarely used. In this work, we propose ELF-BO, an algorithm that uses the objective evaluation latency to headstart the computation of the next suggestion, allowing for fully Bayesian optimization without incurring substantial decision-time costs. This is done by sampling from the hyperparameter posterior \emph{while} the objective is being evaluated, only requiring reweighting of the samples once the objective value is observed. Across synthetic functions and real-world applications, we show that ELF-BO matches the performance of fully Bayesian methods while only incurring decision latency on par with or better than standard BO. Thus, ELF-BO makes fully Bayesian optimization practical in real-world use cases.

</details>


### [152] [The Best Optimizer Depends on Batch Size](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08975)
*Xingyu Dang,Kaiyue Wen,Sadhika Malladi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文质疑"最优优化器在不同批量大小下保持不变"的假设，指出Muon缺乏一致的缩放规则，且语言模型预训练的最优优化器会随批量大小改变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大量自适应优化器通过估计小批量梯度统计来调整参数更新，但通常仅在单一批量大小下进行基准测试。虽然超参数缩放规则被认为能在大批量和小批量间保持性能，从而暗示最优优化器具有批量大小的不变性，但这一做法的合理性尚未被充分验证。

Method: 作者通过系统实验挑战上述开发与评估优化器的思路：探究Muon是否存在一致的超参数缩放规则，并在多种训练设置和训练场景下检验最优优化器与批量大小之间的关系（即使经过充分的超参数调优）。

Result: 实验显示：(1) 对于Muon，没有任何有原则的缩放规则能在不同训练设置下始终成立；(2) 即使经过大量超参数调优，语言模型预训练的最优优化器也会随批量大小而变化。

Conclusion: 优化器的开发和评估不应假设最优策略在单一批量大小下可迁移到其他批量大小。批量大小是决定最优优化器选择的关键因素，未来研究需在多个批量大小下进行基准测试，并谨慎对待缩放规则的普适性。

Abstract: A plethora of new adaptive optimizers are designed to efficiently estimate and use minibatch gradient statistics to shape parameter updates, but they are typically benchmarked at a single batch size. Hyperparameter scaling rules promise to preserve performance as batch size and gradient noise change, suggesting that the best optimizer at one batch size should remain the best at another. We challenge this approach to developing and evaluating optimizers by showing: (1) no principled scaling rule for Muon works consistently across training settings, and (2) the best optimizer for language model pretraining changes with batch size even after extensive hyperparameter tuning.

</details>


### [153] [SNR-Gated LSTM-Conditioned Diffusion Model for MIMO Channel Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08977)
*Jixing Zhou,Xinming Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种时序条件扩散框架，在角度域去噪进行MIMO信道估计，结合LSTM时序条件、可学习SNR门控融合和SNR自适应DDIM采样，实现高精度低时延。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代MIMO系统在移动场景下信道呈现结构稀疏性和强时间相关性，需要高精度且低时延的信道估计。现有扩散方法多基于单快照估计，未充分利用时序动态，且需要大量反向扩散步数导致高时延。

Method: 1) 从最小二乘（LS）观测出发，在角度域进行扩散去噪；2) 用LSTM对短观测序列编码条件信息，捕捉时序动态；3) 引入可学习的SNR门控后融合捷径，通过带可训练中心和尺度的sigmoid门将网络输入注入最终解码阶段，平衡观测保真度与生成先验；4) 采用确定性DDIM反向更新，并进行SNR自适应截断与步数分配，高SNR时显著减少反向步数。

Result: 在时间演化的标准化信道模型仿真中，所提方法相对现有基于扩散的信道估计基线取得一致性能提升，同时通过SNR自适应推理保持低时延。

Conclusion: LSTM时序条件与SNR门控融合的扩散框架可有效提升信道估计精度，SNR自适应DDIM推理在保证性能的同时大幅降低时延，适合高移动MIMO场景。

Abstract: Accurate and low latency channel estimation is critical for modern MIMO systems, particularly under mobility, where channels exhibit structured sparsity and strong temporal correlation. This paper proposes a time-series conditioned diffusion framework for channel estimation that performs denoising in the angular domain. Starting from least squares (LS) observations, we train a diffusion denoiser whose conditioning information is encoded by a long short-term memory (LSTM) network over a short observation sequence, enabling the model to exploit temporal dynamics beyond per-snapshot estimation. To robustly balance observation fidelity and learned generative priors across a wide signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) range, we introduce a learnable SNR-gated late-fusion shortcut that injects the network input into the final decoding stage through a sigmoid gate with trainable center and scale. To reduce inference latency, we adopt deterministic denoising diffusion implicit model (DDIM) style reverse updates with SNR-adaptive truncation and step allocation, which significantly reduces the number of reverse diffusion steps at high SNR while maintaining strong performance in low SNR regimes. Simulations on time-evolving standardized channel models demonstrate that the proposed method achieves consistent performance gains over existing diffusion-based channel estimation baselines, while retaining low latency through SNR-adaptive inference.

</details>


### [154] [LASER: Latent Space Adjoint Matching for Support-Constrained Entropy-Regularized Offline RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08989)
*Songyuan Zhang,Oswin So,Eric Yang Yu,Matthew Cleaveland,Peter Crowley-Dolen,Chuchu Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LASER是一种离线强化学习算法，在流匹配行为克隆策略的潜在空间中进行熵正则化RL，通过潜在空间伴随匹配实现，无需时间反向传播，在40个OGBench任务上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线RL受限于分布外（OOD）动作的风险。近期方法通过流匹配学习行为克隆策略，再在其受限的潜在空间中进行RL，但朴素地优化潜在策略容易导致策略坍缩为脆弱模式或利用critic的尖锐伪影。

Method: LASER在潜在空间RL中引入熵正则化应对上述挑战，采用潜在空间伴随匹配（latent-space adjoint matching）实现具有表达力强的流策略的熵正则化潜在空间RL，同时避免沿时间反向传播。

Result: 在40个具有不同数据集质量的OGBench挑战任务上，LASER取得最先进性能。所有任务使用固定的方法特定超参数，优于包括经任务和数据集特定调参的基线，展现出稳健的适用性。

Conclusion: 熵正则化对潜在空间RL至关重要，LASER通过潜在空间伴随匹配实现了高效、稳健的离线RL，在无需逐任务调参的情况下达到SOTA。

Abstract: While offline reinforcement learning (RL) enables policy optimization from static datasets without costly online interaction, it remains bottlenecked by the risk of executing out-of-distribution (OOD) actions. Recent approaches mitigate this by learning a behavior-cloning policy through flow matching and then performing RL within its constrained latent space. However, naively optimizing the latent policy can easily cause the policy to collapse into a brittle mode or exploit sharp artifacts of the learned critic. In this work, we find that entropy regularization is essential in latent-space RL for addressing these challenges. We introduce LASER, a novel offline RL algorithm that applies latent-space adjoint matching to achieve entropy-regularized latent-space RL with expressive flow policies while avoiding backpropagation through time. Through comprehensive experiments on 40 challenging OGBench tasks with varying dataset qualities, we show that LASER achieves state-of-the-art performance. Notably, LASER uses fixed method-specific hyperparameters across all tasks and outperforms the evaluated baselines, including those with task- and dataset-specific tuning, which highlights the robust applicability of LASER. Project website: https://mit-realm.github.io/laser/.

</details>


### [155] [REFIT: Recognize, Fix, and Test Wearable Sensor Placement Shifts without Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08991)
*Bangxun Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: REFIT是一种针对冻结活动识别模型的无标签输入校准方法，可在传感器佩戴方式改变（如换腕、反向佩戴）时恢复精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署时惯性传感器佩戴方式可能与训练时不同（换腕、翻转佩戴），导致同一动作落在不同坐标轴上，模型精度下降，且无法获取标签或重训练。

Method: 用轴变换族（反射、旋转）描述佩戴偏移，将各族拟合用户数据使简单统计量与训练数据匹配；匹配最优的族即识别出偏移类型；在冻结模型前施加该族最优成员并重新估计归一化统计量，用无标签精度估计检验效果，过低时提示用户重新佩戴。

Result: 在真实左右传感器配对及真实/模拟重新佩戴实验中，REFIT在每个数据集上均优于无标签测试时自适应方法，恢复了重新佩戴造成的大部分精度损失；其偏移命名可靠性远超基于置信度的选择器；在所有带符号轴置换校正后，估计能区分成功与失败的校正。

Conclusion: REFIT无需标签或重训练即可校准冻结活动识别模型，有效应对传感器佩戴偏移，并具备可靠的偏移识别与精度自评估能力。

Abstract: We present REFIT, an input calibration for frozen activity-recognition models whose inertial sensors are worn differently at deployment than in training. When users move a watch to the other wrist or put a strap sensor back on turned, the model sees the same motion on changed axes. REFIT undoes such shifts without labels or retraining. It describes them by families of axis transforms, such as reflections and rotations, and fits each family to the user's data so that simple statistics match those of the training data. The family that removes most of the mismatch names the shift. REFIT fixes the shift by applying the best member of that family before the frozen model and re-estimating its normalization statistics. It tests the fixed model with a label-free accuracy estimate and asks the user to re-wear the sensor when it is low. Experiments on real left/right sensor pairs and on real and simulated re-attachment show that REFIT outperforms label-free test-time adaptation methods on every dataset and restores most of the accuracy lost to re-attachment. It names injected shifts far more reliably than a confidence-based selector. After a correction over all signed permutations of the axes, the estimate separates successful from failed corrections.

</details>


### [156] [Algorithmic Scratchpads and Curriculum Staging for Arithmetic Reasoning in Tiny Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09003)
*Sourabh Kasliwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究紧凑型Tiny Transformer在算术任务中的多步推理机制，发现数据加载、预训练与架构设计是关键基础，而算法式草稿（scratchpad）的表述方式直接决定成败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归LLM在确定性多步算法任务（如多位乘法、长除法）上表现不佳，本文旨在剖析小型Transformer执行多步算术的内在机制，厘清影响成败的训练与架构因素。

Method: 使用约10.6M非嵌入参数（总计49.3M）的Tiny Transformer，在合成数据上训练加减乘除四个操作，采用逐步scratchpad展开。方法包括：连续序列打包、语言预训练、现代架构组件（RoPE、RMSNorm、SwiGLU）与稀疏MoE、4阶段分层发展式课程，以及确定性的逐位长除法scratchpad与FOIL乘法scratchpad。

Result: 序列填充造成83%梯度饥饿，使准确率从40%崩至1%，连续序列打包可修复；无语言预训练仅≤2.0%。逐位长除法scratchpad配合4阶段课程将单位数除法从4.0%提升至86.7%。多位数乘法仍困难，FOIL草稿因需单步同时累加多达9个多位项而无中间成对累加导致失败。4位未见操作数性能降至0.00%，无缓冲训练引发灾难性遗忘，除法从86.7%跌至0.00%。

Conclusion: 多步算术能力高度依赖训练基础（数据打包、语言预训练、现代架构）、算法式scratchpad表述以及课程设计；同时存在泛化边界（未见位数）与稳定性边界（灾难性遗忘）。

Abstract: Autoregressive Large Language Models (LLMs) frequently struggle with deterministic multi-step algorithmic tasks such as multi-digit multiplication and long division. In this paper, we investigate the mechanics of multi-step arithmetic in compact "Tiny" Transformers (~10.6M non-embedding parameters, 49.3M total) trained on synthetic data across four basic operations (+, -, *, /) unrolled as step-by-step scratchpads. First, we establish the necessary training foundations: (1) dataloader sequence padding creates an 83% gradient starvation artifact that collapses accuracy from 40% to 1%, remediated via continuous sequence packing; (2) linguistic pretraining is an essential prerequisite (<= 2.0% without it); and (3) modern architectural primitives (RoPE, RMSNorm, SwiGLU) and Sparse Mixture of Experts (MoE) substantially improve additive reasoning over baseline GPT-2. Second, we demonstrate that algorithmic scratchpad formulation directly dictates success. Introducing a deterministic Digit-by-Digit Long Division scratchpad within a 4-stage Hierarchical Developmental Curriculum dramatically elevates single-digit division from 4.0% to 86.7% accuracy on a 4,000-problem held-out benchmark. In contrast, multi-digit multiplication remained challenging: detailed error analysis revealed that while the model correctly computed single-digit sub-products and place-value zeros, our FOIL scratchpad failed because it forced a simultaneous summation of up to nine multi-digit terms in a single step without pairwise intermediate accumulation. Finally, we identify two key boundaries: performance collapses to 0.00% on unseen 4-digit operands, and unbuffered training induces catastrophic forgetting, collapsing division accuracy from 86.7% down to 0.00%.

</details>


### [157] [Removing Information Content Does Not Certify Tamper Resistance in Open-Weight Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09004)
*Domenic Rosati,Alessa Carbo,Ali Dadsetan,Hong Huang,Matthew Young,Subhabrata Majumdar,Frank Rudzicz,Hassan Sajjad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出仅凭发布时的互信息无法普遍证明开放权重模型对微调攻击具有缓慢恢复的抵抗力，因为保持功能的重新参数化不改变信息却改变梯度下降几何，认证上界由最快可达参数化决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于检验"移除有害信息"是否能让开放权重模型抵抗微调攻击。已有做法常以发布时的互信息作为安全认证依据，但作者认为互信息单独无法普遍保证恢复速度慢，因此需要揭示这一认证缺陷。

Method: 作者提出保持功能的重新参数化原理：它不改变信息量，却改变梯度下降几何，故任何不变性认证受限于最快可达的参量化。将该原理应用于训练数据过滤下的权重—数据互信息，以及能力移除下的标签—表征互信息，并构造显式反例：两个信息量均为零却在一步梯度下恢复。同时辅以受控实验，展示固定信息下训练顺序影响恢复时间、以及参量化影响攻击速度。

Result: 理论分析表明，在固定权重—数据信息下训练顺序可改变恢复时间；精确的表征级独立性仍可能保留整个参数雅可比。显式构造中两个互信息量均为零，却在一次梯度步内恢复。受控实验验证了恢复对训练顺序的依赖以及攻击速度对参量化的依赖。

Conclusion: 结论是：安全认证缺失的关键要求是对攻击动力学的约束，而不仅仅是发布时的互信息。仅靠互信息无法普遍认证微调攻击下的缓慢恢复。

Abstract: Does removing harmful information make open-weight models resistant to fine-tuning attacks? We show that mutual information at release alone cannot universally certify slow recovery. Function-preserving reparameterizations leave information unchanged while altering gradient-descent geometry, so an invariant certificate is bounded by the fastest reachable parameterization. We apply this principle to weight--data mutual information under training-data filtering and label--representation mutual information under capability removal. Training order can change recovery time at fixed weight--data information, while exact representation-level independence can preserve the entire parameter Jacobian. An explicit construction has both information quantities equal to zero and recovers in one gradient step. Controlled experiments illustrate order-dependent recovery and parameterization-dependent attack speed. These results identify the missing requirement for certification: constraints on attack dynamics beyond mutual information at release.

</details>


### [158] [Neighborhood Smoothing for Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09020)
*Idan Horowitz,Avigdor Gal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出将图平滑作为训练时校准的一般原则，通过鼓励表示空间中相邻样本具有相似的预测分布来改善神经网络过度自信问题，并据此提出基于图的训练时正则化方法，在多个标准校准基准上取得优异效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代神经网络常常校准不良，倾向于过度自信。现有训练时校准方法主要修改任务损失或校准惩罚项，未能充分利用所学表示中的邻域结构。

Method: 引入图平滑作为训练时校准的一般原则，鼓励表示空间中相邻样本具有相似的预测分布。分析图平滑的效果，推导将相邻样本间预测散度与局部置信度变化及逐点校准误差传播联系起来的界，并刻画平滑能或不能改善校准的条件。据此提出一种基于图的训练时正则化方法，惩罚相邻样本预测分布之间的Jensen-Shannon散度。

Result: 在标准校准基准上，该方法改善了预测质量，且其提升与事后校准互补：经温度缩放后，在八个图像和表格设置中的七个上取得了所有评估的训练时方法中最低的NLL。

Conclusion: 这些发现证明了在学习表示上进行图平滑对神经网络校准的价值。

Abstract: Modern neural networks are often miscalibrated, with a tendency to overconfidence. Existing train-time calibration methods largely modify task losses or calibration penalties, leaving neighborhood structure in learned representations underexploited. We introduce graph smoothing as a general principle for train-time calibration, which encourages similar predictive distributions across neighboring samples in representation space. We analyze the effects of graph smoothing, deriving bounds that connect predictive divergence between neighboring samples to local confidence variation and to the propagation of pointwise calibration error, and characterize the conditions under which smoothing can or cannot improve calibration. In light of this analysis, we propose \modelNoSpace, a graph-based train-time regularizer that penalizes the Jensen--Shannon divergence between predictive distributions of neighboring samples. We present a thorough empirical analysis, showing that across standard calibration benchmarks, \model improves predictive quality, and the improvement is complementary to post-hoc calibration: after temperature scaling, \model attains the lowest NLL of all evaluated train-time methods in seven of the eight image and tabular settings. These findings demonstrate the value of graph smoothing over learned representations for neural network calibration.

</details>


### [159] [SPIN: Shadow Predictive Indexer for Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09025)
*Yao Fu,Cyrus Chang,Ritchie Zhao,Bryce Long,Yueying Li,Mahdi Kamani,Samkit Jain,Rahul Raman,Tara Safavi,Shreya Gupta,Parsa Ashrafi Fashi,Minseok Lee,Julien Demouth,Bita Darvish Rouhani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPIN提出一种基于轻量历史预测的索引器（Shadow Predictive Indexer），通过预测重要KV块、避免每步全量KV打分，降低索引器开销，在保持任务质量的同时实现30-40%稀疏度，并在vLLM服务中提升吞吐、降低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于索引器的稀疏注意力虽降低了核心注意力成本，但索引器仍需在每个解码步对完整KV缓存打分，随着上下文增长，该打分开销成为主要瓶颈。

Method: SPIN采用轻量的、基于历史的预测来识别重要的KV块，从而避免每个解码步对全部KV缓存打分；同时将KV块与推测解码作为核心设计与实现考量。

Result: 在长上下文与智能体基准上的广泛评估中，SPIN在保持任务质量的同时达到30-40%稀疏度；在端到端vLLM服务中，输出吞吐量最高提升14.9%，中位token间延迟最高降低13.2%。

Conclusion: SPIN通过历史预测替代全量KV打分，有效缓解索引器瓶颈，在长上下文场景下兼顾效率与质量，并可实际提升在线服务性能。

Abstract: Indexer-based sparse attention reduces the cost of core attention by passing only a fixed, small number of important tokens to it. However, the indexer must still score the entire KV cache at every decoding step. This scoring overhead becomes a major bottleneck as the context length grows. We propose SPIN (Shadow Predictive Indexer) to reduce this indexer overhead. SPIN uses lightweight, history-based prediction to identify important KV blocks, avoiding the need to score the full KV cache at every decoding step. SPIN treats KV blocks and speculative decoding as first-class design and implementation considerations. Across extensive evaluations on long-context and agentic benchmarks, SPIN achieves 30-40% sparsity while preserving task quality. In end-to-end vLLM serving, SPIN improves output throughput by up to 14.9% and reduces median inter-token latency by up to 13.2%.

</details>


### [160] [ORACLE: Optimizer-Relative Alignment for Constrained LEarning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09040)
*Utkarsh Grover,Wyatt Mackey,Kaixun Hua,J. Morris Chang,Xiaomin Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出优化器相对约束学习视角（ORACLE），在优化器更新之后评估约束兼容性，跨8个PDE基准和4种优化器在94%配置中提升或持平原生优化器表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有约束处理方法通常在优化器动作之前介入，通过修改目标函数或梯度来实现。然而动量、自适应缩放和结构化预条件会在信号变为参数更新前对其产生实质性重塑，因此仅在前端修改信号可能无法准确反映最终更新对约束的兼容性。

Method: 提出优化器相对约束学习（optimizer relative constrained learning），在优化器更新后的端点上评估约束兼容性。据此设计ORACLE：通过异构约束族的联合端点线性化评估原生优化器实际产生的步长，在优化器自身几何中构造对齐方向，对其权限进行界定，并仅在验证后执行该更新。

Result: 在8个偏微分方程基准和4种优化器（涵盖欧氏、对角自适应和结构化预条件几何）上评估，ORACLE在94%的配置中提升或匹配原生优化器；跨模型分析显示在92%的配置中具有相同表现；匹配对比表明其优于在目标、梯度及优化器后层面介入的替代约束处理方法。

Conclusion: 将约束兼容性评估移至优化器更新之后是一种有效且通用的思路，ORACLE能够适应不同优化器几何结构，并在多种基准与模型上稳定优于或持平原生优化器及现有约束处理方法。

Abstract: Constraint handling methods typically intervene before the optimizer acts, by modifying the objective or the gradient. Yet momentum, adaptive scaling, and structured preconditioning can substantially reshape that signal before it becomes a parameter update. We formulate optimizer relative constrained learning, where constraint compatibility is assessed on the post optimizer update. Building on this view, we introduce ORACLE, which evaluates the native optimizer's realized step through a joint endpoint linearization of heterogeneous constraint families, constructs the resulting alignment in the optimizer's own geometry, bounds its authority, and commits it only after validation. We evaluate ORACLE across eight Partial Differential Equation benchmarks and four optimizers spanning Euclidean, diagonal adaptive, and structured preconditioned geometries, where it improves or matches native optimizer in 94% of configurations. Cross model analysis shows the same behavior in 92% of configurations, while matched comparisons show improvements over alternative constraint-handling methods acting at the objective, gradient, and post-optimizer levels.

</details>


### [161] [Towards Financial World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09048)
*Humzah Merchant,Alec Guthrie,Simon Mahns,Randall Balestriero,Bradford Levy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了包含近一万亿观测的美国股票市场数据集Market-1T，并系统比较18种编码器训练策略，发现预测性能相近的编码器对市场状态的组织方式可能差异显著，为金融市场世界模型的表征学习奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建世界模型需要有助于规划与决策的状态表征，在金融市场中这要求模型能够推理市场整体状况、资产预期收益、流动性、波动率及跨资产关系。然而现有金融表征学习多局限于单一预测任务、单一时期和较窄数据集，缺乏系统性评估。

Method: 作者提出三项贡献：1）构建Market-1T数据集，涵盖2008-2025年美国股票、1Hz分辨率、近一万亿个观测；2）设计严格的评估协议；3）开展大规模系统性研究，在近二十年的多种市场环境下比较18种编码器训练策略，既评估表示在常见金融任务上的预测效用，也通过探针分析其潜在结构。

Result: 研究发现，预测性能相近的编码器在组织市场状态的方式上可能存在很大差异，说明仅凭预测任务指标不足以刻画表征质量。

Conclusion: 本文为训练和评估金融市场表征提供了基础，可支持DINO-WM、V-JEPA 2、LeWM等世界模型的研究。

Abstract: Building a world model requires a state representation useful for planning and decision-making---potentially over tasks unknown at training time. In the context of financial markets, planning and decision-making may require a model to reason about market-wide conditions, asset-specific expected returns, liquidity, volatility, and cross-asset relationships. Yet financial representation learning has largely been evaluated on individual predictive tasks, oftentimes on a single time period using comparatively narrow datasets. We address this through three primary contributions. First, we introduce Market-1T, a dataset containing nearly one trillion observations across U.S. equities from 2008 to 2025 at 1 Hz resolution. Second, we develop and implement a rigorous evaluation protocol. Third, we conduct a systematic large-scale study of financial representation learning, comparing 18 encoder-training strategies across nearly two decades of market regimes. We evaluate learned representations both by their predictive utility on common finance tasks and through probes of latent structure. We find that encoders with similar predictive performance can organize market state very differently. Collectively, we establish a foundation for training and evaluating financial market representations in support of world models such as DINO-WM, V-JEPA 2, and LeWM.

</details>


### [162] [MorphCL: Morphological Contrastive Learning for Inertial-based Human Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10245)
*Marius Bock,Yuwei Zhang,Juergen Gall,Michael Moeller,Kristof Van Laerhoven,Cecilia Mascolo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Morphological Contrastive Learning（MorphCL），一种结构感知分组的自监督预训练框架，将全局结构建模显式注入基于惯性数据的 SSL 方法中，在 F1 分数上提升最高 15 个百分点，并以 4600 倍更少的数据匹敌或超越现有基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴与移动设备中传感器无处不在，产生了大量人类运动数据，但将无标注记录转化为基础运动模型仍是难题。自监督学习虽缓解了昂贵标注需求，但现有方法未充分利用大规模运动数据的全局结构，依赖随机采样批次与局部对比，对以静止、低方差行为为主的野外惯性数据尤为不利。

Method: 提出 Morphological Contrastive Learning（MorphCL），一种自监督预训练框架，通过结构感知分组（structure-aware grouping）将全局结构的显式建模注入基于惯性数据的 SSL 方法。方法建立在运动分析的两大支柱之上：运动原语（motifs）的发现与领域特定的特征描述子。

Result: MorphCL 显著提升学习编码器的线性探测与微调结果，F1 分数最高提升 15 个百分点；与现有基础模型相比，MorphCL 预训练编码器在线性探测性能上达到或超越它们，而训练数据量少 4600 倍。对所得嵌入空间的定性分析显示具有形态学意义的簇结构，运动学相似的活动类别分离度提升。

Conclusion: MorphCL 通过结构感知分组注入全局结构建模，有效改进惯性数据自监督预训练，在更少数据下取得可与现有基础模型相当甚至更优的性能，并产生形态学上有意义的嵌入结构。

Abstract: Despite the ubiquity of sensors in wearable and mobile devices and the abundance of human movement data they generate, translating unlabeled recordings into foundational motion models remains an open challenge. Self-supervised learning (SSL) has alleviated the need for costly annotations, yet existing approaches leave the global structure of large-scale motion data largely untapped, relying on randomly sampled batches and local comparisons that become particularly problematic for in-the-wild inertial data dominated by stationary, low-variance behaviors. Here we introduce Morphological Contrastive Learning (MorphCL), a self-supervised pretraining framework that uses structure-aware grouping to inject explicit modeling of global structure into inertial-based SSL approaches. Building on two well-established pillars of motion analysis, the discovery of motion primitives, or motifs, and domain-specific feature descriptors, we show that MorphCL substantially improves linear probing and finetuning results of learned encoders by up to 15 percentage points in F1-score. In a comparison with existing foundation models, we demonstrate that MorphCL-pretrained encoders match or surpass them models in linear probing performance while trained on $4600\times$ less data. Qualitative analysis of the resulting embedding spaces further reveals morphologically meaningful cluster structure, with improved separation of kinematically similar activity classes.

</details>


### [163] [A Self-Pruning Transformer: Extreme KV-Cache Compression with Universal Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09051)
*Davis Wertheimer,Haochen Shen,Ahan Gupta,Derrick Liu,Yu Chin Fabian Lim,Mudhakar Srivatsa,Raghu K. Ganti,Minjia Zhang,Naigang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Universal Attention统一框架，用复合衰减机制自适应剪枝KV-cache，实现10倍压缩并提升下游性能，长上下文16k达25倍压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM的KV-cache体积过大，阻碍高效部署。已有工作用衰减机制替换RoPE位置嵌入以剪枝KV-cache，但衰减函数表达力有限，实践中退化为类滑窗的驱逐模式。

Method: 提出一个统一框架，用于组合式且新颖的衰减机制，既能捕捉复杂的key统计与交互，又保留表达力强的RoPE嵌入与Softmax注意力。由此得到Universal Attention，一种高表达力、端到端可训练的结构，其复合衰减机制充当自然、自适应的剪枝准则，移除对注意力计算贡献最小的token。

Result: 在自然语言与合成任务数据上实现10倍压缩的SOTA，同时下游性能优于SOTA基线和未剪枝的oracle。在长度为16k时展现更优的长上下文泛化能力，实现前所未有的25倍压缩。

Conclusion: Universal Attention通过统一的自适应衰减剪枝机制，在保持RoPE与Softmax注意力的同时实现高压缩率与性能提升，为高效LLM部署提供新方案。

Abstract: The large KV-cache size of modern LLMs creates a barrier to efficient deployment. Recent work has explored replacing attention layers' RoPE positional embeddings with alternative decay-based mechanisms, which can then be used to prune KV-cache during inference. However, these decay functions have limited expressivity, and in practice devolve into sliding-window-like eviction patterns. In this work, we propose a unifying framework for complementary and novel decay mechanisms, capturing complex key statistics and interactions while preserving expressive RoPE embeddings and Softmax attention. The resulting Universal Attention is a highly expressive and end-to-end trainable architecture, whose composite decay mechanism acts as a natural, $\textit{adaptive}$ pruning criterion, removing tokens that contribute least to attention computation. Experimentally, Universal Attention achieves state-of-the-art $10\times$ compression on natural language and synthetic task data, while $\textit{improving}$ downstream performance compared to both state-of-the-art baselines and unpruned oracles. It further demonstrates superior long-context generalization with unprecedented $25\times$ compression at length 16k.

</details>


### [164] [BeatFlow-ECG: Rectified Flow for ECG Reconstruction from Indirect Wearable Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09052)
*Mohamed Kamel,Sahar Selim,Walaa Medhat,Tamer Nadeem*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 BeatFlow-ECG，一种条件整流流（rectified-flow）模型，用于由同步 PPG 与惯性信号重建单通道 ECG，在多个数据集上取得优于确定型、对抗型与扩散型基线的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床外连续心脏监测需要既有信息量又便于日常采集的信号。ECG 信息丰富但连续采集不便，而可穿戴 PPG 易于连续获取，却只是心血管的间接测量且对运动高度敏感，因此需要从 PPG 等易采信号重建 ECG。

Method: 提出 BeatFlow-ECG 条件整流流模型，将重建建模为从噪声到 ECG 的条件传输，采用卷积编码器-解码器加 transformer 瓶颈，并显式引入流时间条件；通过 IMU 导出条件特征、运动相关损失加权以及由易到难的训练课程引入运动信息。

Result: 在 PPG-DaLiA 与 WESAD 上采用留一被试协议评估，BeatFlow-ECG 在所有波形与心搏时序指标上均优于确定型、对抗型与扩散型基线，Pearson 相关分别为 0.983 和 0.986，R 峰 F1 为 0.946 和 0.955；相比 Conditional DDPM-1D，L1 误差从 0.085 降至 0.062（PPG-DaLiA）、从 0.074 降至 0.055（WESAD）。额外分析显示在 R 峰相对固定波形区域相关性更高，且在低、中、高运动子集上重建误差均更低。

Conclusion: BeatFlow-ECG 能有效地从同步 PPG 与惯性测量中重建单通道 ECG，在精度和运动鲁棒性上优于现有基线，支持日常生活中的连续心脏监测应用。

Abstract: Continuous cardiac monitoring outside clinical settings requires signals that are both informative and practical to collect during daily life. Electrocardiography (ECG) provides rich information about cardiac rhythm and waveform morphology, while wearable photoplethysmography (PPG) is easier to acquire continuously but is only an indirect cardiovascular measurement and is highly sensitive to motion. We present BeatFlow-ECG, a conditional rectified-flow model for reconstructing single-channel ECG from synchronized PPG and inertial measurements. BeatFlow-ECG models reconstruction as conditional transport from noise to ECG using a convolutional encoder-decoder with a transformer bottleneck and explicit flow-time conditioning. Motion information is incorporated through IMU-derived conditioning features, motion-dependent loss weighting, and an easy-to-hard training curriculum. We evaluate the model under leave-one-subject-out protocols on PPG-DaLiA and WESAD. BeatFlow-ECG achieves the best results among the evaluated deterministic, adversarial, and diffusion-based baselines across all reported waveform and beat-timing metrics, with Pearson correlations of 0.983 and 0.986 and R-peak F1 scores of 0.946 and 0.955, respectively. Compared with Conditional DDPM-1D, L1 error decreases from 0.085 to 0.062 on PPG-DaLiA and from 0.074 to 0.055 on WESAD. Additional analyses on PPG-DaLiA show higher correlation in fixed R-peak-relative waveform regions and lower reconstruction error across low-, medium-, and high-motion subsets.

</details>


### [165] [A Geometry-Based Capacity Theory for Finite-Feature Associative Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09056)
*Jianhai Zhang,Donghao Zhang,Pattarawut Charatpangoon,Bijoy Menon,M. Ethan MacDonald,Wu Qiu,Aravind Ganesh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于几何的容量理论，用于压缩有限特征Hebbian联想记忆中的精确键检索，将检索干扰分解为有限特征噪声与结构干扰，并给出无拟合的检索质量预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解表示几何如何决定联想记忆容量，并区分何时可通过增加特征预算提升性能、何时必须改变表示本身。

Method: 将检索干扰分解为随特征维度下降的有限特征噪声和由键间平方核重叠决定、在无限特征极限下仍存在的结构干扰；针对相关值，联合分析键核与值Gram矩阵，推导有限特征近似。

Result: 给出无拟合的检索质量预测、几何相关的容量上限及达到目标检索质量所需的特征预算；在合成、视觉和医学图像表示上验证，预测的检索曲线与经验行为高度吻合。

Conclusion: 该框架将表示几何直接与记忆容量联系起来，能正确识别偏好记忆设计的变化，并指导性能改进应通过增加特征预算还是改变表示来实现。

Abstract: We develop a geometry-based capacity theory for exact-key retrieval in compressed finite-feature Hebbian associative memory. For random or approximately isotropic values, retrieval interference separates into finite-feature noise, which decreases with feature dimension, and structural interference, which is determined by squared kernel overlap among stored keys and persists in the infinite-feature limit. This yields a fit-free prediction of retrieval quality, reveals a geometry-dependent capacity ceiling, and predicts the feature budget required for a target retrieval quality. When stored values are correlated, we show that retrieval depends jointly on the key kernel and value Gram matrix, and derive finite-feature approximations that account for this interaction. We validate the theory on synthetic, visual, and medical-image representations. Overall, the framework links representation geometry directly to memory capacity and distinguishes when performance can be improved by increasing the feature budget and when the representation itself must be changed. Across these settings, the predicted retrieval curves closely match empirical behavior and correctly identify changes in the preferred memory design.

</details>


### [166] [Learning Cross-Model Activation Alignments with Explicit Many-to-Many Layer Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09058)
*Alina Sudakov,Guy Bar-Shalom,Fabrizio Frasca,Haggai Maron*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MATCHA通过学习源模型与目标模型激活之间的对齐映射，将映射分解为可提取检查的层映射矩阵和层间共享特征映射，在42对模型上实现更忠实的激活重建和更好的检索指标，并支持激活干预的跨模型迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型发布迅速，一个自然的问题是：两个独立训练的模型如何关联，包括哪些层相互对应以及特征如何在其间转换。先前工作大多预先固定层对应关系（按大致相同的相对深度配对），缺乏从数据中学习对应关系的能力。

Method: 提出MATCHA方法，学习从源模型逐层激活到目标模型激活的对齐映射。该映射被分解为两部分：(1) 层映射，其输出为显式的目标×源矩阵，可被提取和检查；(2) 隐藏空间之间的层共享特征映射。两个因子均从提示中联合学习，而非预先固定层对应。

Result: 在涵盖三个模型族的七个模型、共42对模型上，MATCHA比先前方法更忠实地重建目标激活，并大幅提升基于检索的指标。恢复的映射在深度上大体单调，但与多数先前方法不同，始终呈现多对多结构：每个目标层利用一个源层带。该对齐还支持激活空间干预的迁移，使为一个模型开发的控制向量和探针能迁移到另一个模型。

Conclusion: MATCHA能以数据驱动方式联合学习层对应与特征映射，揭示跨独立训练模型的多对多层级关系，并赋能激活干预的跨模型迁移，为理解与复用不同模型间的表征提供了有效工具。

Abstract: LLMs are released at a rapid pace, raising a natural question: how do two independently trained models relate, both in which layers correspond and in how features transform between them? We study this by learning an activation alignment, a map from a source model's layerwise activations to a target's. Our method, MATCHA, factors this map into a layer map, whose output is an explicit target-by-source matrix that can be extracted and inspected, and a layer-shared feature map between hidden spaces. Most of prior work fixes the layer correspondence in advance, pairing layers at roughly the same relative depth; in contrast, we learn both factors jointly from prompts. Across 42 pairs of seven models spanning three different families, MATCHA reconstructs the target's activations more faithfully and improves retrieval-based metrics substantially, w.r.t. previous approaches. The recovered maps are broadly monotone in depth but, in contrast with most previous approaches, are consistently many-to-many: each target layer draws on a band of source layers. Our alignments also enable transfer of activation-space interventions, allowing steering vectors and probes developed for one model to transfer to another.

</details>


### [167] [EDiS: Edge Disjoint Subgraph Sparsification Framework for Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09059)
*Sai Karthik Navuluru,Siddhartha Shankar Das,Franck Dernoncourt,S M Ferdous,Ryan A. Rossi,Nesreen K. Ahmed,Baris Coskunuzer,Alex Pothen,Lakshman Tamil,Mahantesh M Halappanavar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EDiS 将稀疏图训练中的结构提取与逐 epoch 图组合分离：一次性把图分解为可缓存的边不相交子图，再按边预算与保留率跨 epoch 重组，无需重复采样或重新提取结构，在 19 个节点分类基准上取得最优平均表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏 GNN 训练虽能降低计算量，但确定保留哪些边代价高昂。复用单一稀疏图虽便宜，却把训练锁定在固定拓扑上；若逐 epoch 改变拓扑，又需要反复采样或重算。核心矛盾在于结构选择的成本与训练拓扑多样性之间的权衡。

Method: EDiS 框架把一次性的结构提取与逐 epoch 的图组合解耦：先将图一次分解为可缓存的边不相交子图，之后在不同 epoch 和保留率下按边预算约束重组这些子图，而无需重新提取结构。默认构造使用基于特征的分数与连续最大分数覆盖森林；同一组合机制也兼容其他边选择规则。作者还对逐 epoch 采样器（从缓存分解中抽取训练图的组合步骤）进行了组合分析：证明在默认覆盖森林选择器下，存储的分解确定性地保留高分割边；并给出与选择器无关的高分割边在组合训练图中存活的条件界。

Result: 在 19 个同配、异配及大规模节点分类基准上，与 17 个基线在相同边预算下比较，EDiS 取得最高平均基准分数（准确率/ROC-AUC），并在排名方法中拥有最低平均排名与最小 gap-to-best。消融实验显示，结构分解与 epoch 变化带来的收益在紧边预算下最明显。

Conclusion: EDiS 通过“一次分解、多次组合”的方式，在避免重复结构提取开销的同时实现逐 epoch 的拓扑变化，并具备可证明的高分割边保留性质，在多种图类型与紧预算场景下均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Sparse GNN training reduces computation, but deciding which edges to keep can be costly. Reusing one sparse graph is cheap, but locks training to a fixed topology, while varying it across epochs can require repeated sampling or recomputation. We introduce EDiS (Edge-Disjoint Subgraph sparsification framework), which separates one-time structural extraction from per-epoch graph composition. EDiS decomposes the graph once into cacheable edge-disjoint subgraphs, then recombines them into graphs with edge-budget constraints across epochs and retention ratios without re-extracting structure. Our default construction uses feature-based scores and successive maximum score covering forests, while the same composition mechanism also supports alternative edge selection rules. We provide a combinatorial analysis of the per-epoch sampler, the composition step that draws a training graph from the cached decomposition. We show that, under the default covering-forest selector, the stored decomposition deterministically preserves high-score cut edges, and we derive a selector-agnostic conditional bound on high-score cut survival in composed training graphs. Across 19 homophilic, heterophilic, and large-scale node classification benchmarks against 17 baselines under the same edge budget, EDiS achieves the highest mean benchmark score (accuracy/ROC-AUC) and the lowest average rank and gap-to-best among ranked methods. Ablations show the clearest benefits of structural decomposition and epoch variation at tight edge budgets.

</details>


### [168] [Multi-Label Topic Assignment via LLM Distillation: A Comparative Analysis of Generative vs. Discriminative Student Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09063)
*Sourabh Kasliwal,Shubhranshu Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评测了SLM参数规模(1B/4B/8B)与架构范式(因果生成式vs双向判别式)在多标签主题分配任务上的表现，并报告了大规模生产部署经验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商UGC(商品评论与买卖对话)的多标签主题分配面临语言不规范、标签极度稀疏、分类体系快速演变等可扩展性挑战；用LLM作标注预言机蒸馏训练学生模型已成行业标准，但学生模型的最优低延迟架构仍无定论。

Method: 在SLM参数规模(1B、4B、8B)与架构范式(因果生成式 vs 双向判别式)两个维度开展全面评测，将生成式文本到标签分类器与判别式基线(DeBERTa-V3、ModernBERT)进行对比，并考虑标签集扩张与长尾分布场景。

Result: 存在数据依赖的权衡：结构化商品评论上判别式模型优于超轻量生成式模型，但在复杂多轮对话数据上，最小的1B生成式模型即超越判别式基线；生成式模型在标签集扩展至112个主题及严重长尾分布下保持稳健，而判别式基线在规模扩大时Macro-F1下降35%。

Conclusion: 优化后的模型已在商品评论与对话两个域成功生产部署，满足严格延迟要求并在全球市场尺度产生实际业务价值。

Abstract: Multi-label topic assignment for user-generated content (UGC) -- including product reviews and buyer-seller conversations -- poses unique scalability challenges in large-scale e-commerce due to informal language, extreme label sparsity, and rapidly evolving taxonomies. While utilizing Large Language Models (LLMs) as labeling oracles to distill ground-truth data has emerged as an industry standard to bypass prohibitive manual annotation costs, determining the optimal, low-latency architecture for the resulting student models remains an open challenge. To address this, we conduct a comprehensive evaluation across Small Language Model (SLM) parameter scales (1B, 4B, and 8B) and architectural paradigms (causal generative versus bidirectional discriminative). Comparing generative text-to-label classifiers against discriminative baselines (DeBERTa-V3 and ModernBERT), our analysis reveals a crucial data-dependent trade-off: while discriminative models outperform ultra-lightweight generative models on structured product reviews, even the smallest 1B generative model surpasses discriminative baselines on complex, multi-turn conversational data. Furthermore, generative models maintain robust performance under massive label-set expansion (up to 112 topics) and severe long-tail distributions, whereas discriminative baselines suffer a 35% drop in Macro-F1 at scale. Finally, we detail the successful production deployment of these optimized models across both product review and conversational domains, demonstrating strict latency compliance and tangible business impact at a global marketplace scale.

</details>


### [169] [TAP: Efficient Long-Horizon Agent Pruning via Trajectory-Anchored Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09074)
*Yuanzhe Li,Pengxin Wang,Yuxin Ren,Jianing Deng,Jingtong Hu,Song Wang,Jingdi Chen,Huanrui Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Trajectory-Anchored Pruning (TAP)，首个面向 RL 训练智能体的结构化剪枝框架，通过将剪枝与高效 on-policy 恢复耦合，在移除 60% FFN 通道的情况下保持 99.2%（ALFWorld）和 88.0%（WebShop）的密集 7B 智能体成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程智能体任务需要反复调用模型，推高了本已高昂的推理成本。虽然窄域智能体任务暗示可以激进剪枝而不损失性能，但为问答任务设计的现有剪枝方法在智能体模型上会严重降低性能。作者将失败归因于两个决策：剪什么（一次性重要性估计无法跟踪剪枝模型的适应）和如何恢复（离线蒸馏只覆盖教师前缀，全轨迹 on-policy 蒸馏导致学生误差在多轮中累积）。

Method: TAP 将结构化剪枝与高效 on-policy 恢复耦合，将交互锚定在教师轨迹上，同时允许学生生成每个推理-动作响应。冻结的稠密教师监督学生的响应前缀，解决响应内的训练-推理不匹配，并防止学生引起的偏差跨训练轮次传播。TAP 不是一次性剪枝，而是使用恢复目标在恢复后学生上的梯度重新给通道打分，将迭代式通道选择与演化的策略连接起来。

Result: 在移除 60% FFN 通道的情况下，TAP 在 ALFWorld 和 WebShop 上分别保留了密集 7B 智能体 99.2% 和 88.0% 的任务成功率，同时每个成功任务的 GPU 时间分别减少约 22% 和 17%。

Conclusion: 这些结果证明了在有限恢复预算下对长时程智能体进行有效结构化压缩的可行性。

Abstract: Emerging long-horizon agentic tasks require repeated model calls, worsening the inference cost of already-costly language models. While narrow agentic tasks suggest potential for aggressive model pruning without performance drop, empirical results show existing methods proposed for question answering tasks severely degrade task performance when applied to agentic models. We trace this failure to two decisions: what to prune and how to recover. For pruning, one-shot importance estimates fail to track how the pruned model adapts. For recovery, offline distillation covers only teacher prefixes, while full-trajectory on-policy distillation causes student errors to compound across turns. In this work, we propose Trajectory-Anchored Pruning (TAP), the first structural pruning framework for reinforcement learning (RL)-trained agents. TAP couples structural pruning with efficient on-policy recovery, anchoring interactions to teacher trajectories while allowing the student to generate each reasoning-action response. A frozen dense teacher supervises the student's response prefixes, addressing within-response training-inference mismatch while preventing student-induced deviations from propagating across training turns. Instead of one-shot pruning, TAP re-scores channels using gradients of the recovery objective on the recovered student, connecting iterative channel selection to the evolving policy. With 60% of FFN channels removed, TAP retains 99.2% and 88.0% of the dense 7B agents' task success rates on ALFWorld and WebShop, respectively, while reducing GPU time per successful task by approximately 22% and 17%. These results demonstrate effective structural compression of long-horizon agents under a limited recovery budget.

</details>


### [170] [Tucker Bottleneck Attention for Multi-Dimensional Sequence Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09090)
*Ryan Solgi,Parsa Madinei,Zheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Tucker瓶颈注意力(TuBA)，利用低秩张量结构实现高效的全局token混合，将隐藏张量投影到紧凑的Tucker核心上进行多头自注意力，从而实现亚二次复杂度，并在视频预测和天气预测任务上取得优于标准注意力的精度-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自注意力的二次计算成本限制了其对多维数据长序列的可扩展性，因此需要一种能降低计算复杂度同时保持建模能力的高效注意力机制。

Method: TuBA将隐藏张量投影到紧凑的Tucker核心，在核心上执行多头自注意力和线性投影，再把更新写回原始空间，实现亚二次计算；其自回归扩展结合核心内的双向交互与核心间的因果注意力。

Result: 在视频预测和全球天气预测任务上，TuBA相比标准自注意力将误差和计算量分别降低最多24.7%/66.6%（视频预测）和37.1%/85.1%（自回归天气预测），加速最高达4.27倍；低秩Tucker核心与多帧生成也分别优于全秩注意力和逐帧生成。

Conclusion: TuBA通过在低秩Tucker核心上进行注意力计算，有效缓解了自注意力的二次复杂度问题，在多个任务上实现了良好的精度-效率权衡，并优于标准和高效注意力方法及任务专用模型。

Abstract: The quadratic cost of self-attention limits scalability to long sequences from multidimensional data. We introduce Tucker bottleneck attention (TuBA), which exploits low-rank tensor structure for efficient global token mixing. TuBA projects hidden tensors into compact Tucker cores, performs multi-head self-attention and linear projections on the cores, and writes updates back to the ambient space, enabling subquadratic computation. Its autoregressive extension combines bidirectional interactions within cores with causal attention across cores. On video prediction and global weather forecasting, TuBA achieves favorable accuracy-efficiency trade-offs over standard and efficient attention and task-specific models. Compared to standard self-attention, TuBA reduces error and computation by up to 24.7% and 66.6% for video prediction and 37.1% and 85.1% for autoregressive weather forecasting, with speedups up to 4.27 times. Low-rank Tucker cores and multi-frame generation also outperform full-rank attention and frame-by-frame generation, respectively.

</details>


### [171] [FedRSPO+: A Heterogeneity-aware Algorithm for Decision-focused Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09091)
*Konstantinos Ziliaskopoulos,Alexander Vinel,Jiaqi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedRSPO+，一个面向异构跨孤岛场景的决策聚焦联邦学习框架，基于RSPO+平滑代理，提供理论与实验支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有决策聚焦学习（DFL）多假设集中式数据；联邦学习中标准方法只优化预测精度而非决策质量，且未处理下游目标与可行集的异构性。多面体问题中微小扰动会导致最优决策不连续变化，破坏客户端更新与聚合的稳定性。

Method: 基于RSPO+（通过投影平滑决策映射的正则化"先预测后优化"代理），提出异构感知框架FedRSPO+。理论证明RSPO+对正则化决策及（精确正则化且LP解选择一致时）原LP决策的决策误差与遗憾的上界；推导依赖目标与可行集异构性的跨客户端异构性界，同构时消失且无需强凸性；采用退火式模块化训练流程，兼容标准联邦个性化与聚合方法。

Result: 在合成背包、最短路径及真实能源定价任务上，对比纯预测联邦学习与DFL基线，在不同异构性与通信预算下展开实验，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 平滑是稳定协同决策学习的有用要素，FedRSPO+为联邦DFL提供了异构感知的基础。

Abstract: Decision-focused learning (DFL) trains predictive models for downstream optimization, but existing methods largely assume centralized data. In cross-silo settings, federated learning offers a natural alternative, yet standard federated methods optimize prediction over decision quality and do not address heterogeneity in downstream objectives or feasible sets. This heterogeneity is especially challenging for DFL because small perturbations in polyhedral problems can cause discontinuous changes in optimal decisions, destabilizing client updates and aggregation. We propose FedRSPO+, a heterogeneity-aware framework for decision-focused federated learning, built on RSPO+, a regularized predict-then-optimize surrogate that smooths the decision map through projection. We show that RSPO+ upper bounds decision error and regret for the regularized decision and, under exact regularization and consistent LP solution selection, for the original LP decision. We further derive cross-client heterogeneity bounds that depend on both objective and feasible-set heterogeneity, vanish at homogeneity, and require no strong convexity. FedRSPO+ uses an annealed, modular training procedure compatible with standard federated personalization and aggregation methods. Experiments on synthetic knapsack, shortest-path, and real-world energy pricing tasks compare against prediction-only federated learning and DFL baselines under varying heterogeneity and communication budgets. Results suggest that smoothing is a useful ingredient for stable collaborative decision learning and provide a heterogeneity-aware foundation for federated DFL.

</details>


### [172] [MaRK: Markov-adapted Recurrent Kernels for Dynamic Operator Conditioning in State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09092)
*Syed Ibrahim Omer,Ginny Y. Wong. Xiangyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG
