Method: 作者在岭正则化非线性最小二乘框架下，假设局部正则性以及沿精确拟合截面的水平集曲率幅值具有持续性。在此基础上证明两类曲率情形的均匀共存性：先建立全局极小点的存在性，再推导全局极小点处Hessian相对误差的上界(1+√2)/8，同时构造同一低代价集合中具有不定Hessian且相对误差至少15/8的点。随后证明一个正岭帽对所有固定邻域内的独立中心与标签扰动、以及所有不超过该帽的正岭权重均成立，且这些邻域不随岭权重趋于零而收缩。进一步提出基于当前预测水平集的点态证书，用于控制Hessian修正的法向、混合与切向分量，并在预测映射与岭变化时给出该点态类上的尖锐相对误差界。最后通过解析例子说明输出对齐、曲率方向与持续性的作用，并给出低代价集合上Jacobi行满秩及在秩亏参考点附近精确插值的结构性结果。

Result: 在所述假设下，全局极小点存在，且每个全局极小点的Hessian相对误差低于(1+√2)/8；与此同时，同一低代价集合中存在一个点，其Hessian不定且相对误差至少15/8，说明Gauss-Newton曲率在同一低代价区域内可能表现出截然不同的两种情形。存在一个正岭帽，使得上述结论对所有固定邻域内的独立中心与标签扰动、以及所有不超过该帽的正岭权重统一成立，且相关邻域随岭权重趋于零不收缩。基于预测水平集的点态证书可分解并控制Hessian修正的法向、混合和切向部分，并在预测映射与岭变化时给出尖锐的相对误差上界。解析例子刻画了输出对齐、曲率方向与持续性的作用。另一个结构性结果表明，在整个低代价集合上Jacobi行满秩，并且在秩亏参考点附近可实现精确插值。

Conclusion: 岭正则化非线性最小二乘中Gauss-Newton曲率的精度并非全局一致：在全局极小点附近误差可控且较小，但在同一低代价集合内仍可能存在误差极大甚至不定Hessian的点。水平集曲率幅值的持续性、正岭帽的存在性以及点态证书共同为刻画这种共存现象提供了统一框架，且结论对扰动与岭权重具有鲁棒性。Jacobi行满秩与精确插值结果进一步揭示了低代价区域的结构性质。该工作有助于理解Gauss-Newton近似的适用边界，并为诊断与算法设计提供理论工具。

Abstract: We study the accuracy of Gauss-Newton curvature in ridge-regularized nonlinear least squares. Under local regularity and persistence of level-set curvature magnitude along an exact-fit section, we prove uniform coexistence of two curvature regimes. Global minimizers exist, and every global minimizer has relative Hessian error below $(1+\sqrt2)/8$, while the same low-cost set contains a point with an indefinite Hessian and relative error at least $15/8$. One positive ridge cap works for all independent center and label perturbations in fixed neighborhoods and every positive ridge weight up to the cap. These neighborhoods do not shrink as the ridge weight tends to zero. A pointwise certificate based on the current prediction level set controls the normal, mixed, and tangent parts of the Hessian correction. We prove a sharp relative-error bound over the stated pointwise class when the prediction map and ridge vary. Analytic examples describe the roles of output alignment, curvature orientation, and persistence. A separate structural result gives full Jacobian row rank throughout low-cost sets and exact interpolation near a rank-deficient reference.

</details>


### [242] [CERO: Where and When to Allocate Rollouts for RL Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09679)
*Yiming Zong,Yige Wang,Xing Hu,Jiashuo Jiang,Zuo-Jun Max Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究如何在整体训练周期内协调有限的rollout预算，提出基于凹代理效用与在线原始-对偶调度的CERO方法，实现提示选择与预算分配的动态调整，并在多个数学推理基准上取得最优表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有组相对强化学习方法通常对每个更新步骤按提示分配固定预算，缺乏对整体训练周期内有限rollout预算的全局协调。作者希望解决在训练全程中如何动态分配与调度预算的问题。

Method: 将问题建模为累积提示曝光量的凹代理效用，提出CERO——一种用于提示准入与预算节奏控制的在线原始-对偶调度器。每个被准入的提示获得固定大小的响应组，CERO自适应决定选择哪些提示、跨轮次 revisit 的频率以及每轮生成的组数。使用紧凑的Fenchel表示线性化对累积曝光的依赖，并通过投影在线梯度下降利用奖励变异反馈与预算偏差更新提示特定支撑斜率和共享预算价格。

Result: 在匹配训练响应预算下，CERO在三种骨干模型、五个数学推理基准上均取得最高的avg@16宏平均。机制分析显示CERO的提示选择与组内奖励对比相关，多种子消融实验表明自适应节奏控制优于均匀和预设支出计划。同时建立了针对固定速率和同路径时变基准的路径级保证。

Conclusion: CERO通过在线原始-对偶调度实现了训练全程内的rollout预算协调，在提示选择与预算节奏上具备自适应能力，实验与理论均验证其有效性。

Abstract: Adaptive rollout methods for group-relative reinforcement learning typically allocate a fixed per-update budget across prompts. We instead study how to coordinate a finite rollout budget over the entire training horizon. We formulate this problem using a concave surrogate utility of cumulative prompt exposure and introduce CERO, an online primal dual scheduler for prompt admission and budget pacing. In our experiments, each admitted prompt receives a fixed-size response group. CERO instead adapts which prompts are selected, how often they are revisited across rounds, and how many groups are generated in each round. A compact Fenchel representation linearizes the dependence on cumulative exposure, while projected online gradient descent updates prompt-specific supporting slopes and a shared budget price using reward-variation feedback and budget deviations. We establish pathwise guarantees for the surrogate allocation objective against fixed-rate and same-path time-varying benchmarks, with explicit terms for proxy discrepancy and rate variation. Under matched training-response budgets, CERO attains the highest avg@16 macro-average on each of three backbones across five mathematical reasoning benchmarks. Mechanistic analyses link CERO's prompt choices to within-group reward contrast, while multi-seed ablations show gains from adaptive pacing over both uniform and preset spending schedules.

</details>


### [243] [Few-Shot Learning for Personalised Automated Pain Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09692)
*Heinke Hihn,Ibrahim Eisawy,Patrick Thiam,Hans A. Kestler,Friedhelm Schwenker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将自动疼痛评估中从群体到个体的评估转变重新解释为任务域偏移，采用少样本学习（元学习子领域）实现个性化分类，并在三个数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 疼痛感知在个体间差异显著，导致基于群体的分类器难以泛化到数据集中的所有受试者，需要个性化分类器来应对受试者间变异性。

Method: 将群体级到受试者级评估的转变视为任务域偏移（观测类别不变，目标受试者改变），采用少样本学习进行个性化；通过k-shot条件化实现支持条件适应，并在BioVid、SenseEmotion和PMED三个数据集上使用留一受试者交叉验证评估，另附消融实验与个性化效果分析。

Result: 二分类与多分类任务下，BioVid达85.75%和35.49%，SenseEmotion达82.37%和41.88%，PMED（仅二分类基准）达90.47%；引入k-shot条件化后分别提升至86.25%、40.06%、83.43%、44.08%和91.25%。

Conclusion: 支持条件化的少样本自适应可有效缓解受试者间变异性，提升平均性能，表明少样本学习是个性化疼痛评估的有效途径。

Abstract: Pain perception varies substantially across individuals, making it difficult for population-based classifiers to generalise across all subjects in a dataset. One way to account for subject variability is to train personalised classifiers. In this work, we evaluate Few-Shot Learning, a sub-area of Meta-Learning, as an approach to personalisation in automated pain assessment. We re-interpret the shift from population-level to subject-level evaluation as a task-domain shift, where the observed classes remain fixed but the target subject changes. We evaluate our method on the BioVid Pain Database, the SenseEmotion Database, and the PainMonit Experimental Dataset (PMED), reaching 85.75% and 35.49% accuracy on BioVid and 82.37% and 41.88% on SenseEmotion in the binary and multi-class settings under a Leave-One-Subject-Out CV protocol respectively, and 90.47% on PMED, for which only a binary benchmark exists. Using samples to implement k-shot conditioning, the accuracies can be improved to 86.25%, 40.06%, 83.43%, 44.08%, and 91.25%, respectively. To further evaluate the effects and robustness of our method, we provide additional ablation experiments and investigate the personalisation effects. Our results suggest that support-conditioned few-shot adaptation can improve average performance under inter-subject variability.

</details>


### [244] [Pretraining Shapes Spectral Structure: Architecture- and Strategy-Conditional Prediction of OOD Robustness in Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09709)
*Sangyoon Bae,Sk Miraj Ahmed,Shinjae Yoo,Jiook Cha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出仅凭预训练权重即可在无目标数据时预测基础模型的分布外（OOD）鲁棒性：OOD 精度差距受源表示集中度上界约束，而权重谱结构可作为该集中度的代理指标；该指标方向随（架构×预训练策略）组合（称为 cell）而反转，需分组使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 OOD 诊断需要源数据或目标数据，在目标域尚不存在时无法使用；仅用权重的诊断方法对所有架构套用同一统计量，无法区分鲁棒与脆弱模型。作者希望实现"在任何目标数据可用之前"判断模型能否 OOD 泛化。

Method: 从预训练权重的谱结构出发，论证架构决定权重矩阵的信息存储方式、预训练策略决定被奖励的内容，二者共同构成支配 OOD 鲁棒性的谱几何。证明 OOD 精度差距由源表示集中度上界约束，并用仅依赖预训练权重的统计量作为该集中度的代理。由于代理方向在不同架构家族间反转，作者按（架构×策略）组合即 cell 分组，在每个 cell 内选择对应方向的统计量。

Result: 在覆盖 7 种模态的 116 个模型上，混合池化后单一统计量排序能力弱（不同方向 cell 相互抵消）；但在 cell 内，所选统计量在样本内可将 92% 的模型对按 OOD 鲁棒性正确排序。方向选择未泄露目标信息：在运行 OOD 评估前即记录每个新模型家族的 cell、指标与符号，EEG、基因组与蛋白质三类预测方向均成立。基于谱集中度采取行动可在保持 87.5% ID 精度时缩小 OOD 差距 24%。

Conclusion: 该诊断方法仅依赖已发布权重，使 OOD 鲁棒性可在模型选择阶段、在为目标域投入数据或算力之前就被检验，从而把 OOD 评估提前到目标数据存在之前。

Abstract: Can we determine whether a foundation model will generalize out-of-distribution (OOD) before any target data is available? Existing diagnostics require source or target data, which rules them out before a target domain exists. Those that use the weights alone apply one statistic to every architecture, and do not separate robust models from fragile ones. We show the answer is encoded in the spectral structure of pretrained weights. Two forces shape that structure. Architecture determines how information is stored in weight matrices. Pretraining strategy determines what is rewarded. Together they set a spectral geometry that governs OOD robustness. We prove that the OOD accuracy gap is bounded by how tightly the source representations concentrate. A statistic computed from the pretrained weights alone serves as a proxy for that concentration. The direction of that proxy reverses between architecture families. We operationalize it: the direction is stable within one (architecture X strategy) combination, the finest grouping we test, which we call a cell. Pooled over 116 models spanning 7 modalities, a single statistic ranks OOD robustness weakly, because cells of opposite direction cancel. Within a cell, the statistic selected for it orders 92% of model pairs by OOD robustness in-sample. The selection does not leak the target: for each model family outside the matrix we logged the cell, metric and sign before running its OOD evaluation, and the predicted direction held in every case: EEG, genomic and protein. Acting on spectral concentration narrows the OOD gap by 24% at 87.5% ID retention. The diagnostic operates on released weights alone, so OOD robustness becomes checkable at model-selection time, before data or compute is committed to a target domain.

</details>


### [245] [EC-EarthFlow: Probabilistic emulation of daily transient global climate model simulations with flow matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09715)
*Kirien Whan,Nikolaj T. Mücke,Karin van der Wiel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EC-EarthFlow，一个生成式流匹配模型，用其模拟EC-Earth3气候模式的温度场，以更低算力复现日变率、空间格局、年循环和长期趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理气候模型（如EC-Earth3）计算成本高，亟需高效代理模型；而仅用温度单一变量建立稳定、可长时间外推的气候模拟器具有挑战性。

Method: 构建生成式流匹配（flow matching）模型EC-EarthFlow，基于EC-Earth3的1950-2166年SSP2-4.5瞬变模拟训练；输入前几日温度场及年均温度，预测次日温度场；采用自回归方式，rollout时长从一个月到延长季节。

Result: 仅用温度这一关注变量，即可复现EC-Earth3的日变率、空间格局、年循环和长期趋势，计算成本显著低于物理模式；且长时间推理稳定，并能学习EC-Earth3所模拟的物理关系。

Conclusion: EC-EarthFlow表明生成式流匹配可构建稳定、低成本的气候模式代理，仅凭单变量即捕捉关键气候特征及物理关联，具备长时效气候模拟潜力。

Abstract: We introduce EC-EarthFlow, a generative flow matching model that emulates simulations from the physical climate model EC-Earth3. The model is trained on transient simulations from EC-Earth3 (1950-2166, SSP2-4.5) to predict the day ahead temperature field from the previous days temperature as well as annual mean temperature. Predictions are made auto-regressively with rollout periods of between a month and an extended season. Using only this variable of interest, we are able to reproduce the daily variability, spatial patterns, annual cycle and long-term trend from EC-Earth3 at a substantially lower computational cost than the physical model. We demonstrate that EC-EarthFlow is stable for long inference periods, and that it can learn the physical relationships as simulated in EC-Earth3.

</details>


### [246] [SoftSEEPS improves ML-based precipitation forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09752)
*Jost Arndt,Utku Isil,Noelia Otero,Rodrigo Almeida,Wojciech Samek,Jackie Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出可微分的SEEPS近似评分SoftSEEPS，使机器学习模型能直接针对降水预报进行训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SEEPS是评估降水预报的常用评分，但其不可微，无法直接作为机器学习模型的训练损失，因此需要一种可微的近似版本。

Method: 开发可微分的SEEPS近似（SoftSEEPS），并在IMERG数据集（0.1度分辨率）上测试：为预训练的低分辨率预报模型的潜在空间训练一个降水解码器。

Result: 将SoftSEEPS与RMSE组合成联合目标函数可行，两个指标各自仅有轻微折衷。

Conclusion: SoftSEEPS为实现直接以降水评分驱动的机器学习预报训练提供了可行途径。

Abstract: In this paper we have developed a differentiable approximation of the well-known SEEPS score, which we name SoftSEEPS. This allows the training of a Machine Learning model to forecast precipitation directly. We test SoftSEEPS on the IMERG dataset (0.1 degree resolution) by training a decoder for precipitation on the latent space of a pre-trained low-resolution forecasting model. Combining SoftSEEPS and RMSE in a joint objective is possible with marginal trade-offs in either metric.

</details>


### [247] [EntroPrefill: Renyi-Guided Context Pruning with Conditional Stability Guarantees for Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09757)
*Inbasekaran S*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EntroPrefill：一种基于Renyi引导的mid-prefill剪枝机制，对丢弃注意力质量施加显式约束，并给出理论基础、界与权衡分析，但暂未报告实验加速或精度结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: mid-prefill剪枝能减少深层Transformer处理的序列长度，但仅凭注意力集中度无法证明被丢弃的上下文可舍弃，需要更严格的理论刻画与约束。

Method: 将EntroPrefill形式化为Renyi引导的提议机制，并显式约束被丢弃的注意力质量；采用sink隔离、正则化的head pooling以适配grouped-query attention；推导mixture-to-head删除包络、可行token删除的可计算上界、自适应选层下有效的有限样本observer保证，以及带明确Lipschitz常数的条件扰动界与首token决策间隔推论；给出反例与系统层面（query-head unions、物理页分配、KV传输载荷）的算术盈亏平衡条件。

Result: 建立了多项理论结论：注意力质量约束下的删除包络、可行剪枝上界、自适应选层的有限样本保证、条件扰动界与首token决策间隔推论；反例说明仅凭浅层观测无法推出无条件的未来输出保证；系统分析给出剪枝的算术盈亏平衡条件。

Conclusion: 本文为理论性工作，定义流程、假设与形式化极限，不报告实测加速或任务精度保持；实验留待后续验证假设、近似紧度与端到端资源权衡。

Abstract: Mid-prefill pruning can reduce the sequence processed by deeper transformer layers, but attention concentration alone does not certify that discarded context is dispensable. We formulate EntroPrefill as a Renyi-guided proposal mechanism coupled to explicit constraints on discarded attention mass. Sink-isolated, regularized head pooling respects grouped-query attention while exposing a quantitative trade-off between specialization and worst-head coverage. We derive a mixture-to-head deletion envelope, a computable upper bound on feasible token removal, and a finite-sample observer guarantee that remains valid when the pruning layer is selected adaptively. We then establish a conditional transformer perturbation bound with explicit sufficient Lipschitz constants and a first-token decision-margin corollary. A counterexample shows why shallow observations alone cannot imply an unconditional future-output guarantee. The systems analysis distinguishes query-head unions, physical page allocation, and KV-transfer payload, and gives an arithmetic break-even condition for pruning. This manuscript is theoretical in scope: it defines the procedure, its assumptions, and its formal limits, but does not report measured acceleration or task-accuracy preservation. Experiments are reserved for subsequent validation of the assumptions, approximation tightness, and end-to-end resource trade-offs.

</details>


### [248] [Unrolled Flow Models for Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09759)
*Faissal Izermine,Hanru Bai,Oscar Davis,T. Konstantin Rusch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文证明双层Transformer流模型可解图可达性问题，且所需积分步数随目标距根距离增加；提出在随机子区间上进行潜在rollout训练、球面投影稳定动态及rollout选择，使流匹配模型在推理任务上受益于更多积分步数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配（flow matching）能以少步生成语言，但增加积分步数是否提升推理能力尚不明确。作者希望从理论与实证两方面探究流模型在推理任务上的潜力及现有方法的局限。

Method: 1) 理论证明：参数化为双层Transformer的流可解图可达性，所需积分步数随目标到根的距离增长。2) 分析局限：标准流语言模型对每个时间点独立监督，未显式训练相邻步相互累积。3) 提出方法：在随机采样的[0,1]子区间上沿模型自身潜在rollout训练，仅在端点解码；对长rollout任务，将潜在状态收缩到球面上以稳定动态；结合无参数选择评分对多次rollout进行采样选择。

Result: 在ProsQA上准确率提升至97%，且性能随积分步数增加而提升。在Sudoku和Maze等需要更长rollout的任务上，球面稳定显著优于参数量三倍以上的基线。多次rollout采样结合无参数选择评分可进一步提升性能，但对较长答案的可靠选择仍是挑战。

Conclusion: 研究为流模型推理建立了理论基础，并表明rollout训练、稳定的潜在动态与rollout选择有助于在实践中实现这一能力。

Abstract: Flow matching enables language generation in few steps, but whether additional integration steps improve reasoning remains unclear. We prove that a flow parameterized by a two-layer Transformer can solve graph reachability, with the required number of integration steps increasing with the target's distance from the root. Yet, standard flow language models can fail to benefit from additional steps on reasoning tasks. We attribute this limitation to objectives that supervise each time point independently, without explicitly training successive steps to build on one another. To address this, we instead train through the model's own latent rollout over a randomly sampled subinterval of [0, 1], decoding only at the endpoint. On ProsQA, this raises accuracy to 97% and enables performance to improve with additional integration steps. For the longer rollouts required by reasoning tasks such as Sudoku and Maze, retracting the latent state onto a sphere stabilizes the dynamics and yields substantial gains over baselines with more than three times as many parameters. Sampling multiple rollouts further improves performance when paired with a parameter-free selection score, although reliable selection remains challenging for longer answers. Together, these results establish a theoretical basis for reasoning with flows and show how rollout training, stable latent dynamics, and rollout selection help realize this capacity in practice.

</details>


### [249] [Leaner Transformers Can Easily Learn to Cluster](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09760)
*Charlotte Park,Kenneth L. Clarkson,Lior Horesh,Takuya Ito,Parikshit Ram*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文改进了Transformer执行k-means聚类（Lloyd算法）的能力，将嵌入维度从 d+k 降至 d+⌈log₂k⌉，并研究了学习型聚类算法的训练、收敛与泛化特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作证明Transformer能精确执行Lloyd算法，但所需嵌入维度为 d+k，导致注意力投影矩阵规模为 (d+k)²，效率较低。本文希望构建表达能力相同但更小的Transformer，并进一步理解学习型聚类算法的收敛、泛化及聚类能力。

Method: 首先设计了一个更小的Transformer结构，以 d_emb = d + ⌈log₂k⌉ 的嵌入维度执行Lloyd算法；接着在给定聚类任务分布上训练这些Transformer以学习聚类算法；同时从理论上刻画并实验验证影响基于随机梯度的学习算法收敛性与分布内泛化的因素；最后探测这些学习到的Transformer算法的通用聚类能力。

Result: 提出了一个表达能力相同但更紧凑的Transformer，将嵌入维度从 d+k 降至 d+⌈log₂k⌉；理论分析和实验验证了影响学习算法收敛性与分布内泛化性的因素；并通过实验探究了学习到的聚类算法成功与失败的情形。

Conclusion: 本文在降低Transformer执行k-means所需嵌入维度的同时，深入分析了学习型聚类算法的收敛性、泛化性及通用聚类能力，为理解Transformer的上下文学习与算法执行提供了理论与实践支持。

Abstract: Transformers have in-context learning capabilities, where some known learning algorithms can be executed in the forward pass through the model. Recent work shows that transformers can exactly perform Lloyd's algorithm for $k$-means clustering with $n$ points in $d$ dimensions with an embedding size $d_{\textsf{emb}} = d+k$ (thus, requiring attention projection matrices of size $(d+k)^2$). In this work, we build upon this result in the following ways: First, we present an equally expressive but smaller transformer that executes Lloyd's algorithm with embedding size $d_{\textsf{emb}} = (d + \lceil \log_2 k \rceil)$. Next, we train these transformers to learn the clustering algorithms given a distribution of clustering tasks, and theoretically characterize and empirically validate the factors affecting the convergence and in-distribution generalization of learning algorithms based on stochastic gradients. Finally, we probe the general clustering abilities of these learned algorithms (in the form of transformers), and try to understand situations where they succeed and fail.

</details>


### [250] [Beyond Policy Support: Interaction Constrained Offline Reinforcement Learning for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09763)
*Mahmoud Selim,Cristina Cipriani,Karl Henrik Johansson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出交互分布偏移（IDS）问题及ICDP框架，在离线强化学习中显式约束 ego 与周围智能体交互层面的分布偏移，提升交互关键场景的驾驶性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习无需在线探索，适合自动驾驶等安全关键领域，但存在分布偏移问题：策略可能偏向离线数据支持不足的动作，导致价值估计不可靠。现有方法主要在策略自身动作空间控制偏移，而交互环境（如自动驾驶）中，ego 轨迹在边缘行为分布下支持良好，但在与周围智能体联合行为下支持不足，即交互分布偏移(IDS)，此问题尚未被针对性解决。

Method: 提出ICDP（Interaction-Constrained Drive Policy）框架，显式控制交互级分布偏移。从 ego 与周围智能体未来的联合数据分布出发，证明联合支持度退化可精确分解为 ego 支持分量与残差交互支持分量；通过对比密度比估计恢复残差交互支持，从而隔离交互兼容性，无需显式联合密度建模、周围智能体预测，也无需在策略优化中于反应式模拟器或学习世界模型内进行 rollout。

Result: 在 nuPlan、InterPlan 以及真实卡车实验的闭环评估中，ICDP 能抑制高价值但交互不支持轨迹的选择，并在交互关键驾驶场景中提升性能。

Conclusion: ICDP 通过显式约束交互层面的分布偏移，有效解决了离线强化学习中交互分布偏移问题，在多个闭环驾驶基准与真实实验中验证了其有效性，为安全关键领域的离线策略优化提供了新思路。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning enables reward-driven policy improvement from fixed datasets without requiring online exploration, making it particularly attractive in safety-critical domains. A central challenge, however, is distribution shift: policy optimization may favor actions that are weakly supported by the offline data, rendering value estimates unreliable. Existing approaches primarily control this shift in the policy's own action space. In interactive environments such as autonomous driving, this can be insufficient: a candidate ego trajectory may remain well supported under the marginal behavior distribution while being poorly supported jointly with the surrounding-agent behavior observed in the logged interaction. We refer to this degradation in interaction support as \emph{interaction distribution shift} (IDS), and introduce \emph{Interaction-Constrained Drive Policy} (ICDP), an offline reinforcement learning framework that explicitly controls interaction-level distribution shift. Starting from the joint data distribution over ego and surrounding-agent futures, we show that joint-support degradation decomposes exactly into an ego-support component and a residual interaction-support component. We recover the latter through contrastive density-ratio estimation, isolating interaction compatibility without explicit joint-density modeling, surrounding-agent prediction, or rollouts in reactive simulators or learned world models during policy optimization. Closed-loop evaluations on nuPlan, Interplan and real-world truck experiments show that ICDP suppresses high-value yet interaction-unsupported trajectory selections and improves performance in interaction-critical driving scenarios. Project webpage: https://mahmoud-selim.github.io/ICDP/

</details>


### [251] [Fluctuations of Nonlinear Observables in Mean Field Neural Network Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09768)
*Arnaud Descours,Geoffrey Lacour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究宽浅层神经网络均值场极限中参数分布涨落如何传播到有限维非线性可观测量，利用加权Sobolev空间中的泛函Delta方法（无需Lions导数）建立可观测量的中心极限定理与协方差公式，并给出可观测量能否由观测函数局部因子化的微分判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 均值场极限虽已由泛函中心极限定理刻画参数经验分布的渐近涨落，但实际关心的是参数分布的非线性可观测量而非分布本身，因此需要研究涨落如何传播到这些有限维非线性可观测量。

Method: 在构造极限涨落过程的加权Sobolev空间中工作，采用普通Fréchet可微性下的泛函Delta方法，避免使用关于测度变量的Lions导数；并在常数秩假设下分析可观测量的可辨识性（局部因子化）问题。

Result: 得到可观测量的中心极限定理，并在微分表示适当的条件下给出由底层均值场涨落理论继承的显式协方差公式；证明在常数秩假设下，量所关注的量能通过观测泛函局部因子化当且仅当在邻域内观测微分的核包含于目标量微分的核中。

Conclusion: 该工作为量化宽神经网络在有限宽度下对具有统计或物理意义的可观测量所产生的不确定性提供了框架，同时也为判断所选观测是否包含识别目标量所需信息提供了判据。

Abstract: Mean field limits describe the training dynamics of wide neural networks through the evolution of the empirical distribution of their parameters. Although functional central limit theorems characterize the asymptotic fluctuations of this distribution, quantities of practical interest are typically nonlinear observables of the parameter distribution rather than the distribution itself. In this work, we show how these mean field fluctuations propagate to finite dimensional nonlinear observables for shallow neural networks trained by stochastic gradient descent. Working in the weighted Sobolev space in which the limiting fluctuation process is constructed, we apply a functional Delta method under ordinary Fr{é}chet differentiability, without requiring Lions derivatives with respect to the measure variable. We obtain a central limit theorem for the observables and, under a suitable representation of their differentials, an explicit covariance formula inherited from the underlying mean field fluctuation theory. We also study whether prescribed quantities of interest can be recovered from the selected observations. Under a constant rank assumption, we prove that a quantity of interest factors locally through the observation functional if and only if, throughout a neighborhood, the kernel of the differential of the observation is contained in that of the quantity of interest. Thus, a differential condition expressed directly in the ambient Sobolev space yields an exact nonlinear local factorization. These results provide a framework both for quantifying finite-width uncertainty on observable, statistically or physically meaningful quantities and for assessing whether the chosen observations contain the information required to identify them.

</details>


### [252] [AdaPS-LiNGAM: Adaptive Predecessor Selection for Linear Non-Gaussian Acyclic Models under Small-Sample Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09782)
*Shun Yanashima,Kentaro Kanamori,Hirofumi Suzuki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了DirectLiNGAM在变量数超过样本量时反复残差化必然退化的结构性缺陷，并提出AdaPS-LiNGAM方法，通过自适应选择前驱变量子集直接从原始观测重构残差，在样本受限场景下实现了更准确的因果结构恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果发现问题在样本量相对于变量数较小时尤为困难，这一问题同样存在于LiNGAM框架中。DirectLiNGAM通过依次识别外生变量并移除其线性效应来确定因果顺序，但当变量数超过样本量时，这种反复残差化的过程会变得退化，导致无法确定完整的因果顺序。论文旨在解决这一结构性问题。

Method: 论文首先建立了DirectLiNGAM的结构性局限：当变量数超过样本量时，反复残差化在确定完整因果顺序前必然退化。分析进一步发现，每个残差只需利用已排在因果顺序较早位置的、由图结构决定的变量子集（称为"活跃边界"）即可重构。基于此提出AdaPS-LiNGAM，它使用自适应选择的稀疏前驱变量子集，直接从原始观测重构每个残差；同样的子集选择原则也应用于边估计的最终剪枝步骤。

Result: 在合成数据上的实验表明，AdaPS-LiNGAM在样本受限场景下能够准确恢复因果结构，并且随样本量减小时性能退化更为平缓。

Conclusion: AdaPS-LiNGAM通过避免退化残差化、直接基于原始观测进行自适应稀疏重构，有效缓解了DirectLiNGAM在高维小样本下的结构性限制，为样本受限的因果发现提供了更稳健的方案。

Abstract: Causal discovery becomes particularly challenging when the available sample size is small relative to the number of variables. This challenge also arises in the linear non-Gaussian acyclic model (LiNGAM), an identifiable framework for causal discovery from observational data. DirectLiNGAM estimates a causal order, which arranges variables so that causes precede their effects, by sequentially identifying an exogenous variable and removing its linear effect from the remaining variables. We establish a structural limitation of this procedure: when the number of variables exceeds the sample size, repeated residualization necessarily becomes degenerate before the full causal order can be determined. Our analysis further reveals that each residual can be reconstructed using only a graph-determined subset of variables already placed earlier in the causal order, termed the active boundary. This result motivates AdaPS-LiNGAM (Adaptive Predecessor Selection LiNGAM), which reconstructs each residual directly from the original observations using an adaptively chosen sparse subset of those earlier variables. The same subset-selection principle is also applied to the final pruning step for edge estimation. Experiments on synthetic data demonstrate that AdaPS-LiNGAM provides accurate causal-structure recovery in sample-limited settings and degrades more gradually as the sample size decreases.

</details>


### [253] [A Proof-of-Concept Study of Weakly Supervised Labeling of Fine-Grained EEG Components for Artifact Attenuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09792)
*Lu Wang-Nöth,Hai Huang,Philipp Heiler,Shuqiong Wu,Liyun Zhang,Helmut Mayer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种结合频率感知高维表征与多示例学习的框架，用于解决脑电中肌电伪迹混叠和缺乏可靠成分级真值的问题，实现细粒度肌电伪迹检测与衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑电极易受肌电伪迹影响，其时间异质性和与神经活动的空间-频谱重叠会使盲源分离后留下混合源。现有去除方法受限于缺少可靠的成分级真值：专家标注成本高且主观，而多通道头皮脑电中肌电污染尚无成熟的基于仿真的真实标注方法。

Method: 提出一个结合频率感知高维表征与多示例学习的框架。该表征将分离成分展开为频率分辨的子成分，构建使混合神经与肌肉活动更可分离的空间；弱监督学习形式则使各子成分的伪迹似然分数能够仅凭试次级标签学习，而无需更细粒度真值。所得子成分分类器支持细粒度肌电伪迹检测与分数引导的衰减。

Result: 在留出被试上的实验表明，该框架学到了有信息量的子成分分数，并能减少与伪迹相关的频谱偏差：对下颌紧张效果最明显，对挑眉有中等效果，对皱眉效果有限。

Conclusion: 该框架通过频率分辨的子成分表征与多示例弱监督，缓解了肌电伪迹成分级真值缺失的问题，能实现细粒度伪迹检测与衰减，但不同面部肌肉活动下的效果存在差异。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) is highly susceptible to electromyographic (EMG) artifacts, whose temporal heterogeneity and spatial-spectral overlap with neural activity can leave mixed sources after blind source separation. Existing artifact-removal methods are further limited by scarce reliable component-level ground truth: expert annotations are costly and subjective, while no established method provides realistic simulation-based ground truth for EMG contamination in multichannel scalp EEG. To address these limitations, we propose a framework combining a frequency-aware high-dimensional representation with Multi-Instance Learning. The representation unfolds separated components into frequency-resolved intra-components, creating a space in which mixed neural and muscular activity becomes more separable, while the weakly supervised learning formulation enables artifact-likelihood scores for individual intra-components to be learned from epoch-level labels without finer-grained ground truth. The resulting intra-component classifier supports fine-grained EMG artifact detection and score-guided attenuation. Experiments on held-out subjects show that the framework learns informative intra-component scores and reduces artifact-related spectral deviations most clearly for jaw tension, with moderate effects for raising eyebrows and limited effects for frowning.

</details>


### [254] [BoT-GRPO: Efficient Process-Reward RL for Reasoning via Bag-of-Token Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09804)
*Yingxiang Yang,Weihang Xiao,Zhunxuan Wang,Joshua Flashner,Niresh Agarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 BoT-GRPO，将 GRPO 扩展到 token 级奖励模型，通过长度不变的"词袋"聚合实现无 critic 的过程监督，在代码生成与数学推理任务上更快收敛且最终质量更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO 中 rollout 的每个 token 共享相同 advantage，无法利用 token 级过程监督；而引入价值网络成本高昂。作者希望在不增加价值网络开销的前提下，实现高效的过程监督，加速收敛并提升最终质量。

Method: 提出 Bag-of-Tokens Group Relative Policy Optimization（BoT-GRPO）：收集所有 rollout 的 token 级奖励，按来源序列长度的倒数加权每个 token 奖励，再基于加权组统计量计算逐 token advantage。该方法无 critic，可在任何使用 GRPO 且可用 token 级奖励的场景中即插即用。

Result: 在 React 前端代码生成任务上，BoT-GRPO 达到 80% 编译率，速度比 GRPO 快至多 1.9 倍，且收敛快于 GSPO、DAPO、PURE 等 GRPO 变体，最终编译率与 VLM 判定胜率更高。在 AIME 数学推理任务上，Pass@k 相对 GRPO 绝对提升至多 8.1%，且步数减半。两个任务均在推理与非推理基座模型族（Qwen2.5-3B、SmolLM3-3B、Phi-4-mini-reasoning）上进行了对比。

Conclusion: BoT-GRPO 是 GRPO 的有效无 critic 扩展，能显著加速收敛并提升最终性能。实验还给出奖励模型的实用经验：奖励的稳定性比丰富性更重要，干净、有界、稳定的细粒度信号能持续加速学习，而噪声较大的替代方案则会停滞。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is now central to eliciting reasoning in large language models, while in the popular algorithm Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) every token in a rollout receives the same advantage. We ask how to make process supervision efficient: accelerating convergence and improving final quality without the cost of value networks. We propose Bag-of-Tokens Group Relative Policy Optimization (BoT-GRPO), which extends GRPO to token-level reward models through a length-invariant "bag of tokens" aggregation: it collects all token-level rewards across rollouts, weights each by the inverse of its source sequence length, and computes per-token advantages relative to weighted group statistics. BoT-GRPO is critic-free, and is a drop-in replacement wherever GRPO is used when token-level reward is available. On React front-end code generation, BoT-GRPO reaches $80\%$ compile rate up to $1.9\times$ faster than GRPO and converges faster than modern GRPO variants (GSPO, DAPO, PURE) while reaching higher final compile and VLM-judged win rates. On a second task, AIME mathematical reasoning, BoT-GRPO delivers absolute Pass@$k$ gains up to $8.1\%$ over GRPO in half the steps. For both tasks we compare the algorithm's performance on reasoning vs. non-reasoning base-model families (Qwen2.5-3B, SmolLM3-3B, Phi-4-mini-reasoning). Our experiments also yield a practical recipe for the reward model itself: reward stability matters more than richness: clean, bounded, stable fine-grained signals consistently accelerate learning where noisier alternatives stall.

</details>


### [255] [Homogenization in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09824)
*Prakhar Ganesh,Kyra Wilson,Luca Zappella,Barry-John Theobald,Nicholas Apostoloff,Lucas Monteiro Paes,Nivedha Sivakumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出多智能体系统（MAS）存在“同质化”失效模式，并用三个指标量化，在代码生成、招聘和同行评审任务中揭示了具体下游风险，且证明简单的增加多样性方法无效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MAS 通过智能体间交互完成复杂任务，虽取得成效，但交互可能导致智能体行为趋同，削弱多样性并强化共同失败。作者希望将同质化操作化并揭示其风险。

Method: 用三个指标量化同质化：对多数的从众、向极端的极化、以及在后续交互中反对变化的惰性增长。评估三类任务：代码生成、招聘、科学同行评审。并测试增加多样性的简单方法（采样随机性、混合模型MAS）。

Result: 代码生成中，同质化隐藏并放大相关错误，造成系统性漏洞；招聘中，有偏智能体的影响在其移除后仍长期存在；同行评审中，不同研究领域间形成不均衡的评估标准。简单的多样性提升方法未能降低同质化风险。

Conclusion: 同质化是MAS的一种失效模式，MAS评估不能只看总体性能，必须分析交互动态；需要有效利用智能体多样性的策略。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) leverage interactions between agents to perform complex tasks. Despite their success, we show that these interactions can also lead to homogenization, i.e., agents converging to similar behaviors. Homogenization in MAS can reduce agent diversity and reinforce shared failures. In this paper, we operationalize homogenization using three metrics: conformity to the majority, polarization towards extremes, and growing inertia against changes over subsequent interactions. We evaluate homogenization in MAS for code generation, hiring, and scientific peer review. Across these tasks, we show that homogenization translates to concrete downstream risks: in code generation, it hides and amplifies correlated errors which can create systemic vulnerabilities; in hiring, it allows the influence of biased agents to persist long after their removal; and in peer review, it creates uneven evaluation standards across research areas. Our results establish homogenization as a failure mode of MAS, demonstrating that MAS evaluations must move beyond aggregate performance to carefully analyze interaction dynamics. Finally, we show that simple approaches to increase diversity---leveraging sampling stochasticity and mixed-models MAS---fail to reduce homogenization risks, highlighting the need for strategies to effectively leverage agent diversity.

</details>


### [256] [Dual-QK: Sharp Queries and Flat Keys for Prunable 2-bit KV Caches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09827)
*Sunjoo Whang,Jungjun Oh,Minsung Kim,Dongho Seo,Jisu Shin,Gregory Kielian,Hoi-Jun Yoo,Sangjin Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Dual-QK 通过配对非正交的 query/key 变换，缓解旋转量化导致的 query 能量分散问题，同时实现 KV 低比特量化与 query 通道剪枝，在长上下文下获得高压缩与高吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长输入与长生成使 KV cache 的存储和访问开销显著上升。低比特量化可降低存储与内存流量，query 通道剪枝可进一步减少 key-cache 读取。但基于旋转的量化需对 query 施加相同的正交变换以保持计算等价，这会分散 query 能量，削弱"少数大分量保留、多数小分量剪枝"的可分离性，形成冲突。

Method: 提出 Dual-QK，采用配对的非正交 query/key 变换。利用校准的 query 与 key 统计量，将部分 key 白化与 query 对齐基相结合：平衡 key 尺度以支持 INT2 量化，同时集中 query 能量以支持动态通道剪枝。另引入 channel-0 保护与 bucket-relative RoPE 以维持长上下文低比特精度。

Result: 在 4 个模型、5 个生成基准及长上下文检索任务上，40% query 通道稀疏度下多数任务精度优于 OSCAR。128K 上下文时，相比未剪枝 BF16 实现 6.8× KV-cache 压缩与约 8.3× KV 读取量减少；SGLang 实现中解码吞吐最高达未剪枝 BF16 的 3.75×。

Conclusion: Dual-QK 证明非正交的 query/key 配对变换可化解旋转量化与 query 剪枝之间的冲突，在保持低比特精度的同时兼顾压缩率与解码效率，为长上下文推理提供有效方案。

Abstract: Long inputs and extended generation increase the storage and access costs of the key-value (KV) cache. Low-bit quantization reduces storage and memory traffic, while query-channel pruning can further reduce key-cache reads. Rotation-based quantization redistributes the energy of key outliers across channels. To maintain computational invariance, the same orthogonal transform must be applied to queries, preserving query-key dot products. However, this rotation can disperse query energy, weakening the separation between a few large components to retain and many small ones to prune. We introduce Dual-QK, which uses paired non-orthogonal query and key transforms to address this conflict. Using calibrated query and key statistics, Dual-QK combines partial key whitening with a query-aligned basis to balance key scales for INT2 quantization and concentrate query energy for dynamic channel pruning. Channel-0 protection and bucket-relative RoPE support low-bit accuracy over long contexts. Experiments on four models across five generative benchmarks and long-context retrieval tasks show improved accuracy over OSCAR on most tasks at 40% query-channel sparsity. At a 128K context, Dual-QK provides $6.8\times$ KV-cache compression and an estimated $8.3\times$ reduction in KV read volume relative to unpruned BF16. Under the evaluated configurations, our SGLang implementation achieves up to $3.75\times$ the decoding throughput of unpruned BF16.

</details>


### [257] [Fully Interpretable Minimal Transformers: From Geometry to Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09838)
*Raneem Mahajne,Toviah Moldwin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个小型 Transformer 框架，通过将嵌入维度和头大小限制为 2，实现内部表示的全二维可视化，并验证“学习到的几何即算法”这一核心主张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Transformer 内部机制复杂、难以直接观察和解释。作者希望通过极简模型设计，使嵌入、Q/K/V 变换、注意力、残差流和决策边界都可直接可视化，从而为可解释性研究提供直观、可操作的实验平台。

Method: 将 Transformer 的嵌入维度与注意力头大小限制为 2，使表示可在 R^2 中完整绘制；在任务中要求模型在数字序列出现“+”运算符时输出最近观察到的偶数；训练后逐步可视化 token 与位置嵌入、Q/K/V 变换、点积注意力矩阵、注意力对 value 的选择，以及表示如何移动到输出层正确预测区域，并配套一组可解释性可视化工具。

Result: 作者能够逐步可视化并解释模型完成任务的完整计算流程，展示点与边界在二维空间中的排列如何对应一个分步算法，包括位置信息编码、注意力形成和值的选择与表示移动。

Conclusion: 该框架表明，极简 Transformer 的学习几何可以被解读为明确的算法过程，为研究“信息几何”如何支撑下一 token 预测提供了教学与实验试验台。

Abstract: We present a framework for building and interpreting minimal transformer models. By constraining a transformer's embedding dimension and head size to 2, we enable full two-dimensional visualization of its internal representations. Embeddings, query/key/value transforms, attention outputs, residual streams, and decision boundaries can all be seen directly. Our central claim is that the learned geometry implies an algorithm; the arrangement of points and boundaries in R^2 can be read as a step-by-step procedure. We train a transformer on a simple task where it must produce the most recently observed even number whenever the '+' operator appears in a sequence of digits. Once trained, we visually walk through every step of the transformer's computation. We show how the model embeds the tokens and their respective positions in the sequence, transforms them via the Q, K, and V matrices, uses the dot product between the Q and K representations to form the attention matrix, and uses the attention matrix to select values that move the representation of each input token to the region of the domain of the output layer that will correctly predict the next token. We introduce a suite of interpretability visualizations that make the algorithmic interpretation of this procedure explicit. Our framework offers a pedagogical and experimental testbed to explore how transformers use informational geometry to implement next-token prediction.

</details>


### [258] [For Those Who Believe in Faithfulness: Optimizing the Area Under Insertion and Deletion Curves for Ranking Relative Feature Importance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09844)
*Bjørn Leth Møller,Bulat Ibragimov,Christian Igel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于忠实性的可微损失函数及随机梯度近似方法，并与神经解释掩码框架结合得到 Ra-NEM，可高效生成准确且鲁棒的归因图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向社会相关任务的机器学习需有效的可解释AI方法来理解模型行为。归因方法（热图）常用插入/删除曲线下面积衡量忠实性，但如何直接优化这一忠实性指标仍是问题。

Method: 从忠实性概念推导目标函数及其梯度近似；建立插入曲线与 top-k 特征选择的联系，构造衡量归因质量的损失函数；通过对损失随机化高效近似梯度；将该损失与神经解释掩码框架结合，得到 Ra-NEM，适用于任意可微模型且不影响模型性能。

Result: 实验表明 Ra-NEM 能稳定、高效地给出准确归因；相比其他算法，归因不仅忠实性更高，在其他 XAI 指标上也表现良好，且推理速度快，适合在线应用。

Conclusion: Ra-NEM 是一种高效、鲁棒、不影响原模型性能的归因方法，兼顾忠实性与推理速度，适合在线场景，代码已开源。

Abstract: The adoption of machine learning for socially relevant tasks requires effective explainable artificial intelligence (XAI) methods to better understand the behavior of machine learning models. Attribution methods are a popular XAI approach in which input-output relationships are characterized by heat maps that reflect the relative importance of input features for a particular prediction. The quality of such maps is often assessed by measuring faithfulness based on the area under insertion and deletion curves, which measures changes in the model output as features are added and removed. In this study, we derive an objective function from this notion of faithfulness and a way to approximate its gradient. We establish the connection between insertion curves and top-$k$ feature selection, which leads to a loss function measuring the quality of attributions. Randomization of the loss allows us to efficiently approximate its gradient. To show the effectiveness of the general approach, we combine the loss function with the neural explanation mask framework. The resulting method, termed Ra-NEM, can be used with any differentiable model without affecting the model's performance. Experiments demonstrate that Ra-NEM provides accurate attributions robustly and efficiently. Compared to other algorithms, the attributions have not only higher faithfulness but also perform well in terms of other XAI metrics. The high inference speed of Ra-NEM makes the method suitable for online applications. The code is available online: https://github.com/baerminator/Ra_Nem

</details>


### [259] [Stream-Based Active Learning with Cooperative Neural Networks for Data-Efficient Partial Inverse Design: An Automotive Glass Run Channel Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09848)
*Agung Nugraha,Hyerin Kwon,Heungjun Im,Gian Antariksa,Jihwan Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CoNN-AL框架，将流式主动学习与带去噪自编码器的协作神经网络结合，用于数据高效的部分逆向设计，并在汽车玻璃导槽数据集上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程逆向设计中，每个带标签训练样本都需昂贵仿真生成，构建大规模数据集既慢又贵；尤其部分逆向设计（仅指定部分设计变量、推断其余变量以达到目标性能）面临标注预算受限的问题。

Method: CoNN-AL在CoNN-DAE基础上加入基于流的主动学习：利用蒙特卡洛dropout估计预测不确定性，实时判断传入候选样本中哪些值得标注，从而将有限标注预算用于最具信息量的设计。

Result: 在超90万条汽车玻璃导槽仿真设计数据集上验证：仅用2万条主动选择标签（约占训练池2.3%），各缺失变量水平下R²达0.967-0.982，接近用更多数据训练的上界模型；在较难水平下比随机采样少用30-40%标签即可达R²≥0.95，在最难水平下是唯一达到R²=0.98的策略。

Conclusion: CoNN-AL实现了数据高效的部分逆向设计，显著降低标注成本，并公开数据集以支持未来数据驱动设计研究。

Abstract: Inverse design in engineering often runs into a simple problem. Each labeled training sample must be produced through expensive simulation, so building a large dataset is slow and costly. This study addresses that problem for partial inverse design, where only some design variables are specified and the rest must be inferred to reach a target performance value. We propose CoNN-AL, a framework for data-efficient partial inverse design that adds stream-based active learning to the Cooperative Neural Network with Denoising Autoencoder (CoNN-DAE). The model estimates predictive uncertainty through Monte Carlo dropout and uses it to decide, in real time, which incoming candidate samples are worth labeling, so the limited labeling budget is spent on the most informative designs. We validate the framework on a real-world automotive glass run channel dataset of more than 900,000 unique simulated designs. With only 20,000 actively selected labels, about 2.3% of the training pool, CoNN-AL reaches R-squared values of 0.967 to 0.982 across all missing-variable levels, approaching the upper-bound models trained on far more data. It reaches R-squared of at least 0.95 with 30 to 40% fewer labels than random sampling at the more difficult missing-variable levels and, at the most challenging level, is the only strategy in this study to reach R-squared of 0.98. Together with this work, we publicly release the dataset to support future research on data-driven design.

</details>


### [260] [MUNITE: Unified Multimodal Latent Inference for Any-to-Any Multimodal Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09866)
*Kyeongmin Yeo,Minhyuk Sung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MUNITE 提出一个隐变量框架，将多模态生成中的编码与隐变量生成统一为同一推断问题，通过条件流模型与自蒸馏训练，实现灵活的任意到任意多模态生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态生成方法通常将编码与生成视为分离任务，难以在任意模态子集输入下灵活生成任意目标模态，且难以从非完整训练样本中学习条件分布。

Method: 将编码和隐变量生成统一为不同观测证据量下的同一推断问题：完整观测对应确定性编码，无观测对应隐变量边缘分布，中间子集对应条件隐变量推断，全部纳入单一条件流模型。共享隐变量样本保证跨生成目标的一致性，模态特定解码器建模各自不确定性。通过自蒸馏扩展条件流匹配，用富证据条件下的预测监督同一模型在更小子集下的预测。

Result: 在 PolyMNIST-D-Q、FFHQ64 以及图像-文本-音频任务上，MUNITE 取得有竞争力或更优的生成质量与源-目标对齐，联合生成一致性更高；在所有一对多及无条件图像-文本-音频对比中获得最高一致性。

Conclusion: 统一隐变量推断能有效支持多样多模态设置下的任意到任意生成，验证了框架的通用性与有效性。

Abstract: We introduce MUNITE, a latent-variable framework for flexible any-to-any multimodal generation that treats encoding and latent generation as the same inference problem under different amounts of observed evidence. Given any subset of modalities, MUNITE models the conditional distribution over the latent representation associated with the complete observation. Full observation recovers deterministic encoding, no observation recovers the latent marginal, and intermediate subsets define conditional latent inference, all within a single conditional flow model. A shared latent sample captures variation that must remain consistent across generated targets, while modality-specific generative decoders model the remaining uncertainty independently. To learn these conditional distributions from incomplete training examples, we extend conditional flow matching through self-distillation: predictions conditioned on richer available observations supervise the same model conditioned on smaller subsets at the same intermediate latent state. When the richer-evidence trajectory follows the exact conditional flow, this provides the same expected learning signal as full-target denoising. Across PolyMNIST-D-Q, FFHQ64, and image-text-audio, MUNITE achieves competitive or better generation quality and source-target alignment, with higher joint-generation coherence. In particular, it attains the highest coherence in all one-to-many and unconditional image-text-audio comparisons, showing the effectiveness of unified latent inference across diverse multimodal settings.

</details>


### [261] [Layerwise Error Attribution for Fast and Robust Mixed-Precision Post-Training Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09877)
*Samy Houache,Yann Traonmilin,Jean-François Aujol*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于逐层概率误差分析的混合精度训练后量化比特分配方法，无需外部求解器，在干净标定下匹配或超越SOTA，在损坏标定下更鲁棒，分配速度提升28倍至2570倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合精度训练后量化需在全局内存预算下逐层分配比特，主要难点是分配问题的组合爆炸性以及标定集小且可能被污染时对噪声的敏感性。因此需要一种计算快速且能在标定数据损坏时保持模型质量的高效分配方法。

Method: 推导量化误差的逐层概率分析，将传播误差与给定层引入的局部扰动分离；利用该局部项构建可分离评分，用于一个无需外部求解器的简单分配算法。方法的概率特性带来对损坏数据的鲁棒性。

Result: 在DRUNet去噪任务上，平均每权重4比特预算下，干净标定时可匹配或改进SOTA混合精度基线；损坏标定下更鲁棒，PSNR增益最高达7.5 dB；比特分配速度相比所研究基线提升28倍至2570倍。在量化扩散模型上，直接应用该框架也改进了SOTA。

Conclusion: 通过概率化的逐层误差分离与可分离评分分配，可在无外部求解器条件下实现高效、鲁棒的混合精度训练后量化，兼顾速度、质量与抗标定污染能力。

Abstract: Mixed-precision post-training quantization is a network compression method that assigns bits layer by layer, under a global memory budget using a small calibration set. The main difficulties are to overcome the combinatorial nature of the allocation problem and to manage the sensitivity to small, potentially corrupted databases. Hence, an efficient allocation method should be fast to compute and preserve model quality when calibration data are corrupted. To design such a method, we derive a layerwise probabilistic analysis of the quantization error that separates propagated error from the local perturbation introduced at a given layer. We use this local term to build a separable score for a simple allocation algorithm, that requires no external solver. The probabilistic nature of our approach brings robustness to corrupted data. On denoising tasks with DRUNet, with an average budget of 4 bits per weight, our method matches or improves state-of-the-art mixed-precision baselines under clean calibration, and is more robust to corrupted calibration, with PSNR gains of up to 7.5 dB under the tested corruptions. Experiments show bit-allocation speed-ups from 28x to 2,570x over the studied baselines. For quantized diffusion models, our experiments show that a direct application of our framework also improves the state-of-the-art.

</details>


### [262] [Identifiability of a dissipative knowledge-dynamics model: exact recovery under designed excitation, degeneration on observational data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09889)
*Arman Kostanian,Armen Beklaryan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将人类学习建模为非线性耗散常微分方程组，证明其参数辨识性定理，并提出高效求解器；在仿真中实现精确恢复，而在真实观测数据中揭示所谓"可解释性"结果实为不可辨识退化现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类学习是耗散动力学过程：掌握随练习累积、随遗忘衰减、并沿相互依赖的概念传播。研究者常希望从数据中估计出具有机制意义的参数（如概念迁移矩阵、遗忘率、练习响应增益），但缺乏对"这些参数究竟能否从数据中恢复"的理论保证。

Method: 1) 建立非线性耗散ODE系统，参数包含概念迁移矩阵（编码先修耦合）、逐概念遗忘率及饱和型练习响应增益；2) 在显式激励条件下证明逆问题的结构可辨识性定理，给出两概念情形的构造性闭式恢复，以及单调性、鲁棒性和L-稳定性结果；3) 为耗散子系统设计半隐式L-稳定格式与批量化求解器（与逐轨迹公式数值等价，预测逐位一致、梯度达1e-10，速度提升两个数量级）；4) 合成实验验证定理，并在大规模观测基准上用四项独立对照检验结果。

Result: 在定理激励条件下，合成恢复是精确的：参数恢复至机器精度，先修结构F1=1.0。但在大规模观测基准上并非如此：一个看似很强的恢复（遗忘率与主题难度Spearman ρ=0.83）被四项独立对照推翻——它能经受破坏数据时间顺序的检验、与经典贝叶斯基线相当、且移除真实时间戳后不受影响。作者将其归因于模型的平稳结构，并指出这正是定理所预测的、在缺乏设计性激励时的退化。

Conclusion: 研究划清了可辨识与不可辨识机制之间的明确边界，并给出针对"可解释性"主张的验证协议，提醒在观测数据上获得的机制参数解释需要经过激励条件检验。

Abstract: Human learning is a dissipative dynamical process: mastery accumulates through practice, decays through forgetting, and propagates across interdependent concepts. We model it as a nonlinear dissipative system of ordinary differential equations whose parameters are mechanistically meaningful (a concept-transfer matrix encoding prerequisite coupling, per-concept forgetting rates, and a saturating practice-response gain), and we study when those parameters can actually be recovered from data. We prove a structural identifiability theorem for the associated inverse problem under explicit excitation conditions, with constructive closed-form recovery for the two-concept case, together with monotonicity, robustness and L-stability results. We derive a semi-implicit L-stable scheme for the dissipative subsystem and a batched solver numerically equivalent to the per-trajectory formulation (bit-exact predictions, gradients to $10^{-10}$) yet two orders of magnitude faster, making estimation feasible on cohorts of $10^5$ learners. The empirical study is two-sided. Under the theorem's excitation conditions, synthetic recovery is exact: parameters to machine precision, prerequisite structure at $F_1 = 1.0$. On large observational benchmarks it is not. An apparently strong recovery, with forgetting rates correlating with topic difficulty at Spearman $ρ= 0.83$, is refuted by four independent controls: it survives destroying the temporal order of the data, is matched by a classical Bayesian baseline, and is unaffected by removing real timestamps. We trace this to the stationary structure of the model and show that it is the degeneration the theorem predicts in the absence of designed excitation. The result delineates a sharp boundary between identifiable and unidentifiable regimes and yields a validation protocol for interpretability claims.

</details>


### [263] [RollVerify: Bridging Efficiency and Accuracy in Long-Tail Rollout Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09914)
*Yongqiang Yao,Jinru Tan,Kaihuan Liang,Zixin Yin,Yazhe Niu,Ruihao Gong,Dahua Lin,Ningyi Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RollVerify框架，在部分展开基础上主动验证并修复样本，通过OPS度量约束缓解off-policy问题，实现接近on-policy的精度并降低训练成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习对提升大语言模型推理与泛化至关重要，但依赖大量且随上下文增长而长尾化的rollouts。在on-policy训练中，长尾 rollout 造成 GPU 气泡，降低系统利用率并限制 RL 扩展性。异步或部分展开方法虽提升吞吐，却引入过时 off-policy 样本，可能损害最终精度。现有方法多在训练时重加权样本，仍与完全 on-policy 训练存在性能差距。

Method: 提出RollVerify，一个基于部分展开的轻量级 RL 框架，在样本进入训练前主动验证与修复。引入 off-policy shift 度量 OPS 量化部分生成轨迹的 off-policy 偏差；在 OPS 约束引导下，执行序列级与 token 级验证，识别并截断轨迹中的无效后缀，从而产出高质量样本。

Result: 在数学推理与工具辅助数学推理任务上，RollVerify 在降低训练成本的同时达到与 on-policy 训练相当的精度；额外的代码生成结果提供了超越数学领域的初步证据。

Conclusion: 相比被动重加权，主动验证与修复部分展开样本能在保留部分展开效率优势的同时保护模型精度，为可扩展的 RL 训练提供有效方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is crucial for improving large language models' reasoning and generalization. It relies on massive rollouts whose lengths become increasingly long-tailed as context windows grow. In on-policy training, these long-tail rollouts can result in GPU bubbles, reducing system utilization and limiting RL scalability. Asynchronous or partial-rollout methods improve throughput by relaxing synchronization, but inevitably introduce stale off-policy samples (trajectories) that may hurt final accuracy. Existing approaches mainly mitigate this off-policy issue by reweighting off-policy samples during training, yet they can still leave a performance gap compared to fully on-policy training. In this work, rather than passively reweighting samples during training, we propose RollVerify, a lightweight RL framework built on partial rollout that actively verifies and repairs samples before they enter training. Specifically, it introduces an off-policy shift metric OPS, to quantify the off-policy deviation of partially generated trajectories. Guided by the OPS constraint, RollVerify performs both sequence-level and token-level verification to identify and truncate invalid suffixes of trajectories. This yields high-quality samples that protect the models' accuracy while preserving the efficiency gains of partial rollout. Experiments on mathematical and tool-assisted mathematical reasoning show that RollVerify achieves accuracy comparable to on-policy training while reducing training cost. Additional code-generation results provide preliminary evidence beyond mathematics.

</details>


### [264] [NeuralZip: Reusable Setup for Fast Lossless Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09916)
*Martín Bravo,Samuel Horváth,Gonzalo Navarro,Andrés Abeliuk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NeuralZip 通过一次性的指数统计结构复用（分组共享 Huffman 码 + 打包指数元组），实现无损压缩的快速化与可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无损压缩可在不改变浮点值的前提下减少模型权重的存储与传输，但重复的统计分析和编码构造带来额外计算开销。论文研究能否将指数统计结构一次性准备并复用。

Method: 将指数分布相似的块分组，共享 Huffman 码；对反复出现的指数元组采用打包指数进行选择性表示。该表示由一次性 setup 阶段选定，后续每次编码仍处理当前张量值；setup 还可从另一兼容架构预计算并迁移。

Result: 在浮点模型检查点上，setup 后的压缩比基线快 1.81–21.33×，且实现逐比特精确重建；setup 可预计算并跨架构迁移，保持相近压缩比并免去摊销成本；训练检查点显示权重演化过程中持续复用；GPU 实验将活跃显存最高降低 27.5%，同时精确复现 logits。

Conclusion: 压缩自适应是可迁移、可复用的，为模型权重的无损压缩提供了低开销且可跨架构迁移的方案。

Abstract: Lossless compression can reduce the storage and movement of model weights without changing their floating-point values, but repeated statistical analysis and code construction add computational overhead. We study whether the statistical structure of exponents can be prepared once and reused. For this, we introduce NeuralZip, which groups chunks with similar exponent distributions, shares Huffman codes, and selectively represents recurring exponent tuples using packed exponents, thereby achieving additional moderate compression ratios. A setup chooses these representations before subsequent encodings, while every encoding still processes the current tensor values. In floating-point model checkpoints, post-setup compression is 1.81-21.33$\times$ faster than the baselines and achieves exact bit-to-bit reconstruction. We show that this setup can be precomputed and transferred from another compatible architecture, preserving similar compression ratios and avoiding the need to amortize setup costs. Therefore, compression adaptation is transferable and reusable. Training checkpoints demonstrate continued reuse as the weights evolve. Finally, GPU experiments reduce active memory usage by up to 27.5$\%$ while reproducing the logits exactly.

</details>


### [265] [Eigenvalues of the Hessian in Deep Learning: The Origin of Symmetry and Its Breaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09919)
*Yossi Arjevani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一框架解释深度模型训练后Hessian谱的普遍结构：大块近零特征值与少数孤立离群值，其根源在于原模型是对某个隐藏的高度对称参考配置的偏离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习训练模型的Hessian谱呈现稳定模式（近零大块+少数离群值），但缺乏统一机理解释。

Method: 将原模型视为对邻近隐藏高对称参考配置的偏离；通过改变架构、数据分布或参数度量暴露该参考配置，利用其丰富不变性精确刻画谱结构，再破坏对称性得到原配置的谱。在三层ReLU网络上详细分析，并推广到卷积、图、Transformer模型及NTK。

Result: 对称参考配置强制产生高维核和大重数特征值；回到原配置后对称性破缺，形成所观测的簇与离群值层级结构。该机制同样出现在逐层Hessian和Gauss-Newton矩阵中。

Conclusion: Hessian谱的簇与离群值结构可由隐藏高对称参考配置的对称性破缺统一解释，具有跨架构与跨矩阵的普适性。

Abstract: Hessian spectra at trained models in deep learning exhibit a persistent pattern: eigenvalues organize into distinct clusters, including a large bulk near zero and a few isolated outliers. This paper shows that a natural account of these spectral phenomena emerges when the original setting is understood as a departure from a nearby, otherwise hidden, highly symmetric reference.
  Modifications, including changes to the architecture, data distribution, or parameter metric, expose a nearby reference configuration whose Hessian exhibits rich invariances-ones not accounted for by weight symmetries. There, symmetry enables a precise description of the spectra, forcing high-dimensional kernels and eigenvalues of large multiplicity. Returning to the original configuration breaks the Hessian symmetry and thereby produces the observed hierarchy of clusters and outliers.
  The framework is developed in some generality, with a detailed analysis of three-layer ReLU networks and applications to convolutional, graph, and transformer models, as well as to the NTK. The same mechanism is further shown to yield analogous spectral structures in layerwise Hessians and the Gauss-Newton matrix.

</details>


### [266] [KGATE : a Knowledge Graph Embedding Training Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09927)
*Benjamin Loire,Galadriel Brière,Célia Brahimi,Antoine Toffano,Anaïs Baudot*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KGATE是一个模块化Python库，基于PyTorch Geometric和TorchKGE，用于训练知识图谱自编码器（KGE），支持灵活组装组件、防止数据泄漏、内置训练流程并保证可复现性；基准测试显示其训练速度与最快库相当，同时功能更全面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识图谱嵌入（KGE）模型多采用自编码器架构，常需同时使用编码器与解码器组件，但现有库很少支持完整自编码器，且往往缺乏维护、依赖未文档化的默认超参数，导致不同库间结果无法比较。因此需要一个模块化、可复现、功能完整的训练环境。

Method: 提出KGATE（Knowledge Graph Autoencoder Training Environment），一个基于PyTorch Geometric和TorchKGE的模块化Python库。用户可将初始化器、编码器、解码器、损失函数、负采样器和评估指标作为构建块进行组装，或插入自定义模块。KGATE还包含控制数据泄漏的预处理流程、内置训练管线，并以可复现性为设计原则。

Result: 与六个现有KGE库进行基准对比，KGATE的训练时间与最快的库相当，同时提供更广泛的功能集。

Conclusion: KGATE通过模块化设计和完整自编码器支持，解决了现有KGE库功能不全、难以比较和可复现性差的问题，在保持竞争力的训练速度的同时提供更丰富的功能。

Abstract: Knowledge graph embedding (KGE) models encode the entities and relations of a knowledge graph into a low-dimensional latent space, enabling tasks such as classification or link prediction. Most KGE models follow an autoencoder architecture, in which an encoder projects the knowledge graph into the latent space and a decoder reconstruct it. Combining both encoder and decoder components is increasingly needed, yet existing libraries rarely support complete autoencoders, are often unmaintained, rely on undocumented default hyperparameters, and produce results that cannot be compared across libraries. Here we present KGATE (Knowledge Graph Autoencoder Training Environment), a modular Python library built on PyTorch Geometric and TorchKGE. KGATE lets users assemble initializers, encoders, decoders, losses, negative samplers, and evaluation metrics as building blocks, or plug in their own block. KGATE includes a preprocessing procedure that controls data leakage, a builtin training pipeline, and reproducibility by design. Benchmarks against six existing KGE libraries show that KGATE training time is comparable with the fastest libraries while offering a broader set of features.

</details>


### [267] [Expected Sample Complexity in Multi-Armed Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09929)
*Nadav Sukenik,Nadav Merlis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究随机多臂老虎机问题的期望样本复杂度，提出"期望近似正确"(ACE)框架，分析已知与未知ε两种设定下的算法及下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 样本复杂度是序贯决策中常用的度量，即智能体与环境交互期间次优决策的数量。作者希望研究随机多臂老虎机问题的样本复杂度，并引入期望样本复杂度这一性能度量，以弥补现有框架中仅提供高概率保证的不足。

Method: 提出新的ACE（approximately correct in expectation，期望近似正确）框架来分析期望样本复杂度。证明ACE保证可推出几乎必然收敛到最优期望奖励，并给出将ACE保证转化为显式期望遗憾界的方法。针对已知容许次优水平ε的情形，设计explore-then-ε-greedy算法；针对ε未知的情形，分析Thompson采样的期望样本复杂度。最后为两种设定建立近乎匹配的下界。

Result: ACE保证蕴含几乎必然收敛到最优期望奖励；可将ACE保证转化为显式期望遗憾界；与现有度量不同，确定性算法无法获得有利的ACE界；两种设定下的算法在ε上都是紧的，并证明了两种情形之间存在性能差异（分离）。

Conclusion: ACE框架为分析期望样本复杂度提供了新视角，揭示了确定性算法的局限，并刻画了ε已知与未知两种设定间的性能分离，为该问题提供了紧的上下界。

Abstract: Sample complexity is a widely used metric in sequential decision-making problems, defined as the number of suboptimal decisions during the interaction between the agent and an environment. We study the sample complexity of stochastic multi-armed bandit problems and introduce the expected sample complexity performance measure, analyzing it in a novel framework called approximately correct in expectation (ACE). We show that ACE guarantees imply almost sure convergence to the optimal expected reward, in contrast to high-probability guarantees found in other frameworks, and also show how to convert ACE guarantees into explicit expected regret bounds. We further show that, in contrast to existing measures, deterministic algorithms cannot obtain favorable ACE bounds, and analyze stochastic algorithms in two settings: when the allowed suboptimality level $ε$ is known to the algorithm and when it is unknown. In the former, we devise an explore-then-$ε$-greedy algorithm, and in the latter, we analyze the expected sample complexity of Thompson sampling. Finally, we establish nearly matching lower bounds for both settings, showing that the algorithms are tight in $ε$ and proving a performance separation between the two regimes.

</details>


### [268] [Learning Traffic Flow Dynamics with Stochastic Physics-Informed Neural Cellular Automata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09946)
*Federica Bragone,Matthieu Barreau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出物理信息神经网络元胞自动机（PI-NCA），用于数据驱动的交通流建模，在保证车辆数守恒与道路拓扑物理一致性的前提下学习局部转移规则，并扩展到随机动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通流建模对理解车辆集体动力学至关重要，元胞自动机虽简单可解释且能重现宏观交通现象，但从数据中学习局部转移规则并保持物理约束（尤其是随机模型）仍然困难。

Method: 在标准神经元胞自动机（NCA）基础上，设计一种与道路拓扑物理一致、且保证车辆总数守恒的神经架构，从而将学习到的转移规则约束在物理可行范围内；并进一步通过参数化概率转移规则将该框架扩展到随机动力学，同时保留相同的物理约束。

Result: 在由经典 Nagel-Schreckenberg 和 Kerner-Klenov-Wolf 元胞自动机生成的多种交通场景上进行评估，PI-NCA 成功学习了两类交通模型的动力学，并持续优于标准 NCA；随机扩展也能在不破坏物理约束的情况下捕捉概率转移规则。

Conclusion: PI-NCA 为数据驱动的交通流建模提供了一种兼具物理一致性与可解释性的框架，能够有效学习确定性与随机交通动力学，并保持车辆守恒等物理约束。

Abstract: Traffic flow modeling is essential for understanding and predicting the collective dynamics of vehicles on road networks. Cellular automata provide a simple, interpretable yet powerful framework for representing these dynamics via local interaction rules, while retaining the ability to reproduce complex macroscopic traffic phenomena. However, learning local transition rules from data while preserving physically meaningful constraints remains challenging, particularly for stochastic models. In this work, we propose a physics-informed neural cellular automaton (PI-NCA) for data-driven traffic flow modeling. Building on the standard neural cellular automaton (NCA), we design a neural architecture that is physically consistent with the road topology and guarantees conservation of the total number of vehicles, thereby constraining the learned transition rules to physically admissible dynamics. We further extend this framework to stochastic dynamics by parameterizing probabilistic transition rules while preserving the same physics-informed constraints. We evaluate the proposed models on multiple traffic scenarios generated by the well-established Nagel-Schreckenberg and Kerner-Klenov-Wolf cellular automata. The results demonstrate that the PI-NCA successfully learns the dynamics of both traffic models and consistently outperforms a standard NCA, while the stochastic extension captures probabilistic transition rules without compromising the imposed physical constraints.

</details>


### [269] [Force without transmission: a depth-induced rank collapse that no loss on the representation reopens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09958)
*Martin Hofmann,Patrick Mäder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了训练中Transformer的秩崩塌现象，发现修复崩塌的关键在于梯度路径而非添加损失项的强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文试图验证一个在注意力崩塌中被证明有效的有界纠正损失项，是否能同样修复因训练导致Transformer出现的秩崩塌(rank collapse)问题。

Method: 通过削弱跳跃连接(skip connection)使小型Transformer发生秩崩塌，并对崩塌网络的多份副本施加附加损失项进行实验；分析任务梯度是否到达query和key权重，以及附加损失项梯度在各block中的衰减情况。

Result: 没有任何附加损失项能修复崩塌，即使最强的一项推力约为任务梯度的十分之一。原因是路径问题：任务梯度无法再到达query和key权重，附加项的梯度在崩塌形成的block之前就已衰减。恢复跳跃连接（不改变任何权重）可立即重新打通该路径，秩随即恢复，但恢复尺度远高于崩塌尺度。在高学习率爆发后，路径保持开放，秩在未经处理的情况下恢复。论文的路径预测能对恢复时间排序，但未能迁移到这种成因。所有情况下，秩恢复后损失仍高于健康网络。

Conclusion: 一个崩塌的网络能否被修复，取决于梯度是否仍能到达需要改变的权重，而不取决于损失项的推动强度。

Abstract: Training can drive a transformer into a rank collapse: all token representations point in one direction, and learning stops. In a related collapse of attention, a loss term with a bounded corrective force repairs the network during the run. We ask whether such a term repairs rank collapse. We collapse small transformers by weakening their skip connection and treat copies of the collapsed network. No added loss term repaired the collapse, although the stronger kind pushed with about a tenth of the task gradient. The reason was the path, not the strength. The task gradient no longer reached the query and key weights, which decide where attention looks, and the added term's gradient faded before the blocks where the collapse forms. Restoring the skip connection, which changes no weight, reopened this path at once. The rank then recovered, but only far above the scale of collapse. After a burst of high learning rate the path stayed open and the rank recovered untreated. Registered predictions from the path ranked recovery times but did not transfer to this cause. In every case the loss stayed above that of a healthy network after the rank recovered. Whether a collapsed network can be repaired depends on whether the gradient still reaches the weights that must change, not on how strongly a loss term pushes.

</details>


### [270] [TR-PTQ: High-Accuracy Integer-Only Transformer Post Training Quantization via Taylor Region Reformulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09969)
*Eliyahu Levy,Adam Teman,Yoni Pugachov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TR-PTQ，一种全整数的训练后量化框架，通过共享的Taylor Region指数/对数原语，将除法、开方等昂贵运算转换到对数域用整数运算完成，并识别出归一化层缩放参数与GELU近似是主要误差来源，SoftMax本身鲁棒，从而在视觉与语言任务上实现<1.5%的精度下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer中的非线性层使其难以被后训练量化（PTQ）。现有方法通常把精度损失归因于数值精度不足，因而不得不回退到浮点计算。作者认为真正原因是特定的结构性误差来源，需要重新定位误差瓶颈并消除浮点回退需求。

Method: 1) 分析误差来源：发现归一化层中的可学习缩放参数、GELU的复合近似是主要误差贡献者，而SoftMax对激进量化本身鲁棒。2) 提出统一的整数形式TR-PTQ，使用共享的Taylor Region（TR）指数与对数原语，将除法、平方根等运算在对数域通过标准整数算术完成。3) 针对LayerNorm参数设计免校准、感知离群值的优化方法。

Result: 方法无需浮点硬件单元即可处理非线性运算，在视觉和语言基准上实现小于1.5%的绝对精度下降。

Conclusion: 通过识别结构性误差源并采用共享Taylor Region对数域整数运算与免校准LayerNorm优化，TR-PTQ实现了完全整数的Transformer后训练量化，去除了非线性部分的浮点依赖，同时保持较高精度。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) enables efficient deployment, yet transformer architectures remain challenging to quantize due to nonlinear layers. While existing methods attribute accuracy loss to insufficient numerical precision, often necessitating floating-point fallbacks, we demonstrate that degradation is actually driven by specific structural error sources. We find that learned scale parameters in normalization layers and compounded approximations in GELU are the primary error contributors, whereas SoftMax remains inherently robust to aggressive quantization. To address these bottlenecks, we introduce TR-PTQ, a unified integer-only formulation using shared Taylor Region (TR) exponential and logarithm primitives. This approach allows computationally expensive operations, including division and square roots, to be performed entirely in the log-domain via standard integer arithmetic. Combined with a calibration-free, outlier-aware optimization for LayerNorm parameters, our method eliminates the need for floating-point hardware units for nonlinearities, achieving less than 1.5\% absolute accuracy degradation across vision and language benchmarks.

</details>


### [271] [Efficient Patch-Based Anomaly Detection Fused with Diffusion Driven Generative Modeling for Semiconductor Wafer Bin Map Open Set Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09993)
*Limon Bin Hossain,Md Sadib Rahman Ananta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EfficientAD与DDPM混合的单类晶圆异常检测框架，在WM-38K上融合检测器AUROC达0.9985且误分类降至618，但固定权重融合仅恢复少量可用校正，需样本自适应融合与不平衡感知评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶圆图缺陷识别面临两大限制：监督分类器只能识别训练中见过的缺陷类型；单一机制的单类检测器往往只能捕捉局部结构偏差或全局分布异常，难以兼顾。因此需要结合两类机制的单类框架以提升未知缺陷的检出能力。

Method: 将基于图块的EfficientAD学生-教师检测器与用于部分扩散重建的DDPM耦合，对两者的百分位校准分数采用固定凸组合进行融合。仅使用WM-38K混合类型数据集中的700片正常晶圆训练，在18,658片留出晶圆上评估。

Result: 融合检测器AUROC为0.9985，误分类从DDPM的852和EfficientAD的1,412降至618，所有成对差异p<0.001。增益来自两模块错误弱重叠，但固定权重融合仅恢复oracle选择器可获得校正的40–70%。在基准的倒置类平衡下，AP与F1饱和，而MCC与NPV揭示正常预测不可靠。像素级图表明图像级可分性强并不意味着空间定位准确，扩散模块的作用是局部密度先验而非全局几何推理。

Conclusion: 混合单类框架有效提升晶圆异常检测，但固定权重融合受限，未来应发展样本自适应融合与不平衡感知的评估方法。

Abstract: Spatial defect signatures on wafer bin maps (WBMs) trace yield loss to specific process faults, yet supervised classifiers recognize only the defect types seen during training, and one-class detectors built on a single mechanism tend to capture either local structural deviations or global distributional violations, but rarely both. This work proposes a hybrid one-class framework that couples a patch-based student-teacher detector (EfficientAD) with a denoising diffusion probabilistic model (DDPM) used for partial-diffusion reconstruction, and fuses their percentile-calibrated scores through a fixed convex combination. Trained on only 700 normal wafers from the WM-38K mixed-type dataset and evaluated on 18,658 held-out wafers, the fused detector reached an AUROC of 0.9985 and reduced misclassifications from 852 (DDPM) and 1,412 (EfficientAD) to 618, with all pairwise differences significant at p < 0.001. Beyond aggregate accuracy, the analysis shows that the gain arises from weakly overlapping errors between the two modules, yet fixed-weight fusion recovers only 40-70% of the correction available to an oracle selector. Under the benchmark's inverted class balance, average precision and F1 saturate, while the Matthews correlation coefficient and negative predictive value expose unreliable normal predictions. Pixel-level maps further show that strong image-level separability does not imply spatial localization, and the diffusion module succeeds as a local density prior rather than through global geometric reasoning. These findings motivate sample-adaptive fusion and imbalance-aware evaluation of hybrid wafer anomaly detectors.

</details>


### [272] [Temporal Predictive Multiplicity: Equally Accurate Time Series Models Yield Different Forecast Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09994)
*Emanuele Albini,Francesca Toni,Saumitra Mishra,Francesco Leofante*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"时序预测多重性"框架，刻画性能相近模型在完整预测轨迹上的分歧，并证明仅约束性能或分层约束都无法消除轨迹层面的多重性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具有近乎相同预测性能的模型可能产生显著不同的预测，此现象被称为预测多重性。既有研究多停留在个体标量输出层面；但在时序预测中，各时间步的预测共同构成一条轨迹，逐时间步比较会掩盖预测行为的重要差异。

Method: 提出"时序预测多重性"（temporal predictive multiplicity）框架，用于刻画性能近乎相同的模型在完整预测轨迹上的分歧程度；并通过理论分析考察仅约束预测性能、以及在单个时间步上约束多重性对轨迹层面多重性的影响；随后在19种神经预测架构与11个数据集上进行实验验证。

Result: 理论分析表明，仅约束预测性能仍可允许大量不同轨迹存在；在单个时间步约束多重性只能部分降低、而不能消除轨迹层面的多重性。实验显示，近乎最优的模型在预测轨迹上表现出显著差异，且轨迹层面的分歧与逐时间步的分歧基本无关。

Conclusion: 该框架揭示了现有预测多重性研究的空白：预测性能无法区分的模型暗含着根本不同的时序轨迹，并可能带来重要的下游影响。

Abstract: Models with near-identical predictive performance can yield substantially different predictions, a phenomenon known as predictive multiplicity. Prior work has mostly studied this at the level of individual scalar outputs. In time-series forecasting, however, predictions across horizons jointly define a trajectory, and horizon-wise comparisons can hide important differences in predictive behavior. To address this problem, we introduce temporal predictive multiplicity, a framework that characterizes disagreement over complete forecast trajectories among models with near-identical predictive performance. We show that constraining predictive performance alone can still admit a broad range of different trajectories. We further show that constraining multiplicity at individual horizons partially reduces, but does not eliminate, trajectory-level multiplicity. Experiments with 19 neural forecasting architectures on 11 datasets confirm that near-optimal models can exhibit substantial variability in the forecast trajectories they produce, and trajectory-level disagreement is largely unrelated to horizon-wise disagreement. Our framework, therefore, exposes a gap in existing multiplicity studies: models with indistinguishable predictive performance imply fundamentally different temporal trajectories, with consequential downstream effects.

</details>


### [273] [Beyond Reward Suppression: Near-Optimal Offline Attacks on Warm-Start Bandits with Bounded Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10000)
*Qirun Zeng,Manhin Poon,Xiangxiang Dai,Qixin Zhang,Jinhang Zuo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究warm-start bandit的有限离线攻击，揭示"目标推广"（直接提升目标臂）并非启发式，而是最优攻击的必要组成部分，并设计了达到最优次线性成本的攻击方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对bandit的对抗攻击通常通过压制非目标臂来诱导学习器选择目标臂，但在现实中（如虚假评论）操纵往往是直接推广目标项。这一理论与实践的差距是本文的研究动机。

Method: 研究warm-start bandits上的有界离线攻击：攻击者在部署前只能向预热历史注入合法的"动作-奖励"对。理论上分析攻击成本分配，并设计针对UCB的最优攻击；进一步扩展到Thompson Sampling、ε-greedy及更广泛的bandit算法。

Result: 证明了当目标臂接近奖励下界时，任何使UCB在几乎所有在线轮次被选中的阶最优成本攻击，都必须将不可忽略比例的成本分配给目标臂；设计的攻击达到最优次线性成本，并刻画了其在目标推广与非目标压制间的成本分配。在真实与合成数据上的实验验证了攻击的有效性。

Conclusion: 目标推广在warm-start bandit攻击中具有理论必要性，最优攻击需要在目标推广与非目标压制之间进行成本分配；所提攻击对多种bandit算法有效且成本最优。

Abstract: Adversarial attacks on bandits aim to mislead a learner toward a target arm while keeping the attack cost small. Existing attacks typically achieve this by suppressing non-target arms. In practice, however, manipulation such as fake reviews often directly promotes the target item. We study this gap through bounded offline attacks on warm-start bandits, where an attacker can inject only valid action-reward pairs into the warm-start history before deployment. We show that target promotion is not merely a heuristic: when the target arm lies near the lower reward boundary, any order-optimal-cost attack against UCB that makes it selected in nearly all online rounds must allocate a nonvanishing fraction of its cost to the target arm. We then design an attack that achieves the optimal sublinear cost and characterize its allocation between target promotion and non-target suppression. We further extend the attack to Thompson Sampling, $ε$-greedy, and a broader class of bandit algorithms. Experiments on real-world and synthetic data validate the effectiveness of our attacks.

</details>


### [274] [A Drosophila Whole-Connectome Network Can Learn Human-Designed Cognitive Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10014)
*Joonghui Cho,Minchan Kang,Daeshik Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 利用雄性果蝇完整中枢神经系统连接组(MaleCNS v1.0)作为固定循环拓扑，每条解剖边学习一个标量，即可同时在有界加法与接地的关系语言任务上获得优于度保持重连基线的性能，表明生物接线图可作为可复用的归纳偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者试图回答一个核心问题：生物接线图能否在其进化所对应的行为之外，充当有用的计算基底。即检验真实神经连接结构是否含有可迁移、可复用的计算归纳偏置。

Method: 以公开的成年雄性果蝇MaleCNS v1.0连接组作为人工网络的固定循环拓扑；对两条任务分别训练独立模型——有界加法任务，以及基于固定100词词表的受控接地关系语言任务；两模型中每条解剖边仅学习一个标量参数；对照基线为保持有向度分布的重连图，并设置多个固定接口与21个图的固定比较。

Result: 有界加法留出集平均准确率92.77%，度保持重连为67.93%；严格配对语言端点上，四个固定接口平均61.59%，匹配重连为44.17%；在规范接口的21图固定比较中排名第一；在匹配的48组干预子集上，打乱任务定义的感官特征使分数从60.94%降至19.27%。

Conclusion: MaleCNS的高阶接线结构为有界加法与接地关系语言提供了一种可复用的归纳偏置，说明生物连接组可超越其进化功能，作为通用性计算基底使用。

Abstract: Can a biological wiring diagram serve as a useful computational substrate beyond the behaviors for which it evolved? We use the publicly released MaleCNS v1.0 connectome, reconstructed from a single adult male Drosophila specimen, as the fixed recurrent topology of an artificial network. We train separate models for bounded addition and for a controlled grounded relational language task built from a fixed 100-word lexicon. In both models, one scalar is learned per anatomical edge. The anatomical graph reaches 92.77% mean accuracy on held-out addition, compared with 67.93% for directed degree-preserving rewires. On the strict paired language endpoint, which matches original and order-reversed scenes to their corresponding descriptions, it reaches 61.59% across four fixed interfaces, compared with 44.17% for matched rewires. At the canonical interface, it ranks first in a fixed 21-graph comparison. On the matched 48-group intervention subset, shuffling task-defined sensory features reduces its score from 60.94% to 19.27%. Together, these results show that higher-order MaleCNS wiring provides a reusable inductive bias for bounded addition and grounded relational language.

</details>


### [275] [Oscillatory Neural Dynamics over Sheaves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10018)
*Jan-Willem Van Looy,Alessandro Trenta,Alessio Gravina,Alessio Borgi,Ferdinando Zanchetta,Pietro Liò,Davide Bacciu,Rita Fioresi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出ONDA框架，将二阶波动力学与层（sheaf）矩阵值传输结合，实现图神经网络中有效的长程传播，并通过stalk-wise敏感性分析证明跨节点影响不会消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNN中长程传播是核心挑战，增加传播深度并不能保证远距离节点间有效互相影响；sheaf神经网络虽通过矩阵值传输增强图传播，但这种表达能力并不能自动带来有效的长程通信。

Method: 提出ONDA，一种基于算子值信息波的长程图学习框架。stalk值表示通过由学习到的sheaf传输算子驱动的二阶动力学演化，将波状传播与富有表达力的局部几何相结合。通过stalk-wise敏感性分析刻画长程影响。

Result: 在长程传播、严重图瓶颈、图迁移和异配性基准测试中，ONDA持续优于标量波传播、扩散式sheaf基线以及最先进模型。

Conclusion: 跨影响力永不消失，将波动力学与矩阵值传输耦合能带来显著收益，验证了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: Effective long-range propagation remains a central challenge in graph neural networks, as increasing a model's propagation depth does not guarantee that distant nodes effectively influence each other. Sheaf neural networks enrich graph propagation through matrix-valued transport between stalks; still, this expressivity alone does not automatically imply effective long-range communication. We introduce ONDA, a long-range graph learning framework based on operator-valued information waves. Stalk-valued representations evolve through second-order dynamics governed by learned sheaf transport operators, combining wave-like propagation with expressive local geometry. We characterize long-range influence through a stalk-wise sensitivity analysis and show that the cross-influence never vanishes. Across long-range propagation, severe graph bottlenecks, graph transfer, and heterophilic benchmarks, ONDA consistently improves over scalar wave propagation, diffusive sheaf baselines, and state-of-the-art models, demonstrating the benefit of coupling wave dynamics with matrix-valued transport.

</details>


### [276] [Matching of signal, noise and hardware timescales for filtering and forecasting of correlated noise signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10037)
*Joshua Donald,Alex Gabbitas,Arthur G. T. Coveney,Sergey Savel'ev,Pavel Borisov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文利用纳米多孔氧化铌物理储备池，研究噪声相关时间、储备池记忆与预测时域之间的关系，提出储备池记忆时域和预测机制指数，阐明相关噪声是被滤除还是被预测的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理储备池计算虽比传统机器学习更节能，但其固有响应时间尺度是固定的，而真实世界信号往往包含跨多个时间尺度的确定性与随机性成分。因此需要理解噪声时间尺度与储备池记忆、预测时域之间的匹配问题。

Method: 采用纳米多孔氧化铌储备池，结合合成含噪信号与加密货币价格波动数据，分析噪声相关时间、储备池记忆和预测时域三者的关系，并引入储备池记忆时域和预测机制指数来区分不同工作区间。

Result: 变化快于相关储备池记忆和预测时域的噪声会被储备池平均化处理；而变化较慢的噪声，其时间结构足以进行算法预测。由此可区分滤波与预测两种工作区域。

Conclusion: 时间尺度匹配可以指导输入时间序列的编码，并为设计能够跨不同时间尺度滤除、分析和预测随机信号成分的物理储备池架构提供依据。

Abstract: Physical reservoir computing exploits the nonlinear dynamics of physical systems to process time-dependent data with greater energy efficiency than conventional machine learning approaches. However, physical reservoirs have fixed intrinsic response timescales, whereas real-world signals combine deterministic and stochastic components across multiple timescales. Here we show, using a nanoporous niobium oxide reservoir, synthetic noisy signals and cryptocurrency-price volatility, that the relationship among noise correlation time, reservoir memory and forecast horizon determines whether correlated noise is filtered or predicted. Noise varying faster than the relevant reservoir memory and forecast horizon is averaged by the reservoir, whereas the temporal structure of slower-varying noise is sufficient for algorithmic forecasting. We introduce the reservoir memory horizon and forecasting regime index to distinguish these operating regimes. These contributions demonstrate that timescale matching can guide the encoding of input time series and development of physical reservoir architectures that filter, analyse and predict stochastic signal components across distinct temporal scales.

</details>


### [277] [WxFM-XL: Adapting Univariate Foundation Models to Multi-Station Weather Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10057)
*Xiao Wang,Changjian Chen,Zhuo Tang,Rongwen Li,Hongwu Liu,Kenli Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出WxFM-XL，将单变量时间序列基础模型适配到多站点天气预报，通过跨站点误差相关先验图与动态融合机制提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单变量时间序列基础模型扩展到多变量场景时主要建模变量间相关性，在多站点天气预报中忽略了两个因素：（1）站点的空间信息；（2）不同站点相对于基础模型的不同误差先验。

Method: WxFM-XL引入跨站点误差相关先验图来捕捉各站点相对于基础模型的误差先验；在此基础上提出动态融合机制，自适应地将空间相关图与误差相关先验图进行融合。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，该模型优于当前最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过引入误差相关先验图与动态融合空间图，可有效将单变量时间序列基础模型适配到多站点天气预报任务并取得更优表现。

Abstract: With the rise of univariate time series foundation models (e.g., Sundial, Timer), initial efforts have been made to extend them to multivariate settings.
