However, these models mainly focus on modeling correlations among variables. When they are applied to multi-station weather forecasting, two important factors are often overlooked: (1) the spatial information of stations, and (2) different error priors of different stations relative to the foundation model. In this paper, we propose WxFM-XL, a model for adapting univariate time series foundation models to multi-station weather forecasting. WxFM-XL introduces a cross-station error correlation prior graph to capture stationwise error priors with respect to the foundation model. Building on this, we further propose a dynamic fusion mechanism that adaptively integrates a spatial correlation graph with the error correlation prior graph. Experiments on multiple datasets demonstrate that our model outperforms state of the art baselines.

</details>


### [278] [Efficient Provably Private Classification with a Tabular Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10068)
*Talal Alrawajfeh,Cristiana Diaconu,Ossi Räisä,Sebastian Rodriguez Beltran,Yuan He,John Bronskill,Richard E. Turner,Antti Honkela*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PrivTab 是一种易用、架构内置隐私机制的表格基础模型，可在中等至强隐私条件下实现准确的差分隐私分类，显著优于基线且大幅缩短拟合时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据广泛用于医学、金融、政府和科学领域的预测与决策，但常含敏感个体信息，需在隐私保护下保持准确预测。传统隐私学习虽提供正式隐私保证，但需针对数据集缓慢优化、在强隐私下效用损失大且难以正确应用；现有表格基础模型虽能快速适应新数据集，却缺乏正式隐私保证且易受成员推断攻击，限制了其在敏感数据上的使用。

Method: 提出 PrivTab，一种将隐私机制嵌入架构的易用差分隐私分类表格基础模型。其在模拟数据集上预训练，利用上下文学习将敏感行转化为紧凑、可证明私密的摘要，本质上是"在隐私下学习如何学习"。

Result: 在中等至强隐私条件下，PrivTab 优于隐私线性模型与神经网络基线；成员泄漏可忽略；在强隐私下仍保持良好校准的预测；数据集拟合时间减少 10,000 倍，仅需一次前向传播。

Conclusion: PrivTab 结合了正式隐私、速度与易用性，将近期 AI 进展带入因敏感个体数据而受限的应用场景。

Abstract: Tabular data underpin prediction and decision-making in medicine, finance, government and science, but often contain sensitive individual-level information, creating a need for accurate prediction while preserving privacy. Traditional private learning provides formal privacy guarantees, but requires slow dataset-specific optimisation, suffers substantial utility loss under strong privacy, and is often difficult to apply correctly. Tabular foundation models adapt rapidly to new datasets, but existing models lack formal privacy guarantees, and are highly vulnerable to membership-inference attacks, limiting their use on sensitive data. Here we introduce PrivTab, an easy to use tabular foundation model for differentially private classification that embeds a privacy mechanism within its architecture. Pretrained on simulated datasets, PrivTab uses in-context learning to transform sensitive rows into compact, provably private summaries---effectively learning how to learn under privacy. PrivTab outperforms private linear and neural-network baselines under moderate-to-strong privacy, shows negligible membership leakage, maintains well-calibrated predictions under strong privacy, and reduces dataset fitting time by 10,000 times, requiring only a single forward pass. By combining formal privacy, speed, and easy of use, PrivTab brings recent advances in AI to applications where sensitive individual-level data have limited their adoption.

</details>


### [279] [Activation-Aware Weight Tensorization: A Calibration-Time Preconditioner for Tensor-Network LLM Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10085)
*Alessandro Beatini,Marco Maronese,Emanuele Rodolà*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AWT方法，在冻结的TT/TTN张量网络压缩求解器前，用激活分布导出的对角缩放对权重矩阵做预条件，仅需输入侧逐元素重缩放，即可在多个大模型上显著缩小压缩后的困惑度与下游任务差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练张量网络压缩（TT/TTN）通常最小化权重空间的Frobenius误差，而非该层激活分布下的函数误差，导致压缩后功能保真度不佳。

Method: AWT（Activation-aware Weight Tensorization）：一种免训练的校准包装器。先用激活导出的对角尺度对每个权重矩阵预条件，再交给不变的TT/TTN求解器分解，部署时仅需输入侧逐元素重缩放。

Result: 在Llama 3.1 8B、Ministral 8B、Qwen2.5 7B上，2-6倍压缩下AWT稳定优于原始TT/TTN：单算子替换缩小WikiText困惑度差距12-35%；Llama后缀多算子替换缩小27-60%；收益可迁移至HellaSwag与ARC-Challenge。此外，稠密全协方差oracle在其自身加权目标上80/81胜出，但对角AWT在53/81情形下取得更好的留出功能保真度。

Conclusion: 对角预条件体现的是鲁棒性与模块性的权衡，而非对角协方差假设；AWT是一种有原则、模块化的预条件器，可在不修改分解求解器的固定TT/TTN压缩流程中提升功能保真度。

Abstract: Post-training tensor-network compression replaces Transformer linear layers with Tensor Train (TT) or Tree Tensor Network (TTN) operators, but standard decompositions minimize weight-space Frobenius error rather than functional error under the layer's activation distribution. We propose Activation-aware Weight Tensorization (AWT), a training-free calibration wrapper that preconditions each weight matrix with a diagonal activation-derived scale before an unchanged TT/TTN solver and deploys the result with only an input-side elementwise rescaling. Across Llama 3.1 8B, Ministral 8B, and Qwen2.5 7B, AWT consistently improves vanilla TT/TTN tensorization at 2-6 times compression: under single-operator replacement, AWT closes 12-35% of the WikiText perplexity gap to the dense baseline across the three model families and 2-6 times compression settings; while under multi-operator Llama suffix replacement it closes 27-60% across attention-group and all-seven-matrix settings. The gains also transfer to downstream HellaSwag and ARC-Challenge evaluations. We further show that diagonal preconditioning is a robustness-modularity tradeoff rather than a diagonal-covariance assumption: a dense full-covariance oracle wins its own weighted objective in 80/81 cases, yet diagonal AWT gives better held-out functional fidelity in 53/81 cases. Together, these results position AWT as a principled, modular preconditioner for improving functional fidelity in fixed TT/TTN compression pipelines without modifying the decomposition solver.

</details>


### [280] [Multi-Agent Coordination via Support-Preserving Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10087)
*Sangmin Lee,Youngju Na,Chanmi Lee,Sung-eui Yoon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现离线多智能体强化学习（MARL）中基于流的教师模型存在训练失败模式，并提出 MoSDOT（模式支撑半离散最优传输）方法来消除该伪影，提升生成策略的多模态建模质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线 MARL 常依赖生成式策略建模多模态联合行为，通常通过在 CTDE 框架下将集中式教师蒸馏为去中心化单步 actor。作者识别出教师训练阶段的一个失败模式：标准基于流的教师将噪声与回放目标独立配对，导致相近噪声样本被路由至冲突的协调模式，教师生成有效模式之间的中间样本。由于蒸馏损失将每个局部 actor 回归到教师输出的条件均值，该误差不被吸收反而传播给学生。作者旨在从教师端消除这一伪影。

Method: 提出 MoSDOT（Mode-Support Semi-Discrete Optimal Transport）：将多模态回放总结为具有指定容量的有限模式支撑集，并使用条件半离散最优传输在教师训练前将每个噪声样本分配给单一模式。此外研究了一种共享随机性变体，在执行时使用共享噪声分量，以揭示严格乘积执行所固有的残余差距。

Result: 在受控诊断实验和离线 MARL 基准上，MoSDOT 改善了端点质量和路由一致性，尤其在表现出多模态联合行为的数据集上效果显著。

Conclusion: 通过在半离散最优传输框架下预先为噪声样本分配单一模式，MoSDOT 消除了教师端产生模式间无效样本的伪影，从而提升离线 MARL 生成式策略的多模态协调建模能力，并揭示严格乘积执行的内在残余差距。

Abstract: Offline MARL increasingly relies on generative policies to model multimodal joint behavior, typically by distilling a centralized teacher into decentralized one-step actors under the CTDE. We identify a failure mode at the teacher training stage: standard flow-based teachers pair noise with replay targets independently, so nearby noise samples can be routed toward conflicting coordination modes. The teacher then produces samples between valid modes, and because the distillation loss regresses each local actor onto the conditional mean of the teacher's output given local input, this error is not absorbed but propagated to the student. To remove this teacher-side artifact, we propose Mode-Support Semi-Discrete Optimal Transport (MoSDOT), which summarizes multimodal replay into a finite mode support with prescribed capacities and uses conditional semi-discrete optimal transport to assign each noise sample to a single mode before teacher training. We additionally study a shared-randomness variant that uses a shared noise component at execution to expose the residual gap intrinsic to strict-product execution. On controlled diagnostics and offline MARL benchmarks, MoSDOT improves endpoint quality and routing consistency, particularly on datasets exhibiting multimodal joint behavior.

</details>


### [281] [CAFE+FNO: Fourier Kernel Generation via Multiplicative Feature Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10105)
*Hyungjoon Juen,Minwoo Shin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 CAFE+FNO，将 Content-Aware Frequency Encoding+（CAFE+）引入傅里叶核生成，通过傅里叶-切比雪夫特征交互提升 FNO 对高频变化的建模能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FNO 通过傅里叶空间核参数化学习 PDE 解算子，但频率截断限制了高频变化的学习。AM-FNO 和 SirenFNO 从谱坐标生成所有网格模式的核，使坐标编码和生成器设计变得重要。近期隐式神经表示（INR）研究提出通过显式特征组合构建频率交互，而非依赖后续 MLP 隐式形成。

Method: CAFE+ 通过并行的仿射分支和 Hadamard 积组合傅里叶-切比雪夫特征，在两个特征族内部及之间形成交互。一个核 MLP 将每个归一化谱坐标的表示映射为复值通道混合矩阵。每层的生成器在所有存储模式间共享，使固定架构下可训练参数量与模式数量无关。

Result: 在五个 PDE 基准上比较了 CAFE+FNO 与现有 FNO 变体，并针对基配置、乘性组合和带宽可学习性进行了消融研究。代码和实验配置见 https://github.com/fabsk101/CAFEPlusFNO.git。

Conclusion: 显式的内容感知频率编码（CAFE+）可有效增强傅里叶核生成，缓解频率截断带来的高频学习限制，且参数量与模式数解耦。

Abstract: The Fourier Neural Operator (FNO) learns solution operators of partial differential equations (PDEs) through Fourier-space kernel parameterization, but frequency truncation can limit the learning of high-frequency variations. AM-FNO and SirenFNO generate kernels for all grid modes from spectral coordinates using shared networks, making coordinate encoding and generator design important. Recent work on implicit neural representations (INRs) has proposed constructing frequency interactions through explicit feature composition rather than relying on subsequent MLPs to form them implicitly. Building on this approach, we propose CAFE+FNO, which incorporates Content-Aware Frequency Encoding+ (CAFE+) into Fourier kernel generation. CAFE+ combines Fourier--Chebyshev features through parallel affine branches and a Hadamard product, forming interactions within and across the two feature families. A kernel MLP maps the resulting representation of each normalized spectral coordinate to a complex channel-mixing matrix. Each layer shares its generator across all stored modes, making the number of trainable parameters independent of the number of modes for a fixed architecture. We compare CAFE+FNO with existing FNO variants on five PDE benchmarks and conduct ablation studies on basis configuration, multiplicative composition, and bandwidth learnability. Code and experimental configurations are available at https://github.com/fabsk101/CAFEPlusFNO.git.

</details>


### [282] [YANchor-4B: Effective Long-Horizon Reasoning in O(N) Time with O(1) Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10118)
*Huishan Ji,Hua Xu,Weiming Zhang,Qirui Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: YANchor-4B是一种通用递归模型，通过将关键记忆保留为"锚点"供后续推理检索，实现O(N)时间生成与O(1)内存下有效长程推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程推理需要访问早期信息且生成成本可控。全历史注意力导致存储和计算不断增长，而递归压缩可能丢失精确细节，因此需要兼顾成本与精度的方案。

Method: 提出YANchor-4B通用递归模型，将关键记忆保留为锚点（ANchors），在后续推理中检索使用；通过多维记忆机制实现O(N)时间生成和O(1)内存。

Result: 在AIME 2024–2026上平均pass@1达82.93%，HMMT达63.64%，显著优于线性时间、常量状态方法（含更大模型）；在H100上批量长生成吞吐量比Transformer和混合基线高数倍；数十个基准测试显示通用能力优越。

Conclusion: YANchor在效率与长程推理能力上均具优势，可作为一种通用且高效的递归模型方案。

Abstract: Long-horizon reasoning demands access to earlier information at a manageable generation cost. Full-history attention incurs growing storage and computation, while recurrent compression can lose precise details. Therefore, we present YANchor-4B, a general-purpose recurrent model that preserves crucial memory as ANchors for retrieval during subsequent reasoning. Beyond $O(N)$-time generation and $O(1)$ memory, YANchor enables effective long-horizon reasoning through its multidimensional memory mechanism. For example, on challenging math problems, it achieves 82.93% mean pass@1 on AIME 2024--2026 and 63.64% on HMMT, substantially outperforming linear-time, constant-state counterparts, including larger models. It also delivers several-fold higher batched long-generation throughput than Transformer and hybrid baselines on H100. Furthermore, evaluations across dozens of benchmarks demonstrate YANchor's superiority in general-purpose capabilities.

</details>


### [283] [What Can a Gaussian Process Design Test](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10122)
*Ivan De Boi,Marnix Van Soom*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出对高斯过程模型检验的"前瞻性"解读：在观测响应之前，设计本身已决定了它能产生何种矛盾（结构性矛盾或先验性不合理），并据此提出用预测功效来选择下一个输入点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GP模型能与数据一致可能有两种原因：假设正确，或所选输入根本无法暴露假设错误。作者希望对这一区别在设计阶段（未观测响应前）就加以刻画，从而判断设计的检验能力。

Method: 对有限特征GP，模型隐含的无噪响应关系恰为核矩阵的零空间；用Gale对偶给出几何解释，将能暴露误差的最小观测组对应为circuits。对其他核，关系变为"软"约束。标准检验结合两类证据：结构性证据（违反关系，随噪声减小而无限增长）与先验性证据（偏离在先验下不太可能）。

Result: 仿真中预测功效与实际拒绝率吻合；按预测功效选择下一输入点，将针对局部差异的功效从0.48提升到0.72（按预测方差选点仅为0.51）；二维网格包含拉丁超立方所缺乏的可加性精确检验。

Conclusion: 检验本身是经典的，贡献在于对该检验的前瞻性解读：在观测响应之前，设计已经决定了它能产生何种矛盾。

Abstract: A Gaussian process (GP) model can agree with the data for two reasons: its assumptions are right, or the chosen inputs could never have shown that they are wrong. The distinction can be checked from the design before any responses are observed. Every model implies relations that its noiseless responses must satisfy at the chosen inputs, such as the middle value lies on the line through its two neighbours. For GPs built from finitely many features, these relations are exactly the null space of the kernel matrix. Gale duality gives them a geometric interpretation, in which each observation has a vector and the smallest groups of observations that can expose an error are the circuits. For other kernels the relations become soft: response patterns may be improbable under the prior rather than algebraically impossible. A standard test then combines two kinds of evidence. Structural evidence comes from a violated relation and grows without limit as the noise falls. Prior-based evidence only says that a departure is improbable under the prior. With all inputs at the two ends of an interval, for example, a GP can reject a straight line against a large curvature, but only because the implied intercept is improbable, never because curvature was seen. In simulations the predicted power matched the observed rejection rates. Choosing the next input by predicted power raised the power against a localised discrepancy from 0.48 to 0.72, against 0.51 when choosing by predictive variance, and a grid in two dimensions contained exact tests of additivity that a Latin hypercube lacked. The test itself is classical. The contribution is the prospective reading of that test: before observing the responses, the design already determines what kind of contradiction it can produce.

</details>


### [284] [m-Set Adversarial Bandits with Winner Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10128)
*Nicolò Cesa-Bianchi,Matteo Papini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究 $m$-set 对抗性老虎机在不同效用和反馈模型下的遗憾上下界，揭示设置变化对学习率的巨大影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望理解在 $m$-set 对抗性老虎机中，当效用函数（赢家奖励或奖励之和）和反馈模型（赢家索引、赢家奖励、奖励之和及其组合）发生变化时，学习速率会如何变化。通过与组合老虎机和 MNL 老虎机的标准界进行比较，揭示细微设置变化对学习率的显著影响。

Method: 作者针对不同的效用和反馈模型，推导了 $m$-set 对抗性老虎机的遗憾上界和下界。主要技术贡献是基于信息论的下界分析。并在合成数据上进行了实验验证。

Result: 得到了不同效用与反馈模型下的遗憾上下界，并通过与组合老虎机和 MNL 老虎机的标准界对比，展示了设置变化对学习率的显著影响。合成数据实验证实了理论分析。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，$m$-set 对抗性老虎机中的细微设置变化（效用与反馈模型）会对学习率产生巨大影响，理论下界和实验均支持这一结论。

Abstract: We show upper and lower bounds on the regret of $m$-set adversarial bandits for different utilities (winner reward or sum of rewards) and feedback models (winner index, winner reward, sum of rewards, and their combinations). By comparing to standard bounds for combinatorial and MNL bandits, our results reveal how subtle changes in the setting can have a dramatic impact on the learning rates. Our main technical contributions are the information-theoretic lower bounds on the regret. Experiments on synthetic data confirm our theoretical analyses.

</details>


### [285] [A Unified Information-Theoretic Approach to Constrained Multi-Fidelity Multi-Objective Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10174)
*Rikuto Matsumoto,Masanori Ishikura,Masayuki Karasuyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从统一信息论视角出发，用查询对最高保真可行帕累托前沿的信息增益来衡量查询效用，并给出可计算的变分下界，实现多目标、约束与多保真度联合优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯优化常涉及多目标、约束和多个保真度层级。现有方法往往将保真度选择与约束处理作为独立的启发式来处理，缺乏统一框架来联合决定"在哪里"以及"用哪个保真度"进行评估，以识别最高保真度下的可行帕累托前沿。

Method: 以信息增益（观测关于目标帕累托前沿的互信息）作为查询效用，但因该互信息不可解，采用对帕累托一致区域的欠截断与过截断近似混合，推导出变分下界。配合多保真度代理模型将信息传播到任意保真度，得到代价感知的采集函数，无需为保真度选择或约束处理单独设计启发式。

Result: 在合成问题、标准基准以及真实世界问题上的实验表明，该方法在多样的目标、约束与保真度设置下均有效。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个统一的信息论框架，将多目标、约束与多保真度贝叶斯优化整合为单一采集函数，避免了分离式启发式设计，并在多类任务上验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Bayesian optimization often involves multiple objectives, constraints, and fidelity levels. We address the challenge of jointly selecting where and at which fidelity to evaluate to identify the highest-fidelity feasible Pareto frontier in this combined setting. From a unified information-theoretic perspective, we measure query utility by the information gain about this frontier, provided by an observation. Since this mutual information is intractable, we derive a variational lower bound using a mixture of under- and over-truncated approximations to the Pareto-consistent region. Multi-fidelity surrogate models propagate the information to arbitrary fidelities, yielding a cost-aware acquisition function without separate heuristics for fidelity selection or constraint handling. Experiments on synthetic, benchmark, and real-world problems demonstrate effectiveness across diverse objective, constraint, and fidelity settings.

</details>


### [286] [Pre-training of Bayesian Optimization Algorithm through Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10186)
*Satoshi Katayama,Shoyo Hunt,Shintaro Masuda,Masayuki Karasuyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个利用高斯过程样本路径来优化贝叶斯优化算法自身参数的框架，并采用外层BO求解该参数优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯优化（BO）算法通常包含需要预先指定的参数，其性能对这些参数选择高度敏感，因此需要一种自动优化这些参数的方法。

Method: 从BO开始时可用信息推断的高斯过程（GP）中抽取样本路径；以累积遗憾作为BO算法的性能指标；在生成的样本路径上运行BO算法，得到给定参数配置下期望累积遗憾的经验估计；通过优化该估计来寻找参数配置；由于该参数优化本身是黑盒优化问题，采用另一个BO过程（称为outer BO）求解。

Result: 实验表明，该框架能够在多个候选参数配置中有效选择出性能较强的参数配置。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架可以基于当前可用信息，有效选择和优化BO算法的参数配置，从而提升其性能表现。

Abstract: Bayesian optimization (BO) is widely used as a standard approach for expensive black-box optimization. However, BO algorithms often involve parameters that must be specified in advance, and their performance can strongly depend on these choices. We propose a framework for optimizing such parameters using sample paths drawn from a Gaussian process (GP) inferred from the information available at the start of BO. We use cumulative regret as the performance metric for a BO algorithm. By running the BO algorithm on the generated sample paths, we obtain an empirical estimate of its expected cumulative regret for a given parameter configuration. Optimizing this estimate allows us to identify parameter configurations that, given the currently available information, are expected to achieve low cumulative regret. Since this parameter optimization is itself a black-box optimization problem, we employ another BO procedure to solve it, which we refer to as outer BO. Through experiments, we demonstrate that the proposed framework can effectively select parameter configurations that achieve strong performance among a range of candidate configurations.

</details>


### [287] [Robust Decentralized Fairness Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10199)
*Sayan Biswas,Jade Garcia Bourrée,Anne-Marie Kermarrec,Palak,Martijn de Vos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Auditopus，一种无中心服务器的去中心化公平性审计方法，通过多轮查询、共享累积统计向量并本地降权不一致审计者，抵御制造公平假象的对抗性审计者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新兴立法要求对 LLM 进行合规审计（尤其是公平性）。黑箱审计通常假设单一审计者拥有大量代表性查询集，但实际难以获得。多审计者协作可覆盖不同人群，但存在信任问题：审计者可能代表 LLM 提供方制造公平假象（fairwashing）。

Method: Auditopus 采用无中心服务器的多轮去中心化审计：每轮每位审计者向 LLM 发出固定数量查询，仅向其他审计者发送结果的累积统计向量而非明文敏感查询；通过聚合所有向量估计公平性。为应对单个对抗审计者伪造向量操纵估计，每个诚实审计者本地对累积统计向量与历史不一致的审计者进行降权。

Result: 在两个数据集、两个预训练 LLM 上，面对优化发送向量以掩盖不公平的攻击者，Auditopus 相比无防御平均将审计误差降低最多 78%，相比稳健聚合基线至少降低 62%；即使 49% 审计者为对抗性，也从未让极不公平或中等不公平的 LLM 通过审计。

Conclusion: Auditopus 能在去中心化、存在对抗审计者的条件下实现稳健的 LLM 公平性审计，且无需中心服务器与明文查询共享。

Abstract: Emerging legislation requires large language models (LLMs) to be audited for compliance with regulatory standards, particularly fairness. Such black-box audits typically assume a single auditor with access to a large, representative set of queries. In practice, it can be difficult for an auditor to obtain such a query set, but multiple auditors can together cover the relevant demographic groups by auditing the LLM collaboratively with their individual query sets. However, relying on multiple auditors raises a fundamental trust problem, as they may act on behalf of the LLM provider to portray a misleading appearance of fairness, i.e., fairwashing. We propose Auditopus, a novel approach for robust decentralized fairness auditing. In Auditopus, auditing proceeds in rounds without a central server. In each round, every auditor issues a fixed number of queries to the LLM, and sends only cumulative statistics vectors of its query results to other auditors instead of sensitive queries in clear. The fairness of the audited LLM is then estimated by aggregating all the vectors. We show theoretically and empirically that even a single adversarial auditor in the network can steer this estimate by fabricating the vectors it sends, making an unfair LLM appear fair. To address this threat, Auditopus has each honest auditor locally down-weight any auditor whose cumulative statistics vectors are statistically inconsistent with previous ones. We implement Auditopus and compare it to robust aggregation baselines on two datasets with two pre-trained LLMs. Against an attacker that optimizes the vectors it sends to make the LLM appear fair, Auditopus reduces audit error by up to 78% on average relative to no defense and at least 62% relative to the robust aggregation baselines. Even when 49% of the auditors are adversarial, Auditopus never lets a very unfair or moderately unfair LLM pass as fair.

</details>


### [288] [How to train your model organism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10203)
*Xilin Wang,David Bau,Byron C. Wallace*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出当前"模型生物"（植入后门、谄媚、伪相关等行为的模型）多只针对单一目标训练，导致验证不足；提出以目标行为植入、通用能力保持、输出自然性三目标框架来验证，并给出基于模型合并的多目标训练方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型生物是评估白盒可解释性技术的关键工具，但主流做法只用一个目标（植入目标行为）来训练，忽略了通用能力与输出自然性的退化，从而可能使关于可解释性方法的结论不可靠、不可泛化。

Method: 提出包含三项指标（目标行为植入、通用能力保持、输出自然性）的验证框架，重新审视两个公开的模型生物套件；提出基于模型合并的多目标训练方法以训练更真实的模型生物；并新建一套针对临床推理中人口统计偏差的模型生物套件，比较DPO与监督微调等训练配方，并用"调查者智能体"进行审计。

Result: 发现聊天质量与思维链自然性在不同训练配方下显著退化；验证指标能预测可解释性方法恢复所植入行为的效果（如logit lens读数与模型通用能力协变）；在临床偏差套件中，DPO比监督微调更接近基础模型，所提模型优化方法更好地保持能力与自然性，且验证指标同样能跟踪偏差恢复情况。

Conclusion: 训练方法会塑造模型生物所能支持的可解释性结论，因此应综合考虑多个目标，才能用（更真实的）模型生物对可解释性方法得出可泛化的结论。

Abstract: Model organisms of alignment-relevant behaviors (e.g., backdoors, sycophancy, spurious correlations) have emerged as a key tool for evaluating whitebox interpretability techniques. We argue that the prevailing practice of training model organisms to a single objective of installing the target behavior is insufficient and propose validating model organisms with respect to three objectives with associated metrics: target-behavior installation, general-capability preservation (i.e., parametric knowledge, chat quality), and output naturalness (i.e., CoT and activations). We re-visit two publicly released organism suites using this validation framework and show that (1) chat quality and CoT naturalness degrade substantially across training recipes, and (2) validation metrics predict how well interpretability methods recover the installed behavior, e.g., a logit lens readout covaries with an organism's general capabilities. We introduce a multi-objective training approach based on model merging to train more realistic model organisms. Finally, on a new suite of model organisms targeting demographic biases in clinical reasoning, we compare training recipes and find that DPO training stays closer to the base model than supervised finetuning, and the proposed model optimization approach better preserves capabilities and naturalness. Auditing this suite with an investigator agent, we again observe validation metrics tracking bias recovery. In sum, training methods shape the interpretability conclusions an organism supports, and we argue that one should consider multiple objectives to draw generalizable conclusions about interpretability methods using (realistic) model organisms.

</details>


### [289] [OrthoGen: A Generative Orthogonal Learner for Time-Varying Treatments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10210)
*Tomàs Garriga,Valentyn Melnychuk,Konstantin Hess,Eduard Serrahima de Cambra,Axel Brando,Gerard Sanz,Stefan Feuerriegel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出生成式递归g-computation调整策略与OrthoGen（Neyman正交、双稳健的生成式学习器），用于估计时变治疗下的条件分布潜在结果（CDPOs）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 估计随时间变化的CDPOs在医学中很重要（如评估不同治疗序列下患者的个体化风险），但时变混杂使该任务极具挑战，且现有调整策略有限。

Method: 1）提出针对该场景的调整策略——生成式递归g-computation，递归传播完整的条件结果分布而非条件均值，直接建模目标变量而非完整轨迹；基于此构建简单的生成式学习器；2）针对其易受冗余参数估计误差影响的问题，提出OrthoGen，一种Neyman正交且双稳健的生成式学习器，可灵活搭配归一化流、扩散模型等生成骨干。

Result: 理论上证明OrthoGen在适当条件下实现率双稳健与准预言效率；在合成、半合成及真实世界数据集上的实验中，OrthoGen表现高度有效。

Conclusion: 这是首个面向时变治疗下CDPO估计的生成式正交学习器。

Abstract: Estimating conditional distributional potential outcomes (CDPOs) over time is important in medicine (e.g., to estimate patient-specific risks under different treatment sequences). However, this task is challenging because of time-varying confounding, yet existing adjustment strategies for this task are limited. In this paper, we aim to learn CDPOs under time-varying treatments using flexible generative models. Our contributions are two-fold. (1) We introduce a tailored adjustment strategy for our setting, namely, generative recursive g-computation. Our adjustment strategy recursively propagates full conditional outcome distributions rather than conditional means, modeling the variables of interest directly rather than full trajectories. Building on our adjustment strategy, we formulate simple generative learners for CDPO estimation. However, these learners can be sensitive to nuisance estimation errors, which motivates an orthogonal learner. (2) We thus introduce OrthoGen, a Neyman-orthogonal and doubly robust generative learner. Importantly, we show that OrthoGen further achieves rate double robustness and quasi-oracle efficiency under suitable conditions. Our learners are flexible and can be instantiated with different generative backbones (e.g., normalizing flows and diffusion models). Across experiments with synthetic, semi-synthetic and real-world datasets, we find that OrthoGen is highly effective. To the best of our knowledge, we are the first to propose a generative orthogonal learner for estimating CDPOs under time-varying treatments.

</details>


### [290] [Edge Accuracy Is Not Enough: Why Dynamics-Learned Structure Fails to Transfer to Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10213)
*Nicholas Tan Jerome,Fangnian Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 从动力学预测中学到的关系结构（如NRI）在逆问题中迁移失败，即使满足标准理论条件；这种失败源于边准确度无法控制的近似误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆问题标注数据稀缺，常用策略是迁移从丰富前向仿真数据中学到的关系结构。尽管理论上结构应有助于降低样本复杂度，但作者发现该策略会系统性失败，即便它满足标准理论上的合理性条件，因此需要揭示这一失败并给出诊断方法。

Method: 理论证明：当边误差满足 Δ < n² - kn 时，近似结构可将样本复杂度从 O(n²) 降至 O(kn+Δ)。实验：在180个CFD氢泄漏仿真与180个声学场景的源定位任务上，对比NRI学到的结构、基于物理的先验（Green函数）与灵活的任务优化注意力基线。并开展四条独立证据：增加18倍训练数据、改变NRI边阈值、对比动力学图与任务最优图的边重叠、以及两种其他动力学衍生结构估计器（相关性与互信息）。最后提出轻量可迁移性测试（对部分观测目标图计算Jaccard相似度）。

Result: NRI结构相对任务优化基线性能下降116%和201%，物理先验仅下降69-72%。NRI在数据增加18倍时仅提升0.5%（基线提升16.6%，p<0.001）；性能对NRI边阈值不敏感；动力学图与任务最优图仅6%边重叠；相关性和互信息估计器无明显收益，相关估计器更差。Jaccard相似度测试在四组域/结构对中均能区分成功与失败的迁移，耗时不到一小时、使用15-20%目标域数据。

Conclusion: 从动力学学到的结构迁移到逆问题可能系统性失败，这一失败被形式化为边准确度条件无法控制的近似误差，需更强条件如可迁移性子空间对齐；Jaccard相似度可作为启发式诊断工具，但仅基于少量案例校准，尚非通用阈值。

Abstract: A natural strategy for inverse problems with scarce labelled data is to transfer relational structure learned from abundant forward-simulation data. We show this strategy fails systematically, even when it satisfies the standard theoretical justification for why structure should help. We prove that approximate structure provides estimation-error benefits whenever the edge error satisfies $Δ< n^2 - kn$, reducing sample complexity from $O(n^2)$ to $O(kn+Δ)$. Structure learned via Neural Relational Inference (NRI) from dynamics prediction satisfies this condition, yet on a source-localisation task across 180 CFD-simulated hydrogen-leak scenarios and 180 acoustic scenarios, it degrades performance by 116% and 201% relative to a flexible, task-optimised attention baseline, while a physics-based prior (Green's function) degrades by only 69-72%. Four independent lines of evidence show this is not a tuning failure: NRI improves only 0.5% when given 18x more training data (versus 16.6% for the task-optimised baseline, $p<0.001$); performance is insensitive to the NRI edge threshold across a wide range; the dynamics-learned graph overlaps the task-optimal graph on only 6% of edges; and two further dynamics-derived structure estimators (correlation- and mutual-information-based) show no measurable benefit over a structure-free baseline, with the correlation-based estimator performing markedly worse. We formalise this gap as a statement about approximation error that the edge-accuracy condition cannot control, and we provide a lightweight transferability test (Jaccard similarity against a partially-observed target-task graph) that separates successful from failed transfer in all four domain/structure pairs we evaluate, using under an hour of computation and 15-20% of target-domain data; we present this as a heuristic calibrated on few cases, not a validated general threshold.

</details>


### [291] [Finite-Sample Approximation of Hessian-Guided Perturbed Wasserstein Gradient Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10218)
*Ryotaro Kawata,Atsushi Nitanda,Taiji Suzuki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究PWGF（Hessian引导的扰动Wasserstein梯度流）用有限粒子近似时，在增长时间尺度上保持精度的条件，并给出高概率跟踪界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wasserstein梯度流将梯度下降推广到概率测度，PWGF通过高斯扰动帮助逃离非凸问题中的鞍点。但用有限多粒子近似该流时，随时间增长近似是否仍然准确尚不清楚。

Method: 保留沿种群驱动参考路径累积的曲率：负曲率放大误差，随后正曲率抑制误差。在正则性假设和给定共同扰动计划下，对满足累积曲率显式条件的参考路径，证明粒子与目标值跟踪界。针对状态依赖高斯跳变，构造population-first耦合，保持参考粒子条件独立并将跳变误差化为协方差比较。

Result: 在高概率事件上得到粒子与目标值跟踪界；在variance-plus-cosine模型中验证条件，曲率恢复带来增长时间尺度的跟踪保证；在正则化矩阵分解模型中建立局部吸引、横向下降与正二阶变分，支撑正-负-正曲率模式。

Conclusion: 表明临时不稳定与增长时间尺度上的精确跟踪可以兼容，曲率累积分析是刻画PWGF粒子近似长期精度的关键。

Abstract: Wasserstein gradient flow extends gradient descent to probability measures. Its Hessian-guided perturbed variant (PWGF) adds Gaussian perturbations to escape saddle points in nonconvex problems. We investigate when its approximation by finitely many interacting particles remains accurate over growing time horizons. Our analysis retains the curvature accumulated along the population-driven reference path: negative curvature can amplify approximation errors, while subsequent positive curvature can damp their influence. This captures favorable scenarios in which temporary instability is compatible with accurate tracking over growing horizons. Under regularity assumptions and a prescribed common perturbation schedule, we prove particle and objective-value tracking bounds on a high-probability event for reference paths satisfying explicit conditions on accumulated curvature. To handle state-dependent Gaussian jumps, we construct a population-first coupling that preserves the reference particles' conditional independence and reduces jump errors to covariance comparison. We verify the conditions in a variance-plus-cosine model, where curvature recovery yields a growing-horizon tracking guarantee. We also establish local attraction, transverse descent, and positive second variation in two regions of a regularized matrix-factorization model, motivating a positive-negative-positive curvature pattern.

</details>


### [292] [Evaluating Sequence Assembly Strategies for Differentially Private Synthetic Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10222)
*Guoxiong Long,Huizhen Huang,Qikun Cai,Tao Huang,Chen Hou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究差分隐私时间序列生成器输出的固定长度合成窗口在用于长序列预测训练时的“生成后组装”过程，系统改变重叠率与窗口加权方式，并在四类数据集、五个预测模型上评估边界连续性、保真度与TSTR效用，发现组装偏好依赖具体预测模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差分隐私时间序列生成器通常只产生固定长度合成窗口，而下游预测模型往往需要长连续训练序列；因此窗口的组装方式会改变实际输入预测器的合成数据，即便生成器本身不变。作者希望系统研究这一被忽视的“生成后组装”环节对预测效用的影响。

Method: 在生成阶段之后，系统变化重叠率（overlap rates）和窗口加权方案（window-weighting schemes），将合成窗口拼装为长序列；在四类公开数据集（ETTh1、ETTm1、Weather、Appliances）和五个预测模型上，从边界连续性、统计与时间保真度、以及TSTR预测效用三方面进行评估，并构建完整的五预测器组装网格，同时配套TRTR参考。

Result: TSTR效用由预测器、重叠率和窗口加权方案共同决定，不同预测模型呈现不同的组装偏好。提高重叠率总体可改善边界连续性，但连续性或单项保真度指标的提升并不总能降低预测误差，说明这些诊断指标本身不足以用于选择组装配置。完整的组装网格与TRTR参考刻画了这些规律，并量化了相对于真实数据训练的组装依赖效用。

Conclusion: 序列组装是影响差分隐私合成时间序列下游预测效用的关键生成后因素，且最优组装配置依赖具体预测模型；边界连续性和单项保真度指标不足以指导组装选择。作者通过稳健性与生成器变异性分析进一步验证了所发现的原则。

Abstract: Differentially private time-series generators commonly produce fixed-length synthetic windows, whereas downstream forecasting models often require long continuous training sequences. How these windows are assembled after generation can therefore alter the effective synthetic data presented to a forecaster, even when the trained generator remains unchanged. We study this post-generation sequence assembly process by systematically varying overlap rates and window-weighting schemes and evaluating the resulting sequences in terms of boundary continuity, statistical and temporal fidelity, and Train-on-Synthetic-Test-on-Real (TSTR) forecasting utility. Across four types of public datasets (ETTh1, ETTm1, Weather, and Appliances) and five forecasting models, the results reveal a clear forecaster-dependent assembly principle: downstream TSTR utility is jointly shaped by the forecaster, overlap rate, and window-weighting scheme, leading to distinct assembly preferences across forecasting models. Increased overlap generally improves boundary continuity, but improvements in continuity or individual fidelity diagnostics do not consistently reduce forecasting error, indicating that these diagnostics alone are insufficient for selecting assembly configurations. Complete five-forecaster assembly grids, together with matched Train-on-Real-Test-on-Real (TRTR) references, further characterize these regularities and quantify assembly-dependent utility relative to real-data training. We then validate the identified principles through additional analyses of robustness and generator variability.

</details>


### [293] [From Prompts to Trees: Effective LLM-Guided Tree Generation for Few-Shot Tabular Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10227)
*Yue Qiu,Zekang Du,Yiqun Diao,Bingsheng He,Qinbin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种将大语言模型知识蒸馏为可解释决策树的框架，用于小样本表格数据分类，通过三阶段"生成规则-组织成树"范式，兼顾准确率与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型具备丰富世界知识与泛化能力，但直接用于表格数据分类存在推理成本高、可解释性差的问题；决策树快速透明，但在低数据场景下性能不佳。作者希望结合二者优势。

Method: 在小样本学习设定下，将LLM知识蒸馏进可解释决策树。不直接让LLM生成完整树（不稳定且低效），而是采用三阶段范式：先提示LLM生成规则，再将规则组织成树。

Result: 在多个真实世界表格数据集上的实验表明，该方法在准确率和可解释性上优于现有基线，且提示开销显著更低。

Conclusion: 通过规则生成与树组织的蒸馏框架，可有效融合LLM知识获取能力与决策树的可解释性，在小样本表格分类中实现更高效、更透明的性能表现。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) possess rich world knowledge and impressive generalization capabilities, their direct application to tabular data classification is hindered by high inference costs and limited interpretability. In contrast, decision trees are fast and transparent but often underperform in low-data regimes. In this work, we propose a novel framework that bridges these paradigms by distilling LLM knowledge into interpretable decision trees under a few-shot learning setting. Instead of directly prompting the LLM to generate full trees, which is often unstable and inefficient, we develop a three-stage paradigm that prompts the LLM to generate rules and organize the rules into a tree. Experiments on multiple real-world tabular datasets demonstrate that our method achieves superior accuracy and interpretability with significantly lower prompting overhead compared to existing baselines.

</details>


### [294] [ProtocolMatch: Protocol-Dependent Model Selection for Scientific Dynamics Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10239)
*Lu Wei,Yufeng Wang,Haibin Ling*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出ProtocolMatch框架，主张科学动力学预测的模型选择应基于协议而非单纯架构，并报告精度、物理有效性与分布偏移可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学动力学预测常被简化为架构选择问题，但实际部署还取决于观测历史、rollout反馈、算力预算、物理目标和测试分布等因素，因此需要一种考虑协议依赖性的模型选择方法。

Method: 提出ProtocolMatch——一个算力匹配、基于验证集选择、且保留失败案例的评估框架。在驱动量子自旋动力学上，比较循环网络、分块注意力、因果注意力和低秩线性预测器，并跨三个独立生成的数据集进行测试。

Result: 在固定双自旋任务中，随着训练集增大，因果注意力与循环网络的排序发生反转；六自旋局部可观测量比较中线性预测器误差最低；限制观测历史会恶化刷新历史视图但改善闭环视图；最新状态MLP在状态刷新下各数据集均优于持续性预测，但闭环排名因系统而异并存在爆炸误差；物理惩罚改善一致性但不稳定提升误差；分布内区间在驱动频率偏移后大幅失去覆盖率。

Conclusion: 科学模型选择应返回预测器及其对应协议，并分别报告精度、物理有效性和偏移分布下的可靠性。

Abstract: Scientific dynamics forecasting is often framed as an architecture choice, although deployment is also determined by observed history, rollout feedback, compute budget, physical objective, and test distribution. We formulate protocol-dependent model selection and introduce ProtocolMatch, a compute-matched, validation-selected, and failure-preserving evaluation framework. On driven quantum-spin dynamics, we compare recurrent, patched-attention, causal-attention, and low-rank linear predictors across three independently generated datasets. The causal-attention--recurrence ordering reverses as the training set grows within a fixed two-spin task, while a linear predictor has the lowest mean error in the six-spin local-observable comparison. Restricting observed history worsens every refreshed-history view but improves every closed-loop view in the four-spin study. A latest-state MLP has lower error than persistence on every dataset under state refresh across all five cells, yet its closed-loop rank varies by system and includes finite explosive errors. Physical penalties improve targeted consistency without reliably improving prediction error, and in-distribution intervals lose most coverage after a driving-frequency shift. Thus scientific model selection should return a predictor with its protocol and report accuracy, physical validity, and shifted-distribution reliability separately.

</details>


### [295] [Stationary Bias and Extrapolation in Nonlinear Two-Timescale Stochastic Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10246)
*A. Ch. Madhusudanarao,Rahul Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究非线性双时间尺度随机逼近在常步长下由外生有限状态马尔可夫链驱动的稳态偏差，给出均匀有效的一阶偏差展开，揭示步长间的混合依赖并指导偏差校正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常步长随机逼近存在非零稳态均值误差，即使时间平均也难以消除。已有分析多针对线性或单时间尺度情形，对由马尔可夫链驱动的非线性双时间尺度递推的偏差结构（尤其是快慢步长差异很大时的行为）缺乏刻画。

Method: 在既定光滑性假设与平稳分布条件下，采用快流形坐标使协方差方程在慢步长远小于快步长的极限下保持正则性，推导一阶偏差展开；并对有限次运行下两个时间尺度尾部平均的初始化误差进行界定，以及在加性独立噪声情形下利用三阶矩符号抵消获得更精细的余项。

Result: 偏差展开显示除各步长的线性项外，还存在混合贡献 ε²/η（快步长 η、慢步长 ε）；在幂律步长路径上偏差指数未必为整数，故 Richardson–Romberg 外推需匹配路径权重的权重。一个可精确求解的非线性马尔可夫例子验证了系数，并在时序差分学习中验证了局部化，同时在相等更新预算下比较了有限次运行外推。

Conclusion: 双时间尺度常步长随机逼近的偏差由步长线性项与 ε²/η 混合项共同决定，其非整数偏差指数要求外推权重按路径匹配；该分析为时序差分学习等场景的偏差校正与有限次运行误差控制提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Constant-step stochastic approximation generally has a nonzero stationary mean error that persists under time averaging. This paper studies that error for nonlinear two-timescale recursions driven by an exogenous finite-state Markov chain. Under stated smoothness assumptions and conditions on the stationary distribution, we derive a first-order bias expansion whose error bound remains uniform as the slow step size becomes much smaller than the fast step size. Fast-manifold coordinates keep the associated covariance equation regular in this limit. For fast step $η$ and slow step $\varepsilon$, the expansion reveals a mixed contribution $\varepsilon^2/η$ alongside terms linear in each step size. This dependence matters for bias reduction: along power-law step-size paths, the bias exponents need not be integers, so Richardson--Romberg extrapolation requires weights matched to the path. An exactly solvable nonlinear Markov example verifies the coefficients. We verify localization for temporal-difference learning and compare finite-run extrapolation at equal update budgets. For finite runs, we bound the initialization error of tail averages on both timescales under an additional coupling assumption. In the special case of additive independent noise, signed third-moment cancellation yields a sharper remainder.

</details>


### [296] [Logarithmic Regret via Passive Change Detection in Piecewise-Stationary Self-Tuning Regulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10250)
*A. Ch. Madhusudanarao,Rahul Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究带外生输入、系数在未知时刻变化的未知自回归系统的最小方差控制，提出 PIECE-CD 算法，在给定条件下实现对数级遗憾界并保证低误报与对数检测延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对未知自回归系统在系数于未知时刻发生变化的场景下的最小方差控制问题，现有切换赌博机中未选臂变化不可观测，而本文关注可利用开采阶段信息进行变化检测的可容许系统变化设定。

Method: PIECE-CD 算法：初始阶段与报警后进行探索，随后采用带门控的递归最小二乘进行控制；通过能量检验比较加窗输出功率与高于噪声底的阈值来检测变化；扩展到控制器不稳定失配时，额外监控参考控制器的输入建议；对输入进行有界裁剪；跨时间范围控制误报并证明对数检测延迟。

Result: 在有界独立扰动、固定检测间隔、稳定性与可行性条件及变化间充分间隔下，以至少 1-δ 的概率证明遗憾界为 O((C+1)log((T+1)/δ))，其中 T 为时间跨度、C 为变化次数；检测延迟为对数级；在使裁剪经过有限预热后失效的显式条件下同样成立对数遗憾。

Conclusion: 在所述可行性条件下，扩展检测器可覆盖具有固定窗口内可检测过剩能量的失稳变化，实现对数级遗憾保证。

Abstract: We study minimum-variance control of an unknown autoregressive system with exogenous inputs and coefficients that change at unknown times. Under bounded independent disturbances, fixed detection gaps, stability and feasibility conditions, and sufficient time between changes, we prove \(O((C+1)\log((T+1)/δ))\) regret with probability at least \(1-δ\), where \(T\) is the horizon and \(C\) the number of changes. Unlike switching bandits, where unselected arms can change unobserved, admissible plant changes provide information during exploitation: the correct feasible controller leaves only the disturbance in the output, whereas a detectable change raises output energy under the old controller. PIECE-CD explores initially and after alarms, then uses gated recursive least squares for control. Its energy test compares windowed output power with a threshold above the noise floor; the extension to unstable controller mismatches also monitors the reference controller's input proposal. We control false alarms across the horizon and prove logarithmic detection delay. Inputs are clipped to prescribed bounds. Logarithmic regret also holds under an explicit condition ensuring that clipping becomes inactive after a finite burn-in. Under the stated feasibility conditions, the extended detector covers destabilizing changes with detectable excess energy over a fixed window.

</details>


### [297] [PairAudit: Guiding Human Review with Graph Tokens under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10260)
*Jiran Tao,Binyan Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PairAudit，利用图令牌捕捉连接节点间的预测模式，通过异常关系模式发现被忽视的入侵检测错误，从而在固定审查预算下改进人工审查优先级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 入侵检测器在面对训练中未见过的攻击时会自信地误分类，人工审查虽能纠正错误但可审查数量有限。基于不确定性的审查可能忽略自信的错误，而单靠异常分数无法判断调整审查计划是否能纠正更多错误。

Method: PairAudit使用图令牌捕捉连接节点间的预测模式，不构建新的预测器或进行特征聚合，而是利用异常的关系模式来发现已有预测中的潜在错误。随后借助人工反馈判断这些发现是否值得调整审查优先级。

Result: 在多种安全任务上的实验表明，PairAudit平均比基于不确定性的审查纠正更多错误，包括在未见攻击上纠正更多错误。这些收益已计入所有审查成本，且无需重新训练检测器。

Conclusion: PairAudit能够在固定预算下发现被忽视的错误并改进审查，优于基于不确定性的方法，且不需重新训练检测器。

Abstract: Intrusion detectors can confidently misclassify attacks that were not seen during training. Human review can correct these errors, but only a limited number of cases can be checked. Uncertainty-based review may overlook confident errors, while anomaly scores alone do not show whether changing the review plan will correct more errors. We introduce PairAudit to find overlooked errors and improve review under a fixed budget. Its graph tokens capture prediction patterns across connected nodes. Rather than building another predictor through feature aggregation, PairAudit uses unusual relational patterns to uncover potential errors in existing predictions. Human feedback then helps decide whether these findings justify changing review priorities. Experiments across security tasks show that PairAudit corrects more errors on average than uncertainty-based review, including more errors on unseen attacks. These gains account for all review costs and do not require retraining the detector.

</details>


### [298] [SemanticFold: Latent Sequence Compression SeparatesLanguage Modeling, Decodability, and Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10304)
*Mingyan Liu,Min Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 SemanticFold 前缀隐状态折叠压缩方案，在五个模型尺度与五类评测端点上进行固定目标协议评估，发现压缩效果非单调、各端点不共享单一压缩阈值，且无单一标量证书可证明能力保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究提示前缀的潜在序列压缩是否保留大模型推理所依赖的能力，并检验"能力保持"能否用单一指标刻画。

Method: 提出 SemanticFold：在学习到的边界处折叠前缀隐状态。采用固定目标协议，冻结前缀分别原生执行或压缩，两臂对相同续写 token 强制教师，排除目标选择解释。评测五个模型尺度（Qwen3-1.7B、Qwen3-8B、SmolLM2-1.7B、Pythia-1.4B、Pythia-6.9B）与五类端点：固定目标 NLL、有限标签推理准确率、线性探针可及性、开放式生成、系统级内存与延迟；并用 NLL 分解区分序列缩短与学习残差变换，另设仅 MLP（不缩短）对照。

Result: 端点随压缩非单调变化且阈值不一致。Qwen3-1.7B 在 R=1.7 时压缩减原生平均 NLL 下降 0.135（配对 bootstrap，10000 次抽样）；SmolLM2 在 R=1.2 时平均变化比原生高 0.013；两个 Pythia 检查点 NLL 基本不变。NLL 分解显示 Qwen 的有利似然主要归因于残差适应而非单纯缩短；仅 MLP 变体比完整 SemanticFold 低 0.082 NLL。线性探针准确率与宏 AUC 在各条件下绝对变化小于 0.03，置信区间跨越零。

Conclusion: 潜在压缩下的能力保持不存在单一标量证书：语言模型拟合度、可解码性与推理行为回答的是不同问题，且在同一压缩操作下可能朝不同方向变化。

Abstract: We study whether latent sequence compression of prompt prefixes preserves the capabilities that large language models rely on during inference. We introduce SemanticFold, a compression scheme that folds prefix hidden states at learned boundaries, and evaluate it across five model scales: Qwen3-1.7B, Qwen3-8B, SmolLM2-1.7B, Pythia-1.4B, and Pythia-6.9B. We use a fixed-target protocol: a frozen prefix is executed natively or compressed, and both arms teacher-force identical continuation tokens. This design rules out target-selection explanations for likelihood changes. We examine five endpoint families: fixed-target negative log-likelihood, finite-label reasoning accuracy, linear probe accessibility, open-ended generation, and systems-level memory and latency. We find that compression moves these endpoints non-monotonically and that they do not share a single compression threshold. On Qwen3-1.7B at compression ratio R=1.7, compressed-minus-native mean NLL decreases by 0.135 under paired bootstrap with 10000 draws. On SmolLM2 at R=1.2, the mean change is 0.013 higher than native. On both Pythia checkpoints, NLL is effectively unchanged. An NLL decomposition separating sequence shortening from the learned residual transform shows that the favorable Qwen likelihood is attributable primarily to residual adaptation rather than to shortening alone. MLP-only, which applies the transform without shortening, achieves 0.082 lower NLL than Full SemanticFold. Linear probe accuracy and macro AUC change by less than 0.03 in absolute value across conditions, with confidence intervals crossing zero. We conclude that preservation under latent compression has no single scalar certificate: language-model fit, decodability, and reasoning behavior answer different questions and can move in different directions under the same compression operation.

</details>


### [299] [A Closed-Loop Non-Asymptotic Convergence Analysis of PPO with Learned Critics and Clipping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10273)
*Junwei Su,Mengfan Liu,Yanyong Zhang,Chuan Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文对PPO-Clip作为闭环actor-critic系统进行非渐近分析，刻画了actor-critic耦合、非光滑裁剪、有限批量复用与早期停止的交互，并给出同步与异步收敛保证及调参指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PPO-Clip广泛使用但难以调参，critic学习、裁剪与rollout复用之间的相互作用理解不完整，缺乏对闭环actor-critic系统的严格非渐近理论分析。

Method: 将PPO-Clip建模为闭环actor-critic系统，在显式覆盖与critic正则性假设下，使用原始GAE与蒙特卡洛critic目标，分析非光滑概率比裁剪、有限批量复用和可预测早期停止；对有限分层MDP使用表格critic，以实际裁剪梯度类的统一界替代完全轨迹计数，并采用两时间尺度调度。

Result: 同步与异步保证同时刻画策略平稳性和critic跟踪精度，并显式依赖算法参数；给出优化、critic跟踪、裁剪与有限批量误差的共同放大界；异步结果需要延迟相关的critic步长限制。验证的增长时域族具有多项式样本复杂度，两时间尺度调度达到O(T^{-2/5})的平稳性与critic跟踪界，并显式计入新rollout。

Conclusion: 研究推进了对PPO的理论理解，为调参提供了理论指导；违反耦合或步长条件并不证明发散。

Abstract: Despite its widespread use, Proximal Policy Optimization with clipping (PPO-Clip) remains difficult to tune, and the interactions among critic learning, clipping, and rollout reuse remain incompletely understood. We develop a \emph{non-asymptotic} analysis of PPO-Clip as a \emph{closed-loop actor--critic} system. It captures actor--critic coupling, nonsmooth probability-ratio clipping, finite-batch reuse, and predictable early stopping under explicit coverage and critic regularity assumptions, using raw GAE and Monte Carlo critic targets. Our synchronous and asynchronous guarantees jointly characterize policy stationarity and the tracking accuracy of the learned critic, with explicit dependence on algorithmic parameters. A sufficient coupling condition gives optimization, critic tracking, clipping, and finite-batch errors a common amplification bound. The asynchronous result also requires a delay-dependent critic stepsize restriction; violating these conditions does not establish divergence. For finite layered MDPs with tabular critics, a uniform bound on the actual clipped-gradient class replaces complete-trajectory counting. A verified growing-horizon family has polynomial sample complexity, and a two-time-scale schedule gives $O(T^{-2/5})$ stationarity and critic-tracking bounds with explicit fresh-rollout accounting. These results together advance our understanding about PPO and provide theoretical guidance in tuning.

</details>


### [300] [Fault-tolerant foundation models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10311)
*Trevor McCourt,Ila R. Fiete,Isaac L. Chuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 大语言模型可被训练以容忍不可靠硬件，且随规模增大其容错能力反而增强，作者据此推测适当训练的LLM可能具备形式化容错性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新兴计算机硬件常以可靠性换取能效。若LLM能容忍这种不可靠性，则可在低能耗、易出错硬件上运行AI推理，从而相较现状实现大幅节能。

Method: 在模拟的不可靠数字硬件上进行约40,000 GPU小时的训练实验，并据此推导修正的神经缩放定律，量化模型容错能力随规模变化的趋势。

Result: 模型规模增大时容错能力不降反升；其计算似乎学习落在"好的"纠错码内，且这种纠错的相对开销无论模型多大都保持有限。

Conclusion: 作者由此推测，经过适当训练的LLM可能具有形式化容错性；若成立，则在低能耗、易出错硬件上运行AI推理可能成为实现显著节能的途径。

Abstract: Emerging computer hardware often trades reliability for energy efficiency; here we show that large-language models (LLMs) can be trained to tolerate this unreliability, and that rather than degrading, their error resilience actually increases as they grow. Modified neural scaling laws inferred from 40,000 GPU-hours of training runs on simulated faulty digital hardware quantify this trend and suggest that models learn to compute within "good" error-correcting codes, whose relative overhead remains finite no matter how large the model gets. This finding leads us to conjecture that appropriately trained LLMs may be formally fault-tolerant; if true, running AI inference on low energy, faulty hardware may be a path to substantial energy savings over the status quo.

</details>


### [301] [Sparse Planning in Visual World Models via Cost Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10274)
*Yingchen Xu,Edward Grefenstette*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: COSTGRAD是一种免训练、目标条件化的空间token选择器，通过规划代价对输入token的梯度范数排序，实现稀疏世界模型规划加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于token的世界模型支持细粒度潜在规划，但反复处理大规模空间token网格导致动作搜索代价高昂，需要降低规划时的token处理量。

Method: 提出COSTGRAD：免训练、目标条件化的选择器，按规划代价对每个输入token的梯度范数对空间token排序，从下游控制目标导出重要性，优先保留对规划而非仅对预测重要的token。

Result: 在AdaLN条件预测器上50%稀疏度时，COSTGRAD在4个连续控制基准中的3个上匹配或超过全token规划，环境规划步实测2.6倍加速；结合token稀疏与缩减的CEM搜索可达约5倍总加速且仍超过全token基线。同时发现架构相关的失效模式：匹配的AdaLN与concat对比中，concat保持相当的全token性能，但纯COSTGRAD相对随机选择的优势消失；该差异与动作通路漂移相关。

Conclusion: 选择器与架构的兼容性是稀疏世界模型规划的一个重要设计维度，COSTGRAD在AdaLN架构下有效加速规划，但在concat架构下失效。

Abstract: Token-based world models enable fine-grained latent planning, but repeatedly processing large spatial token grids makes action search expensive. We introduce COSTGRAD, a training-free, goal-conditioned selector that ranks spatial tokens by the gradient norm of the planning cost with respect to each input token. By deriving importance from the downstream control objective, COSTGRAD targets tokens that matter for planning rather than merely for prediction. On AdaLN-conditioned predictors at $50\%$ sparsity, COSTGRAD matches or exceeds full-token planning on three of four continuous-control benchmarks, while giving a measured $2.6\times$ wall-clock speedup per environment planning step. Combining token sparsity with reduced CEM search increases this to a $\sim 5\times$ total speedup while still exceeding the full-token baseline. We also identify an architecture-dependent failure mode: in a matched AdaLN-vs-concat comparison, concat maintains comparable full-token performance but pure COSTGRAD loses its advantage over random selection. This difference tracks action-pathway drift: gradient-selected removal produces less drift than random removal on AdaLN, but more on concat. These results highlight selector-architecture compatibility as a design axis for sparse world-model planning. Project page and demos: https://ycxuyingchen.github.io/costgrad/

</details>


### [302] [Training Parallel Speculative Draft Models by Directly Minimizing Expected Decoding Rounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10411)
*Yunxiao Zhao,Changxiao Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将投机解码训练建模为马尔可夫奖励过程，提出EDR目标，精确定义期望解码轮数，无需额外超参，并给出无偏时序差分梯度与离线评估器，在九个基准上提升两个SOTA drafter，性能优于现有训练目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并行与半自回归drafter可单次前向提出整块token，但训练困难：某位置的草稿分布依赖解码轮起点，而轮起点又由前轮接受token数决定，形成跨轮耦合。现有训练目标多用块内替代目标，忽略这种耦合，未直接优化全局解码效率。

Method: 将投机解码表示为马尔可夫奖励过程，推导出Expected Decoding Rounds（EDR）目标：以状态占用加权局部拒绝代价，精确等价于期望解码轮数，且不引入辅助超参；进一步推导精确的时序差分梯度，支持基于目标模型rollout的无偏随机优化；同一框架还给出精确的离线轮数评估器，可在共享rollout上配对比较drafter而无需实际运行投机解码。

Result: 用EDR微调两个SOTA drafter（DSpark与DFly），在数学推理、代码生成和对话等九个基准上，平均接受长度持续提升，并优于现有训练目标。

Conclusion: EDR为并行/半自回归drafter提供了理论严谨、无需额外超参的训练与评估框架，可直接优化全局解码轮数，实验验证其优于块内替代目标，具备通用性与实用价值。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates large language model inference by using a low-cost draft model to propose tokens that the full-size target model verifies in parallel. Parallel and semi-autoregressive (semi- AR) drafters improve drafting efficiency by proposing an entire block in a single forward pass, but training them raises a new difficulty: the draft distribution for a given position depends on where the decoding round starts, and where rounds start depends on how many tokens earlier rounds accepted. Existing training objectives typically rely on block-local surrogates that ignore this cross-round coupling, and therefore do not directly optimize the global decoding efficiency. In this work, we develop a theoretical framework for training and evaluating these drafters by representing speculative decoding as a Markov reward process. This formulation yields the Expected Decoding Rounds (EDR) objective, which weights local rejection costs by state occupancies and exactly equals the expected number of decoding rounds. Unlike prior surrogate objectives, EDR introduces no auxiliary hyperparameters. We then derive an exact temporal-difference gradient that supports unbiased stochastic optimization from target-model rollouts. The same framework also yields an exact offline evaluator for round counts, enabling paired drafter comparisons on shared target rollouts without running speculative decoding. Finetuning two state-of-the- art drafters, DSpark and DFly, with EDR consistently improves mean accepted length and outperforms existing training objectives across nine benchmarks spanning math reasoning, code generation, and chat.

</details>


### [303] [PatchBench: Measuring Collateral Damage in Activation Patching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10276)
*Alexi Canesse,Mathis Le Bail,Maël Jenny,Clément Elliker,Mahammed El Sharkawy,Sonia Vanier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 PatchBench 与 PatchBench-Local，用于更精确地评估越狱修复是否“行为精准”，而非仅仅通过基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协议主要测试模型能否被攻破，聚合指标（攻击成功率、拒答率、全局能力）无法区分“选择性修复”与“更广泛的局部抑制”。一个修复补丁可能通过基准，却对近似有害变体失效，或通过过度拒答共享措辞/结构的良性提示来压制有害行为。

Method: 从 37 个公开数据集的 27,870 个提示出发，筛选出 15,314 个英文提示，查询 8 个开源指令微调模型；结合 WildGuard 过滤、成对 Elo 排序与人工验证，保留 400 个高置信度越狱失败样本，构建 PatchBench。进一步提出 PatchBench-Local 评估协议：为每个有害源提示生成三类局部邻居——保留恶意意图的有害变体、结构匹配的良性提示、复用关键有害词的良性提示，并评估有害邻居纠正率与良性邻居保持率。

Result: 用 PatchBench-Local 与 MMLU 评估四种激活引导方法，发现全局能力几乎不变时，局部良性回退却可能很严重，说明聚合指标会遗漏重要的附带损害。

Conclusion: PatchBench-Local 为开发与比较越狱修复方法提供了更精确的基础，强调修复应同时满足有害邻居纠正与良性邻居保持。

Abstract: An LLM safety patch can pass a benchmark while still being a poor repair. This risk is especially acute for jailbreak repairs, where the goal is to correct a specific unsafe behaviour without changing unrelated behaviours. A patch may block exact evaluation prompts yet fail on close harmful variants, or suppress harmful behaviour by over-refusing benign prompts that share its wording or structure. Existing protocols primarily test whether models can be broken, while aggregate metrics (attack success, refusal rates, global capability) cannot distinguish selective repairs from broader local suppression. To address this gap, we introduce PatchBench, a benchmark of empirically observed model-specific jailbreak failures inducing actionable harmful answers. Starting from 27,870 prompts from 37 public datasets, we curate 15,314 English prompts and query 8 open-source instruction-tuned models. Combining WildGuard filtering, pairwise Elo ranking, and manual verification, we retain a curated bank of 400 high-confidence jailbreak failures. We further introduce PatchBench-Local, an evaluation protocol testing whether a patch is behaviourally precise. For each harmful source prompt, PatchBench-Local generates three families of local neighbours: harmful variants preserving malicious intent, benign prompts with matched structure, and benign prompts reusing key harmful terms. It evaluates harmful-neighbour correction and benign-neighbour preservation, distinguishing selective repair from broader local suppression. Evaluating four activation steering methods with PatchBench-Local and MMLU shows that global capability can remain nearly unchanged while local benign regressions are severe, confirming aggregate metrics miss important collateral damage. PatchBench-Local provides a more precise basis for developing and comparing jailbreak repair methods.

</details>


### [304] [Q-Learning with Scalar Adjoint Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10437)
*Yonghoon Dong,Minsung Yoon,Jaehyuk Kim,Jungwoo Park,Changyeon Kim,Jinwoo Shin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 SQAM 方法，用"标量伴随匹配"替代逐流步骤的向量-雅可比乘积，显著降低流策略离线强化学习微调的计算开销，并在最难任务与真实机器人上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流策略（flow policies）能刻画多样化的动作分布，用离策略强化学习微调可超越示教水平，但微调过程并不简单：策略的动作由多个流步骤生成，直接针对价值函数微调很困难。Adjoint matching 虽能通过将最终动作的价值信息回传到各流步骤来更新流模型，但需要在每个步骤计算向量-雅可比乘积（VJP），其代价随流步骤数与策略规模增长，代价高昂。

Method: 作者观察到预训练流策略的批次平均速度雅可比主要集中在对角线上。基于此，推导出一个闭式标量伴随：仅用流时间对最终动作处的价值梯度进行缩放，从而消除逐步骤的向量-雅可比乘积。同时发现，在标量伴随下，控制由策略生成动作处的 critic 价值尤为重要。据此提出 SQAM（Q-learning with Scalar Adjoint Matching），将标量伴随与这些动作上的价值惩罚相结合。

Result: SQAM 的增益集中在四个最难的 OGBench 领域，在每一领域的成功率均超过最强基线 18 至 35 个百分点。为验证其可扩展到大型预训练策略，作者还在真实双臂机器人上微调了视觉-语言-动作策略，SQAM 在全部三个任务上均优于监督微调。

Conclusion: 基于流策略速度雅可比近对角化的经验发现，标量伴随能以极低计算代价替换 adjoint matching 的 VJP 计算，配合策略动作处的价值惩罚，SQAM 在难任务与真实机器人上均取得了显著且可扩展的性能提升。

Abstract: Flow policies capture rich and diverse action distributions, and fine-tuning them with off-policy RL to improve beyond the demonstrations has drawn growing interest. However, fine-tuning a flow policy against a learned value function is not trivial, because the policy generates its action over many flow steps. Adjoint matching offers a principled way to update the flow model itself by propagating value information from the final action back to each flow step, but it requires a vector--Jacobian product through the policy at every step, a cost that grows with the number of flow steps and the policy size. We observe that the batch-averaged velocity Jacobian of pretrained flow policies concentrates on its diagonal. Motivated by this finding, we derive a closed-form scalar adjoint that scales the value gradient at the final action by the flow time, eliminating the per-step vector--Jacobian products. We further find that controlling the critic's value at policy-generated actions is particularly important under the scalar adjoint. Based on these findings, we propose Q-learning with Scalar Adjoint Matching (SQAM), which combines the scalar adjoint with a value penalty at those actions. SQAM's gains concentrate on the four hardest OGBench domains, where its success rate exceeds that of the strongest baseline in each domain by 18 to 35 percentage points. To test whether SQAM extends to large pretrained policies, we also fine-tune a vision-language-action policy on a real bimanual robot. SQAM improves over supervised fine-tuning on all three tasks.

</details>


### [305] [Physics-Aligned Electronic Ground-State Learning Improves Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10298)
*Eike S. Eberhard,Xaver Kainz,Viktor Kotsev,Abdulrahman Aldossary,Stephan Günnemann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出电子基态描述符模型（GSM），通过物理约束和消除无关自由度优化，提升机器学习势的分布外泛化能力，在能量和力的预测上大幅超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习原子间势（MLIPs）在分布内任务上表现优异，但在分布外任务上泛化能力差。作者希望在不达到Kohn-Sham密度泛函理论（KS-DFT）高成本的前提下，推动成本-精度帕累托前沿。

Method: 设计可观测无关的电子基态描述符模型（GSM），计算成本介于MLIPs和KS-DFT之间；通过强制物理约束、去除对非物理或不相关自由度的优化压力，使学习目标和架构与KS-DFT的控制方程对齐。提出ON-Loss（正交归一损失）与GROOT（Grassmann受限占据轨道训练）用于密度GSM，ON-Loss与ROCKET（残差最优规范条件感知KS方程训练）用于哈密顿量GSM；并使用自洽拒绝准则和免标签自洽微调。

Result: 在QM9到QM40的尺寸外推实验中，密度GSM的能量和力MAE相比先前最优方法分别降低79.1%和83.4%；哈密顿量GSM的能量和力MAE相比最强基线分别降低99.8%和95.9%。在QMugs上，自洽拒绝准则仅拒绝不到0.4%的预测，能量MAE达到0.07 mHa。迁移到Transition1x反应化学任务，能量误差低于化学精度。

Conclusion: GSM通过物理对齐的设计有效提升分布外泛化能力，在密度和哈密顿量建模上均显著优于现有方法，并能高效迁移到反应化学任务。

Abstract: Machine-learned interatomic potentials (MLIPs) excel at in-distribution tasks, accelerating drug and material development, yet they struggle to generalize out-of-distribution. We propose to push the cost-accuracy Pareto frontier by designing observable-agnostic electronic ground-state descriptor models (GSMs) with computational costs situated between MLIPs and Kohn-Sham density functional theory (KS-DFT). We align the learning objectives and architectures of GSMs with the governing equations of KS-DFT by enforcing physical constraints and removing optimization pressure on unphysical or irrelevant degrees of freedom. In our size-extrapolation experiments from QM9 to QM40, our combined contributions OrthoNormal-Loss (ON-Loss) and Grassmann Restricted Occupied-Orbital Training (GROOT) reach a 79.1% energy and 83.4% force mean absolute error (MAE) reduction over previous state-of-the-art density GSMs. For Hamiltonian GSMs, ON-Loss and Residual Optimal-gauge Conditioning-aware KS-Eq. Training (ROCKET) together reduce the energy and force MAEs of the strongest baseline by 99.8% and 95.9%, respectively. Using a self-consistency rejection criterion, we filter out extrapolation errors on QMugs, rejecting fewer than 0.4% of predictions while reaching an energy MAE of 0.07 mHa. Finally, we demonstrate the efficiency of label-free self-consistency fine-tuning, and transfer GSMs to reactive chemistry in Transition1x, reaching energy errors below chemical accuracy.

</details>


### [306] [A Good Self-Teacher Meets the Student Where They Are: Joint On-Policy Learning and Teaching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10447)
*Randy Ardywibowo,Arnav Dalal,Jiantao Jiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析自蒸馏中特权教师提供监督的有效性，推导教师更新成为学生奖励梯度正倍数的充要条件，提出JOLT方法联合训练特权教师与非特权学生，在数学推理、代码、工具使用等任务上提升训练效率与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习仅依赖结果奖励在长时程困难任务上监督稀疏，成功轨迹稀少且生成成本高；在线策略蒸馏（OPD）可提供稠密token级监督，自蒸馏进一步免去独立教师模型，但特权信息可能使教师走捷径，其监督与学生当前行为不匹配，甚至导致学生性能下降。因此需要分析特权教师选择如何影响学生更新。

Method: 论文分析特权教师选择对学生更新的影响，推导教师局部蒸馏更新为学生奖励梯度正倍数的充要条件，表明教师不仅要任务表现好，还应匹配学生当前能力；据此提出教师训练代理目标（结合结果奖励与向学生KL正则），并设计JOLT方法：联合训练单一策略的两个角色——使用KL正则目标的有特权教师，以及使用稠密在线策略蒸馏的无特权学生。

Result: 在数学推理、代码、工具使用和终端使用任务上，JOLT提升了训练效率和性能，加入学生奖励后还能获得进一步提升。

Conclusion: 特权条件本身不足以保证蒸馏更新改善学生；通过理论条件指导，让教师兼顾任务表现与学生当前能力，并以KL正则联合训练教师与学生，是一种有效提升在线策略蒸馏效果的方法。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) from outcome rewards suffers from sparse supervision, particularly on difficult, long-horizon tasks where successful trajectories are rare and costly to generate. On-Policy Distillation (OPD) offers an attractive alternative by providing dense token-level supervision from a stronger teacher along the student's own generations. Self-distillation methods further remove the need for a separate teacher model by conditioning the same policy on privileged information to serve as its own teacher. However, privileged conditioning alone does not guarantee that the resulting distillation update improves the student. Indeed, privileged information can lead the teacher to solve tasks through shortcuts unavailable to the student, producing supervision poorly matched to the student's current behavior. Consequently, even a higher-performing teacher can provide guidance that degrades student performance. To address this, we analyze how the choice of privileged teacher affects the student's update. We derive a necessary and sufficient condition for the teacher's local distillation update to be a positive multiple of the student's reward gradient. Our analysis suggests that the teacher should not only perform well on the task, but also provide guidance suited to the student's current capabilities. This characterization motivates a practical teacher-training surrogate that combines outcome rewards with token-level Kullback-Leibler (KL) regularization toward the student. Based on this result, we propose Joint On-Policy Learning and Teaching (JOLT), which jointly trains a single policy in two roles: a privileged teacher using a KL-regularized objective, and an unprivileged student using dense on-policy distillation. Across mathematical reasoning, coding, tool use, and terminal use, JOLT improves training efficiency and performance, with further gains from student rewards.

</details>


### [307] [Shared Gaussianization: What Gaussian Regularizers Certify About Contrastive Learning, and What They Miss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10299)
*Ruoyu Zhao,Yuting Chen,Jinheng Zhang,Zhehao Zou,Tong Che*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究共享高斯化(SG)正则项在对比学习中的作用，证明其能同时检测视图错位与非均匀性，且在总体InfoNCE对齐均匀极小点处消失，并给出InfoNCE超额风险的平方根界、尖锐性分析与线性界条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SIGReg等分布匹配正则项(如LeJEPA中)被广泛使用，但其在对比学习中的理论保证尚不清楚。作者希望厘清这类正则项究竟能"证明"什么性质，特别是它能否捕捉对比学习关心的对齐与均匀性，以及何时其界优于InfoNCE。

Method: 提出共享高斯化(SG)，对两个归一化视图的平均施加特征函数高斯性检验，并由独立χ_d半径缩放。利用两视图不一致会缩短平均这一几何现象，设计单一检测同时捕获错位与非均匀。给出SG损失与InfoNCE超额之间的不等式，分析尖锐性与维度无关常数；刻画带显式对齐项时旋转不变均匀性检验取得线性界的充要条件；研究各向同性噪声通道下最优解的差异、对齐权重阈值、SIGReg权重规则及有限批U统计去偏。

Result: SG恰在总体InfoNCE的对齐均匀极小点处消失；等边缘条件下InfoNCE超额被4·3^{3/4}β倍SG损失平方根加线性项所界，且该平方根速率与常数尖锐，任何视图对的均方嵌入距离都无法更快。旋转不变均匀性检验取得线性界当且仅当其谱主导InfoNCE核e^{βu^T v}；SG检验满足，高斯核e^{-γ||u-v||^2}在γ≥β/2时成立，矩匹配则永不满足。远离最优时目标不同：纯SG在共享码非均匀时通过加入逐视图噪声降低损失；对齐权重超过通道增益使无噪声解成为严格局部极小，LeJEPA由此得到随批大小递减的临界SIGReg权重。有限批下非对角U统计消除偏向错位的插值偏差。在受控隐变量模型中，纯SG保留逐视图风格，对齐权重超过测得增益可移除之；LeJEPA三个批大小下测得增益能区分保留与不保留风格的编码器。InfoNCE训练达到比从头SG训练更低的SG_{0.2}损失，提示优化差距。

Conclusion: SG作为分布匹配正则项能认证对比学习的对齐与均匀性，并给出尖锐的InfoNCE超额界与线性界条件；但其与InfoNCE在远离最优时目标不一致，存在被噪声利用及优化差距问题，需配合足够对齐权重或去偏U统计才能获得理想的局部极小与稳定性。

Abstract: What can a distribution-matching regularizer such as SIGReg in LeJEPA certify about contrastive learning? We study shared Gaussianization (SG), a characteristic-function Gaussianity test on the average of two normalized views, scaled by an independent $χ_d$ radius. Because disagreeing views shorten the average, one test detects both misalignment and non-uniformity. SG vanishes exactly at the aligned, uniform minimizers of population InfoNCE, and under equal marginals it bounds the InfoNCE excess by $4\cdot 3^{3/4}β$ times the square root of the SG loss, plus a term linear in the loss. The square-root rate and this dimension-free constant are sharp, and no squared mean-embedding distance on view pairs achieves a faster rate. With an explicit alignment term, a rotation-invariant uniformity test gives a linear bound if and only if its spectrum dominates that of InfoNCE's kernel $e^{βu^\top v}$; SG's own test does, Gaussian kernels $e^{-γ\|u-v\|^2}$ qualify exactly when $γ\ge β/2$, and moment matching never does. Away from the optimum, the objectives differ. Along an isotropic nuisance channel, pure SG lowers its loss by adding per-view nuisance whenever the shared code is non-uniform. An alignment weight above the channel's gain makes the nuisance-free solution a strict local minimizer; for LeJEPA, the same rule gives a critical SIGReg weight that decreases with the batch size. At finite batch size, an off-diagonal U-statistic removes a plug-in bias toward misalignment. In controlled latent-variable models, pure SG retains per-view style, an alignment weight above the measured gain removes it, and for LeJEPA at three batch sizes the measured gain separates the encoders that retain style from those that do not. InfoNCE training also reaches a lower SG$_{0.2}$ loss than SG$_{0.2}$ training from scratch, which points to an optimization gap.

</details>


### [308] [Composing What Each Teacher Learned: Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation through Teacher-Relative Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10460)
*Hejian Sang,Zhengze Zhou,Shayan Mohajer Hamidi,Xiaomin Li,Rohit Jain,Alborz Geramifard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 Δ-MOPD，将多教师蒸馏中的监督目标从教师端点策略改为“教师减基座”的 logit 偏移，并重新锚定在学生冻结初始化上；在组合与路由两类设置下验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多教师在线策略蒸馏通常迁移教师的端点策略，而端点策略混合了后训练改变与教师基座继承的偏好；作者发现继承的基座拉力可能超过后训练偏移，从而阻碍端点迁移，因此需要一种更纯净的目标构造方式。

Method: 提出 Δ-MOPD，迁移每个教师的“教师减基座”logit 偏移，并将其重新锚定在学生模型的冻结初始化上；在共同域组合与路由域蒸馏两类设置中，与端点监督对比，同时保持教师选择固定。

Result: 去除基座拉力后，教师项范数比与目标-学生 KL 降低。三个教师组合时，Δ-MOPD 比端点组合高出 4.11 Math 与 1.95 五基准点；两个教师时与端点准确率相当。分阶段路由下，两种阶段顺序均取得更高平均性能，并将顺序差距从 10.50 降至 6.42 点。交错路由下两者表现相近。

Conclusion: 目标构造是多教师在线策略蒸馏中一个独立的设计轴，与教师选择互补；当教师信号在同一状态被组合时，偏移目标尤其有用，且分阶段结果支持该收益可扩展到跨训练阶段累积的信号。

Abstract: Multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD) is used in two settings. In common-domain composition, several teachers score each student rollout from one prompt domain and their signals form a single target; in routed-domain distillation, prompts from different domains are assigned to the corresponding specialist. Both settings usually transfer each teacher's endpoint policy, which mixes what post-training changed with preferences inherited from the teacher's base. We introduce $Δ$-MOPD, which transfers each teacher's teacher-minus-base logit shift re-anchored at the student's frozen initialization, and compare it with endpoint supervision in both settings while holding teacher selection fixed. We first expose the mechanism that impedes endpoint transfer: inherited base pull can exceed the post-training shift. Removing it reduces the teacher-term norm ratio and target--student KL.
  Across our experiments, the results suggest that shift targets are particularly useful when teacher signals are combined at a state. With three composed teachers, $Δ$-MOPD exceeds endpoint composition by $4.11$ Math and $1.95$ five-benchmark points; with two, it matches endpoint accuracy. Under phased routing, it achieves higher mean performance in both phase orders and reduces the observed order gap from $10.50$ to $6.42$ points. Under interleaved routing, where each update involves one teacher, the two targets perform comparably. The phased results provide supporting evidence that the benefit may extend to signals accumulated across training phases. Target construction is thus an independent design axis in MOPD, complementary to teacher selection.

</details>


### [309] [Revisiting Explainable AI through Model-Independent Concept Dictionaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10301)
*Thomas Schnake,Doreen Schöppenthau,Alexander Meyer,Jacques Corbeil,Klaus-Robert Müller,Grégoire Montavon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DictXAI通过字典在输入域直接定义概念，用稀疏编码将预测归因到可解释字典元素，实现可操作、架构无关的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有XAI方法通常假设输入特征本身可解释，或依赖难以刻画且高度依赖具体架构的中间内部抽象，导致跨模型使用不一致。为克服这些局限，作者提出在输入域直接定义概念的方法。

Method: DictXAI通过一个字典（大规模、可能过完备的预定义元素集合，每个元素具有可解释含义）在输入域定义概念。技术上，先计算输入的稀疏编码，再将模型预测归因到相关的字典元素上。

Result: 实验表明DictXAI的解释具有可操作性，能将AI故障（如Clever Hans效应）直接归因到数据中可识别的伪影模式，并在复杂生物医学信号上促进人机对齐。方法可跨多种字典运行，包括学习到的图像基、心电图解析波形以及实验获取的字典元素。

Conclusion: DictXAI相比经典XAI或现有基于概念的方法，提供了更可解释、更可操作、更具架构无关性的洞见。

Abstract: Modern applications of AI rely on increasingly complex models. Explainable AI (XAI) has emerged as a set of techniques aimed at improving model transparency. However, existing XAI methods typically assume input features to be inherently interpretable, or they rely on intermediate internal abstractions that are difficult to characterize and highly architecture-specific, hindering consistent use across models. To address these limitations, we propose DictXAI, a method that defines concepts directly in the input domain via a dictionary---a large, potentially overcomplete set of predefined elements, each carrying an interpretable meaning. Technically, DictXAI first computes a sparse code of the input and then attributes the model's prediction to the associated dictionary elements. We demonstrate the actionable nature of DictXAI explanations, showing that they can attribute AI malfunctions (e.g., Clever Hans effects) directly to identifiable artifact patterns in the data, while fostering human-AI alignment on intricate biomedical signals. We further demonstrate our method's ability to operate across a wide variety of dictionaries, including learned image bases, analytically defined waveforms for electrocardiography, and experimentally acquired dictionary elements. Overall, our results show that DictXAI provides more interpretable, actionable, and architecture-agnostic insights than classical XAI or existing concept-based approaches.

</details>


### [310] [Continual Graph Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10302)
*Tommaso Marzi,Ahmed Hendawy,Jan Peters,Carlo D'Eramo,Andrea Cini,Cesare Alippi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出持续图多智能体强化学习（CGMARL）框架，将任务序列映射为属性图，并发布首个基准GRAFO，进一步提出FROG方法，用冻结图编码器保留历史结构信息，实验证明其能显著缓解遗忘并提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在持续多智能体强化学习（CMARL）中，智能体需在任务序列上学习协作策略，兼顾新任务适应与旧任务保持。许多应用中任务的结构不同（如电网拓扑、编队配置）。现有CMARL方法缺乏利用这种结构信息的专门机制，无法促进迁移并缓解遗忘。

Method: 提出CGMARL框架：将任务序列映射为一系列属性图，每个图建模任务特定结构，决定环境动态（下一状态和/或奖励）以及相应任务的智能体数量。构建首个CGMARL基准GRAFO，展示遗忘现象。提出Frozen Graph Encoder（FROG），依赖冻结的图主干网络，在图式CMARL策略中保留过去的结构信息。

Result: 在GRAFO上的实验表明，将FROG与现有持续学习方法配对，可在多个CGMARL场景中大幅提升性能。

Conclusion: 结构信息（图表示）可有效用于持续多智能体强化学习；冻结图编码器FROG结合现有持续学习方法是缓解遗忘、提升多场景性能的有效途径。

Abstract: In Continual Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (CMARL), agents learn cooperative policies across sequences of tasks, aiming to adapt effectively to new tasks while preserving the ability to solve previously encountered ones. In many applications, tasks differ in their underlying structure, which can represent, for example, distinct operational conditions or target configurations (e.g., different network topologies in power grids or arrangements in formation control). Existing CMARL methods lack dedicated mechanisms to leverage this structural information when learning new tasks, failing to promote transfer and mitigate forgetting. To fill this gap, we propose Continual Graph Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (CGMARL), a novel framework for CMARL problems in which task sequences are mapped into a series of attributed graphs, each modeling a task-specific structure. In CGMARL, each graph determines the environment dynamics (next states and/or rewards) and the number of agents for the corresponding task. Then, we present Graph-based Formation (GRAFO), the first CGMARL benchmark, and show how forgetting arises in this setting. Finally, to address this limitation, we propose Frozen Graph Encoder (FROG), a method that relies on a frozen graph backbone to preserve past structural information in graph-based CMARL policies. Experiments on GRAFO show that pairing FROG with existing CL methods substantially improves performance on multiple CGMARL scenarios.

</details>


### [311] [Decoupling Exploration from Optimization in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10536)
*Saif Punjwani,Micah Goldblum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Exploration-Distillation（ExpDis）框架，通过将探索与优化解耦，在 RLVR 中实现激进的探索而不会损害学生策略的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR 的一个核心承诺是发现新的推理策略，理论上模型可以采样出训练数据中不存在的新想法。然而实践中，为 RLVR 添加强新颖性激励效果有限，甚至会降低模型质量；由于可验证奖励只能监督模型知识和行为的狭窄部分，这种质量退化难以恢复。因此需要一种能兼顾探索与优化、避免质量退化的方法。

Method: 提出 Exploration-Distillation（ExpDis）框架，将探索与优化解耦：首先用一个或多个带新颖性奖励bonus的探索策略（explorer policies）进行训练，对其轨迹按正确性和质量进行过滤，再蒸馏到一个独立的学生策略中；学生策略不带新颖性奖励进行训练。该过程交替进行多轮探索与优化。

Result: 在七个数学推理基准和两个模型家族上，ExpDis 在相同的墙钟时间预算下优于 DAPO。此外，观察到 pass@k 扩展性提升，表明 ExpDis 生成的模型能产生更多样化的正确解。

Conclusion: 通过解耦探索与优化，ExpDis 能够激进地扩展探索而不损害学生策略，从而在推理任务上取得更优性能并提升解的多样性。

Abstract: Modern language models undergo reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) on top of already-trained checkpoints. A key promise of RLVR is the discovery of new reasoning strategies. In principle, a model can sample novel ideas absent from its prior training data. In practice, however, augmenting RLVR with strong novelty incentives has seen limited success and can degrade model quality. Because verifiable rewards supervise only a narrow slice of the model's knowledge and behavior, such degradations are difficult to recover from. Instead, we decouple exploration from optimization in a framework we call Exploration-Distillation (ExpDis). We train one or more explorer policies with a novelty bonus in the reward, filter their trajectories for correctness and quality, and distill them into a separate student policy. The student policy is then trained without a novelty bonus. We repeat the above procedure for several rounds, alternating between exploration and optimization. This decoupling allows us to aggressively scale exploration without degrading the student policy. Across seven mathematical reasoning benchmarks and two model families, ExpDis outperforms DAPO at the same wall-clock budget. Moreover, we observe improved pass@$k$ scaling, indicating that ExpDis produces models that generate more diverse correct solutions.

</details>


### [312] [How Do Transformers Learn to Represent Symmetries?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10305)
*Eduardo Santos-Escriche,Valerie Engelmayer,Ya-Wei Eileen Lin,Stefanie Jegelka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究朴素Transformer通过有限数据增强学习点云数据集各类对称性的能力，发现不同对称群的学习难度存在递增顺序，并识别出诱导不变性的可解释机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于Transformer的架构结合有限数据增强在几何机器学习中日益流行，但其架构、对不同对称性的不变性与增强预算之间的相互作用仍未充分探索。

Method: 研究朴素Transformer通过有限数据增强学习点云数据集各种对称性的能力，分析不同对称群的可学习性顺序，并对基础保角子群进行外推行为研究和训练模型的结构分析，最后将分析扩展到等变函数。

Result: 识别出对称群可学习性的递增顺序：（i）非保角对称性，（ii）保角对称性，（iii）基础保角子群（如平移、旋转、缩放）；并发现诱导不变性的可解释机制。

Conclusion: 所检测到的近似不变性机制也可为学习等变性提供关键构建模块。

Abstract: Training Transformer-based architectures with finite data augmentation has become an increasingly popular approach in geometric machine learning. Despite its empirical success, the interplay between the Transformer architecture, invariance to different symmetries, and augmentation budgets remains underexplored. In this paper, we study the ability of a vanilla Transformer to learn various symmetries through finite data augmentation for point cloud datasets. We identify an ordering of increasing learnability across the following symmetry groups: (i) non-angle-preserving symmetries, (ii) angle-preserving symmetries, and (iii) base angle-preserving subgroups, such as translation, rotation, and scale. For the base angle-preserving groups, we further investigate the Transformer's extrapolation behavior and conduct a structural analysis of the trained models, allowing us to identify interpretable mechanisms that induce invariance. Finally, we extend our analysis to equivariant functions and show that the detected mechanisms for approximate invariance can also provide a key building block for learned equivariance. Our project page is available at https://transformers-learn-symmetries.github.io/

</details>


### [313] [RSIGym: A Flexible Environment for Recursive Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10310)
*Fanqing Meng,Lingxiao Du,Haocheng Lu,Qiguang Chen,Ziqi Zhao,Zijian Wu,Jiayuan Zhuo,Mengkang Hu,Michael Qizhe Shieh*
